File `crates/planrun-tui/src/grid.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! The cell grid as text, for snapshots (decision D93): each row's characters, then its style
//! runs by token name (`3-9 act/bg2 b`), so a snapshot can be read against the design's
//! artboards cell for cell. Cells in the app's base style (fg on bg0, no modifier) are left out
//! of the runs.

use std::fmt::Write;

use ratatui::buffer::Buffer;
use ratatui::style::{Color, Modifier};

use crate::theme::Token;

/// The grid of `buffer`: one text line per row (trailing spaces cut), followed by the row's
/// style runs when it has any.
pub fn grid(buffer: &Buffer) -> String {
    let area = buffer.area;
    let mut out = String::new();
    for y in area.top()..area.bottom() {
        let mut text = String::new();
        let mut runs: Vec<(u16, u16, String)> = Vec::new();
        let mut x = area.left();
        while x < area.right() {
            let cell = &buffer[(x, y)];
            text.push_str(cell.symbol());
            let style = describe(cell.fg, cell.bg, cell.modifier);
            match runs.last_mut() {
                Some((_, end, s)) if *s == style && *end + 1 == x => *end = x,
                _ => runs.push((x, x, style)),
            }
            x += 1;
        }
        let _ = writeln!(out, "{y:>2} {}", text.trim_end());
        let shown: Vec<String> = runs
            .into_iter()
            .filter(|(_, _, s)| s != "fg/bg0")
            .map(|(a, b, s)| format!("{a}-{b} {s}"))
            .collect();
        if !shown.is_empty() {
            let _ = writeln!(out, "   {}", shown.join(", "));
        }
    }
    out
}

fn describe(fg: Color, bg: Color, modifier: Modifier) -> String {
    let mut s = format!("{}/{}", color(fg), color(bg));
    if modifier.contains(Modifier::BOLD) {
        s.push_str(" b");
    }
    if modifier.contains(Modifier::REVERSED) {
        s.push_str(" r");
    }
    s
}

fn color(c: Color) -> String {
    if let Some(token) = Token::of(c) {
        return token.name().to_owned();
    }
    match c {
        Color::Reset => "-".to_owned(),
        Color::Rgb(r, g, b) => format!("#{r:02x}{g:02x}{b:02x}"),
        other => format!("{other:?}"),
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use ratatui::layout::Rect;
    use ratatui::style::Style;

    use super::*;
    use crate::theme::{base, bold, on};

    #[test]
    fn a_row_shows_its_text_and_its_styled_runs() {
        let mut buffer = Buffer::empty(Rect::new(0, 0, 12, 2));
        buffer.set_style(buffer.area, base());
        buffer.set_string(0, 0, " PLANRUN", on(Token::Fg, Token::Bg1));
        buffer.set_string(1, 0, "PLANRUN", bold(Token::Fg).bg(Token::Bg1.color()));
        buffer.set_string(9, 1, "ok", Style::new().fg(Token::Ok.color()));
        let text = grid(&buffer);
        assert_eq!(
            text,
            " 0  PLANRUN\n   0-0 fg/bg1, 1-7 fg/bg1 b\n 1          ok\n   9-10 ok/bg0\n"
        );
    }
}
```

File `crates/planrun-tui/src/jobs.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! Background work (docs/tui-design.md §2, decision D90): each job runs on a thread of its own
//! and sends one result back; the render thread never waits on git, a shell or the network.
//! Each result carries the fingerprint of its input, so a result for an input that has changed
//! since is shown as stale. Background checks never replace preflight.

use std::ffi::OsString;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::UNIX_EPOCH;

use planrun_exec::journal::{self, End, Line, Record};
use planrun_exec::lock;
use planrun_exec::runner::resume::analyse;
use planrun_parse::{Parsed, Registry, Severity, parse_plan, source};

/// What the TUI knows about the repository and itself, shared with every job.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Context {
    /// The repository root (canonical).
    pub root: PathBuf,
    /// Its git directory (`rev-parse --absolute-git-dir`).
    pub git_dir: PathBuf,
    /// The repository's name, the first crumb.
    pub repo: String,
    /// The target as the footer shows it.
    pub target: String,
    /// The plans directory (absolute).
    pub plans_dir: PathBuf,
    /// The plans directory as the footer shows it.
    pub plans_label: String,
    /// planrun's version.
    pub version: String,
    /// Added to every git call and every session (the tests keep the user's configuration out).
    pub env: Vec<(OsString, OsString)>,
}

impl Context {
    /// The journal directory, `<git-dir>/planrun`.
    pub fn journal_dir(&self) -> PathBuf {
        journal::dir(&self.git_dir)
    }

    /// The plan's path as a journal records it: repository-relative with forward slashes, or
    /// canonical when it is outside the repository (D76).
    pub fn plan_path(&self, file: &Path) -> String {
        let canonical = std::fs::canonicalize(file).unwrap_or_else(|_| file.to_path_buf());
        match canonical.strip_prefix(&self.root) {
            Ok(rel) => rel.to_string_lossy().replace('\\', "/"),
            Err(_) => canonical.display().to_string(),
        }
    }
}

/// A file's fingerprint for change detection: its length and modification time.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Default)]
pub struct Stamp {
    /// Bytes.
    pub len: u64,
    /// Milliseconds since the Unix epoch.
    pub modified: u64,
}

impl Stamp {
    /// The stamp of `path`, or `None` when it cannot be read.
    pub fn of(path: &Path) -> Option<Stamp> {
        let meta = std::fs::metadata(path).ok()?;
        let modified = meta
            .modified()
            .ok()
            .and_then(|t| t.duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).ok())
            .map_or(0, |d| u64::try_from(d.as_millis()).unwrap_or(u64::MAX));
        Some(Stamp {
            len: meta.len(),
            modified,
        })
    }
}

/// The five states of background work.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Bg<T> {
    /// Started; no result yet.
    Running,
    /// The result for the input as it is.
    Current(T),
    /// A result for an input that has changed since; a new one is on its way.
    Stale(T),
    /// The input is not there (no journal directory, no update check).
    Unavailable(String),
    /// The work failed.
    Failed(String),
}

impl<T> Bg<T> {
    /// The result, current or stale.
    pub fn value(&self) -> Option<&T> {
        match self {
            Bg::Current(v) | Bg::Stale(v) => Some(v),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    /// The state with its result marked stale.
    pub fn stale(self) -> Bg<T> {
        match self {
            Bg::Current(v) | Bg::Stale(v) => Bg::Stale(v),
            _ => Bg::Running,
        }
    }
}

/// What `planrun update --check` found (D83); planrun-cli runs it.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum UpdateState {
    /// Not checked (yet, or not at all).
    Unchecked,
    /// Up to date.
    Current,
    /// A newer release.
    Available(String),
    /// The check failed.
    Failed,
}

/// The update check planrun-cli hands in.
pub type UpdateCheck = Box<dyn FnOnce() -> UpdateState + Send>;

/// One diagnostic of a plan check, with its line.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Diag {
    /// An error (the plan does not parse), or a warning or note.
    pub error: bool,
    /// Its stable code.
    pub code: &'static str,
    /// Its message.
    pub message: String,
    /// Its 1-based line in the plan, when it has a span.
    pub line: Option<u32>,
}

/// The journal of a plan, as the Plans view shows it.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum RunState {
    /// No journal, or an empty one.
    NotRun,
    /// The last chain completed; when.
    Done(u64),
    /// The last chain stopped; resume goes on.
    Stopped,
    /// The last chain has no `run-end`: killed, or running now.
    Interrupted,
    /// A journal of this stem written for another plan path (D76).
    Foreign(String),
    /// The journal could not be read.
    Unreadable(String),
}

/// A plan file, checked.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct PlanFile {
    /// Its lines.
    pub lines: usize,
    /// The parsed plan, or `None` when it has errors.
    pub parsed: Option<Arc<Parsed>>,
    /// Every diagnostic, errors included, in source order.
    pub diags: Vec<Diag>,
    /// No format detects it: not a plan.
    pub not_a_plan: bool,
    /// Its journal.
    pub run: RunState,
}

impl PlanFile {
    /// Errors, warnings.
    pub fn counts(&self) -> (usize, usize) {
        let errors = self.diags.iter().filter(|d| d.error).count();
        let warnings = self.diags.iter().filter(|d| !d.error).count();
        (errors, warnings)
    }
}

/// One piece of background work.
pub enum Job {
    /// Lists the plans directory's `*.md` files.
    Scan,
    /// Parses and checks a plan, and reads its journal.
    Check {
        /// The file.
        path: PathBuf,
        /// Its stamp when the check started.
        stamp: Stamp,
    },
    /// Probes `lock.live` (D90).
    Live,
    /// `planrun update --check`.
    Update(UpdateCheck),
}

/// What a job found.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum Done {
    /// The plans directory's files with their stamps, sorted by name.
    Scanned(Result<Vec<(PathBuf, Stamp)>, String>),
    /// A plan's check.
    Checked {
        /// The file.
        path: PathBuf,
        /// The stamp it was checked at.
        stamp: Stamp,
        /// What it found, or why it could not read the file.
        file: Result<PlanFile, String>,
    },
    /// A run holds the lock now (with the pid in `lock.pid`), or not.
    Live(Result<Option<Option<u32>>, String>),
    /// The update check.
    Update(UpdateState),
}

/// What a job sends when it panicked: its own failed state, with the panic's message (§2).
pub type OnFailure = Box<dyn FnOnce(String) -> Done + Send>;

/// A panic's message, from its payload.
pub fn panic_message(payload: &(dyn std::any::Any + Send)) -> String {
    if let Some(s) = payload.downcast_ref::<&str>() {
        return (*s).to_owned();
    }
    if let Some(s) = payload.downcast_ref::<String>() {
        return s.clone();
    }
    "(no message)".to_owned()
}

impl Job {
    /// The result to send if the job panics: the failed state of what it works on.
    pub fn on_failure(&self) -> OnFailure {
        match self {
            Job::Scan => Box::new(|e| Done::Scanned(Err(e))),
            Job::Check { path, stamp } => {
                let (path, stamp) = (path.clone(), *stamp);
                Box::new(move |e| Done::Checked {
                    path,
                    stamp,
                    file: Err(e),
                })
            }
            Job::Live => Box::new(|e| Done::Live(Err(e))),
            Job::Update(_) => Box::new(|_| Done::Update(UpdateState::Failed)),
        }
    }

    /// Does the work.
    pub fn run(self, ctx: &Context) -> Done {
        match self {
            Job::Scan => Done::Scanned(scan(&ctx.plans_dir)),
            Job::Check { path, stamp } => {
                let file = check(ctx, &path);
                Done::Checked { path, stamp, file }
            }
            Job::Live => Done::Live(live(ctx)),
            Job::Update(check) => Done::Update(check()),
        }
    }
}

fn scan(dir: &Path) -> Result<Vec<(PathBuf, Stamp)>, String> {
    let entries = std::fs::read_dir(dir).map_err(|e| format!("{}: {e}", dir.display()))?;
    let mut files: Vec<(PathBuf, Stamp)> = entries
        .flatten()
        .map(|e| e.path())
        .filter(|p| p.is_file() && p.extension().is_some_and(|x| x == "md"))
        .filter_map(|p| Stamp::of(&p).map(|s| (p, s)))
        .collect();
    files.sort_by(|a, b| a.0.cmp(&b.0));
    Ok(files)
}

fn check(ctx: &Context, path: &Path) -> Result<PlanFile, String> {
    let bytes = std::fs::read(path).map_err(|e| format!("{}: {e}", path.display()))?;
    let registry = Registry::with_builtins().map_err(|e| format!("the built-in formats: {e}"))?;
    let name = path.file_name().map_or_else(
        || path.display().to_string(),
        |n| n.to_string_lossy().into_owned(),
    );
    let lines = bytes.iter().filter(|&&b| b == b'\n').count();
    let run = journal_state(ctx, path);
    let file = match parse_plan(&registry, &name, &bytes, None) {
        Ok(parsed) => {
            let diags = diags(&parsed.plan.source, &parsed.diagnostics.0);
            PlanFile {
                lines,
                parsed: Some(Arc::new(parsed)),
                diags,
                not_a_plan: false,
                run,
            }
        }
        Err(diagnostics) => {
            let not_a_plan = diagnostics.0.iter().any(|d| d.code == "no-format");
            let source = source::load(&name, &bytes).ok();
            let diags = match &source {
                Some(source) => diags(source, &diagnostics.0),
                None => diags_without_lines(&diagnostics.0),
            };
            PlanFile {
                lines,
                parsed: None,
                diags,
                not_a_plan,
                run,
            }
        }
    };
    Ok(file)
}

fn diags(source: &planrun_model::PlanSource, all: &[planrun_parse::Diagnostic]) -> Vec<Diag> {
    all.iter()
        .map(|d| Diag {
            error: d.severity == Severity::Error,
            code: d.code,
            message: d.message.clone(),
            line: d.span.map(|s| source.line_col(s.start).line),
        })
        .collect()
}

fn diags_without_lines(all: &[planrun_parse::Diagnostic]) -> Vec<Diag> {
    all.iter()
        .map(|d| Diag {
            error: d.severity == Severity::Error,
            code: d.code,
            message: d.message.clone(),
            line: None,
        })
        .collect()
}

/// The plan's journal: its stem's file in `<git-dir>/planrun` (D76).
fn journal_state(ctx: &Context, path: &Path) -> RunState {
    let stem = path
        .file_stem()
        .map_or_else(|| "plan".to_owned(), |s| s.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
    let lines = match journal::read(&journal::path_for(&ctx.git_dir, &stem)) {
        Ok(lines) => lines,
        Err(e) => return RunState::Unreadable(e.to_string()),
    };
    run_state(&lines, &ctx.plan_path(path))
}

/// What the journal's last chain says about the plan at `plan_path`.
pub fn run_state(lines: &[Line], plan_path: &str) -> RunState {
    let foreign = lines.iter().find_map(|l| match &l.record {
        Record::Run { plan, .. } if plan != plan_path => Some(plan.clone()),
        _ => None,
    });
    if let Some(other) = foreign {
        return RunState::Foreign(other);
    }
    let Some(analysis) = analyse(lines) else {
        return RunState::NotRun;
    };
    match analysis.end {
        Some(End::Completed) => RunState::Done(lines.last().map_or(0, |l| l.ms)),
        Some(End::Stopped) => RunState::Stopped,
        None => RunState::Interrupted,
    }
}

fn live(ctx: &Context) -> Result<Option<Option<u32>>, String> {
    let dir = ctx.journal_dir();
    match lock::live(&dir) {
        Ok(true) => Ok(Some(lock::holder(&dir))),
        Ok(false) => Ok(None),
        Err(e) => Err(format!("{}: {e}", dir.join("lock.live").display())),
    }
}
```

```rust
//! Keys as the TUI reads them (docs/tui-design.md §7, decision D94). crossterm reports a press
//! and a release on Windows: only presses count, and a held key's repeats only for movement.
//! Ctrl-C arrives as a key in raw mode; the dock confirms it (D91).

use ratatui::crossterm::event::{KeyCode, KeyEvent, KeyEventKind, KeyModifiers};

/// One key, normalised.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Key {
    /// A character; shift is in the character itself (`R`).
    Char(char),
    /// Ctrl and a letter, lower case.
    Ctrl(char),
    /// Enter.
    Enter,
    /// Esc.
    Esc,
    /// Tab.
    Tab,
    /// Shift-Tab.
    BackTab,
    /// Backspace.
    Backspace,
    /// Up.
    Up,
    /// Down.
    Down,
    /// Left.
    Left,
    /// Right.
    Right,
    /// Page up.
    PageUp,
    /// Page down.
    PageDown,
    /// Home.
    Home,
    /// End.
    End,
    /// A function key.
    F(u8),
}

impl Key {
    /// Whether a held key's repeats count: movement only.
    fn repeats(self) -> bool {
        matches!(
            self,
            Key::Up | Key::Down | Key::Left | Key::Right | Key::PageUp | Key::PageDown
        )
    }
}

/// The key `event` stands for; `None` for a release, a repeat of a key that does not move, and
/// keys the TUI does not use.
pub fn key(event: &KeyEvent) -> Option<Key> {
    let key = match event.code {
        KeyCode::Char(c) if event.modifiers.contains(KeyModifiers::CONTROL) => {
            Key::Ctrl(c.to_ascii_lowercase())
        }
        KeyCode::Char(c) => Key::Char(c),
        KeyCode::Enter => Key::Enter,
        KeyCode::Esc => Key::Esc,
        KeyCode::Tab if event.modifiers.contains(KeyModifiers::SHIFT) => Key::BackTab,
        KeyCode::Tab => Key::Tab,
        KeyCode::BackTab => Key::BackTab,
        KeyCode::Backspace => Key::Backspace,
        KeyCode::Up => Key::Up,
        KeyCode::Down => Key::Down,
        KeyCode::Left => Key::Left,
        KeyCode::Right => Key::Right,
        KeyCode::PageUp => Key::PageUp,
        KeyCode::PageDown => Key::PageDown,
        KeyCode::Home => Key::Home,
        KeyCode::End => Key::End,
        KeyCode::F(n) => Key::F(n),
        _ => return None,
    };
    match event.kind {
        KeyEventKind::Press => Some(key),
        KeyEventKind::Repeat if key.repeats() => Some(key),
        _ => None,
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use ratatui::crossterm::event::KeyEventState;

    use super::*;

    fn event(code: KeyCode, modifiers: KeyModifiers, kind: KeyEventKind) -> KeyEvent {
        KeyEvent {
            code,
            modifiers,
            kind,
            state: KeyEventState::NONE,
        }
    }

    #[rustfmt::skip]
    #[test]
    fn presses_count_releases_never_and_repeats_only_for_movement() {
        let none = KeyModifiers::NONE;
        let (press, repeat, release) = (KeyEventKind::Press, KeyEventKind::Repeat, KeyEventKind::Release);
        assert_eq!(key(&event(KeyCode::Char('R'), KeyModifiers::SHIFT, press)), Some(Key::Char('R')));
        assert_eq!(key(&event(KeyCode::Char('C'), KeyModifiers::CONTROL, press)), Some(Key::Ctrl('c')));
        assert_eq!(key(&event(KeyCode::Char('y'), none, release)), None);
        assert_eq!(key(&event(KeyCode::Char('y'), none, repeat)), None);
        assert_eq!(key(&event(KeyCode::Down, none, repeat)), Some(Key::Down));
        assert_eq!(key(&event(KeyCode::Tab, KeyModifiers::SHIFT, press)), Some(Key::BackTab));
        assert_eq!(key(&event(KeyCode::F(2), none, press)), Some(Key::F(2)));
        assert_eq!(key(&event(KeyCode::Insert, none, press)), None);
    }
}
```

```rust
//! The planrun TUI (docs/tui-design.md; decisions D84–D94): a second [`Ui`](planrun_exec::Ui)
//! over the same crates as the line prompts, composed by planrun-cli as `planrun tui`. It shows
//! the domain model and adds no model of its own: resume, policy and preflight stay
//! authoritative; the TUI explains and asks, and never decides.

#![forbid(unsafe_code)]

pub mod app;
pub mod chrome;
pub mod draw;
pub mod driver;
pub mod glyph;
pub mod grid;
pub mod jobs;
pub mod keys;
pub mod plans;
pub mod terminal;
pub mod text;

pub use terminal::{Host, run};
```

File `crates/planrun-tui/src/plans.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! The Plans view (docs/tui-design.md §6.A, decision D87): a table of the plan files,
//! `PLAN · FORMAT · TASKS · CHECK · RUN`, and the selected plan's detail: its outline with a
//! coverage strip per task, the check's diagnostics, the journal's status, and a word on
//! preflight, which is never a background check.

use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::Arc;

use ratatui::buffer::Buffer;
use ratatui::layout::Rect;
use ratatui::style::Style;

use planrun_model::ActionKind;
use planrun_parse::{Parsed, TaskCoverage};

use crate::draw::{Pen, band, hrule, section, select_row};
use crate::glyph::{Glyph, Glyphs};
use crate::jobs::{Bg, Job, PlanFile, RunState, Stamp};
use crate::keys::Key;
use crate::text;
use crate::theme::{Token, bold, fg};

/// The detail panel's width, its hairline included.
const DETAIL: u16 = 48;

/// One plan file.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Entry {
    /// Its path.
    pub path: PathBuf,
    /// Its file name.
    pub name: String,
    /// Its stamp as last scanned.
    pub stamp: Stamp,
    /// Its check.
    pub check: Bg<PlanFile>,
    /// The stamp of the check in `check`.
    pub checked: Option<Stamp>,
}

/// What the Plans view holds.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct Plans {
    /// The files, by name.
    pub entries: Vec<Entry>,
    /// The selected entry, among the shown ones.
    pub selected: usize,
    /// The filter: `Some` while it is typed or applied.
    pub filter: Option<String>,
    /// Typing the filter.
    pub editing: bool,
    /// Why the directory could not be listed.
    pub error: Option<String>,
}

/// What a key did in the Plans view.
pub enum Outcome {
    /// Nothing for the app.
    None,
    /// Open the selected plan in Inspect.
    Inspect(PathBuf),
    /// `R`: open the selected plan in Inspect to run it from there; a run is always preceded by
    /// a visible dry run and policy (D84).
    Run(PathBuf),
    /// Check these files again.
    Check(Vec<Job>),
    /// The key is not the view's.
    Unused,
}

impl Plans {
    /// The shown entries: all, or those whose name holds the filter.
    pub fn shown(&self) -> Vec<&Entry> {
        let filter = self.filter.as_deref().unwrap_or("").to_lowercase();
        self.entries
            .iter()
            .filter(|e| e.name.to_lowercase().contains(&filter))
            .collect()
    }

    /// The selected entry.
    pub fn current(&self) -> Option<&Entry> {
        self.shown().get(self.selected).copied()
    }

    /// The scan's result: new and changed files are checked (again); a changed file's check is
    /// stale until then.
    pub fn scanned(&mut self, files: Result<Vec<(PathBuf, Stamp)>, String>) -> Vec<Job> {
        let files = match files {
            Ok(files) => files,
            Err(e) => {
                self.error = Some(e);
                self.entries.clear();
                return Vec::new();
            }
        };
        self.error = None;
        let mut jobs = Vec::new();
        let mut entries = Vec::new();
        for (path, stamp) in files {
            let old = self.entries.iter().find(|e| e.path == path).cloned();
            let entry = match old {
                Some(mut e) if e.checked == Some(stamp) || e.stamp == stamp => {
                    e.stamp = stamp;
                    e
                }
                Some(mut e) => {
                    e.stamp = stamp;
                    e.check = e.check.stale();
                    jobs.push(Job::Check {
                        path: path.clone(),
                        stamp,
                    });
                    e
                }
                None => {
                    jobs.push(Job::Check {
                        path: path.clone(),
                        stamp,
                    });
                    Entry {
                        name: path
                            .file_name()
                            .map_or_else(String::new, |n| n.to_string_lossy().into_owned()),
                        path,
                        stamp,
                        check: Bg::Running,
                        checked: None,
                    }
                }
            };
            entries.push(entry);
        }
        self.entries = entries;
        self.selected = self.selected.min(self.shown().len().saturating_sub(1));
        jobs
    }

    /// A check's result; one for a stamp that has changed since stays stale.
    pub fn checked(&mut self, path: &PathBuf, stamp: Stamp, file: Result<PlanFile, String>) {
        let Some(entry) = self.entries.iter_mut().find(|e| &e.path == path) else {
            return;
        };
        entry.checked = Some(stamp);
        entry.check = match file {
            Ok(file) if entry.stamp == stamp => Bg::Current(file),
            Ok(file) => Bg::Stale(file),
            Err(e) => Bg::Failed(e),
        };
    }

    /// The parsed plan of `path`, when its check found one.
    pub fn parsed(&self, path: &PathBuf) -> Option<Arc<Parsed>> {
        let entry = self.entries.iter().find(|e| &e.path == path)?;
        entry.check.value()?.parsed.clone()
    }

    /// How many shown plans have a journal without `run-end`.
    pub fn interrupted(&self) -> usize {
        self.entries
            .iter()
            .filter(|e| matches!(e.check.value().map(|f| &f.run), Some(RunState::Interrupted)))
            .count()
    }

    /// Handles `key`.
    pub fn key(&mut self, key: Key) -> Outcome {
        if self.editing {
            return self.filter_key(key);
        }
        let shown = self.shown().len();
        match key {
            Key::Up | Key::Char('k') => self.selected = self.selected.saturating_sub(1),
            Key::Down | Key::Char('j') => {
                self.selected = (self.selected + 1).min(shown.saturating_sub(1));
            }
            Key::Home => self.selected = 0,
            Key::End => self.selected = shown.saturating_sub(1),
            Key::Char('/') => {
                self.editing = true;
                self.filter.get_or_insert_with(String::new);
            }
            Key::Esc if self.filter.is_some() => {
                self.filter = None;
                self.selected = 0;
            }
            Key::Enter => return self.with_current(Outcome::Inspect),
            Key::Char('R') => return self.with_current(Outcome::Run),
            Key::Char('c') => {
                let jobs = self
                    .shown()
                    .iter()
                    .map(|e| Job::Check {
                        path: e.path.clone(),
                        stamp: e.stamp,
                    })
                    .collect();
                for e in &mut self.entries {
                    e.check = std::mem::replace(&mut e.check, Bg::Running).stale();
                }
                return Outcome::Check(jobs);
            }
            _ => return Outcome::Unused,
        }
        Outcome::None
    }

    fn with_current(&self, f: fn(PathBuf) -> Outcome) -> Outcome {
        match self.current() {
            Some(e) if e.check.value().is_some_and(|f| f.parsed.is_some()) => f(e.path.clone()),
            _ => Outcome::None,
        }
    }

    fn filter_key(&mut self, key: Key) -> Outcome {
        let filter = self.filter.get_or_insert_with(String::new);
        match key {
            Key::Char(c) => filter.push(c),
            Key::Backspace => {
                filter.pop();
            }
            Key::Enter => self.editing = false,
            Key::Esc => {
                self.editing = false;
                self.filter = None;
            }
            _ => return Outcome::None,
        }
        self.selected = 0;
        Outcome::None
    }

    /// The keys the footer shows.
    pub fn footer(&self) -> Vec<(String, String)> {
        if self.editing {
            return vec![
                ("enter".into(), "apply".into()),
                ("esc".into(), "clear".into()),
            ];
        }
        vec![
            ("enter".into(), "inspect".into()),
            ("R".into(), "run…".into()),
            ("c".into(), "re-check".into()),
            ("/".into(), "filter".into()),
            ("ctrl+k".into(), "commands".into()),
            ("?".into(), "help".into()),
        ]
    }

    /// Draws the view in `area` (the body between the header and the footer).
    pub fn draw(&self, buf: &mut Buffer, area: Rect, labels: &Labels, g: Glyphs) {
        let split = area.right().saturating_sub(DETAIL);
        let table = Rect::new(area.x, area.y, split - area.x, area.height);
        let detail = Rect::new(split + 2, area.y, area.right() - split - 2, area.height);
        crate::draw::vrule(buf, split, area.y, area.bottom(), g);
        self.draw_table(buf, table, labels, g);
        self.draw_detail(buf, detail, labels, g);
    }

    fn draw_table(&self, buf: &mut Buffer, area: Rect, labels: &Labels, g: Glyphs) {
        let count = self.entries.len();
        let what = format!(
            "{count} file{} in {}",
            if count == 1 { "" } else { "s" },
            labels.plans
        );
        band(buf, area, true, "PLANS", &what, g);
        let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x, area.y, area.right().saturating_sub(1), g);
        match (&self.filter, self.editing) {
            (Some(f), true) => pen.right(
                &format!("/ {f}{}", g.get(Glyph::Caret)),
                fg(Token::Act).bg(Token::Bg1.color()),
            ),
            (Some(f), false) => pen.right(&format!("/ {f}"), fg(Token::Fg2).bg(Token::Bg1.color())),
            (None, _) => pen.right("/ filter", fg(Token::Fg3).bg(Token::Bg1.color())),
        };
        let y = area.y + 2;
        let head = bold(Token::Fg3);
        let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x + 2, y, area.right(), g);
        pen.cell("PLAN", 25, head)
            .cell("FORMAT", 13, head)
            .cell("TASKS", 6, head)
            .cell("CHECK", 12, head)
            .text("RUN", head);
        if let Some(e) = &self.error {
            let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x + 2, y + 1, area.right(), g);
            pen.text(e, fg(Token::Err));
        }
        let legend = area.bottom().saturating_sub(4);
        for (i, entry) in self.shown().iter().enumerate() {
            let row = y + 1 + u16::try_from(i).unwrap_or(u16::MAX);
            if row >= legend {
                break;
            }
            if i == self.selected {
                select_row(buf, area, row, g);
            }
            self.draw_entry(buf, area, row, entry, i == self.selected, g);
        }
        hrule(buf, area.x + 1, area.right().saturating_sub(1), legend, g);
        let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x + 2, legend + 1, area.right(), g);
        let dim = fg(Token::Fg3);
        pen.text("check ", dim)
            .glyph(Glyph::Done, fg(Token::Ok))
            .text(" valid  ", dim)
            .glyph(Glyph::Warning, fg(Token::Warn))
            .text(" warnings  ", dim)
            .glyph(Glyph::Failed, fg(Token::Err))
            .text(" errors  ", dim)
            .glyph(Glyph::Background, fg(Token::Fg2))
            .text(" checking  grey ", dim)
            .glyph(Glyph::Done, dim)
            .text(" stale", dim);
        let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x + 2, legend + 2, area.right(), g);
        pen.text("background checks on start and on file change ", dim)
            .glyph(Glyph::Dot, dim)
            .text(" ", dim)
            .text("c", bold(Token::Fg2))
            .text(" re-checks", dim);
    }

    fn draw_entry(
        &self,
        buf: &mut Buffer,
        area: Rect,
        y: u16,
        e: &Entry,
        selected: bool,
        g: Glyphs,
    ) {
        let bg = if selected { Token::Bg2 } else { Token::Bg0 };
        let on = |t: Token| fg(t).bg(bg.color());
        let name = if selected {
            bold(Token::Fg).bg(bg.color())
        } else {
            on(Token::Fg)
        };
        let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x + 2, y, area.right(), g);
        pen.cell(&e.name, 25, name);
        let file = e.check.value();
        let parsed = file.and_then(|f| f.parsed.as_ref());
        let format = match (parsed, file) {
            (Some(p), _) => p.plan.format.0.to_string(),
            (None, Some(_)) | (None, None) => g.get(Glyph::HRule).to_owned(),
        };
        pen.cell(&format, 13, on(Token::Fg2));
        let tasks = parsed.map_or_else(
            || g.get(Glyph::HRule).to_owned(),
            |p| p.plan.tasks.len().to_string(),
        );
        pen.cell(&text::right(&tasks, 4), 6, on(Token::Fg2));
        let start = pen.x;
        let (glyph, word, style) = check_cell(&e.check);
        if let Some(glyph) = glyph {
            pen.glyph(glyph, on(style)).gap(1);
        }
        let word_style = match &e.check {
            Bg::Current(f) if f.counts().0 == 0 && f.counts().1 > 0 => on(Token::Warn),
            Bg::Current(f) if f.counts().0 > 0 && !f.not_a_plan => on(Token::Err),
            Bg::Running => on(Token::Fg2),
            _ => on(Token::Fg2),
        };
        pen.text(&word, word_style);
        pen.to(start + 12);
        let run = file.filter(|f| !f.not_a_plan).map(|f| &f.run);
        let (glyph, word, style) = run_cell(run, g);
        if let Some(glyph) = glyph {
            pen.glyph(glyph, on(style)).gap(1);
        }
        pen.text(
            &word,
            on(if style == Token::Ok {
                Token::Fg2
            } else {
                style
            }),
        );
    }

    fn draw_detail(&self, buf: &mut Buffer, area: Rect, labels: &Labels, g: Glyphs) {
        let name = self.current().map_or("", |e| e.name.as_str());
        let band_area = Rect::new(area.x - 1, area.y, area.width + 1, 1);
        band(buf, band_area, false, "PLAN", name, g);
        let Some(entry) = self.current() else {
            return;
        };
        let right = area.right().saturating_sub(1);
        let mut y = area.y + 2;
        let file = entry.check.value();
        let parsed = file.and_then(|f| f.parsed.as_ref());
        if let Some(p) = parsed {
            let plan = &p.plan;
            let title = plan
                .meta
                .title
                .as_ref()
                .map_or(entry.name.as_str(), |t| plan.text(t));
            Pen::new(buf, area.x, y, right, g).text(title, bold(Token::Fg));
            y += 1;
            let steps: usize = plan.tasks.iter().map(|t| t.steps.len()).sum();
            let actions: usize = plan
                .tasks
                .iter()
                .flat_map(|t| t.steps.iter())
                .map(|s| {
                    s.actions
                        .iter()
                        .filter(|a| !matches!(a.kind, ActionKind::Note { .. }))
                        .count()
                })
                .sum();
            let dot = g.get(Glyph::Dot);
            let line = format!(
                "{} {dot} {} tasks {dot} {steps} steps {dot} {actions} actions",
                plan.format.0,
                plan.tasks.len()
            );
            Pen::new(buf, area.x, y, right, g).text(&line, fg(Token::Fg2));
            y += 1;
        }
        if let Some(f) = file {
            let dot = g.get(Glyph::Dot);
            let rel = labels.relative(&entry.path);
            let line = format!(
                "{rel} {dot} {} lines {dot} {}",
                f.lines,
                clock_utc(entry.stamp.modified)
            );
            Pen::new(buf, area.x, y, right, g).text(&line, fg(Token::Fg3));
        }
        y += 2;
        if let Some(p) = parsed {
            y = draw_outline(buf, area, y, p, g);
        }
        y = self.draw_check(buf, area, y, entry, g);
        y = draw_status(buf, area, y, file.map(|f| &f.run), labels, g);
        section(
            buf,
            Rect::new(area.x, area.y, right - area.x, area.height),
            y,
            "PREFLIGHT",
            &[],
            g,
        );
        Pen::new(buf, area.x, y + 1, right, g)
            .text("never a background check: it runs right", fg(Token::Fg2));
        Pen::new(buf, area.x, y + 2, right, g)
            .text("before each task, and in every dry run", fg(Token::Fg2));
        if parsed.is_some() && y + 4 < area.bottom() {
            Pen::new(buf, area.x, y + 4, right, g).chip(
                "enter",
                "inspect with a dry run",
                Token::Bg0,
            );
        }
    }

    fn draw_check(&self, buf: &mut Buffer, area: Rect, y: u16, entry: &Entry, g: Glyphs) -> u16 {
        let right = area.right().saturating_sub(1);
        let width = Rect::new(area.x, area.y, right - area.x, area.height);
        let (glyph, word, style) = check_cell(&entry.check);
        let mut tail = Vec::new();
        if let Some(glyph) = glyph {
            tail.push((format!("{} ", g.get(glyph)), fg(style)));
        }
        tail.push((word, fg(Token::Fg2)));
        if let Some(s) = entry.checked {
            tail.push((
                format!(" {} {}", g.get(Glyph::Dot), clock_utc(s.modified)),
                fg(Token::Fg3),
            ));
        }
        section(buf, width, y, "CHECK", &tail, g);
        let mut y = y + 1;
        let Some(file) = entry.check.value() else {
            return y + 1;
        };
        let limit = area.bottom().saturating_sub(12);
        for d in file.diags.iter().take(4) {
            if y >= limit {
                break;
            }
            let (glyph, style) = if d.error {
                (Glyph::Failed, Token::Err)
            } else {
                (Glyph::Warning, Token::Warn)
            };
            let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x, y, right, g);
            pen.glyph(glyph, fg(style))
                .gap(1)
                .text(d.code, fg(Token::Fg));
            if let Some(line) = d.line {
                pen.gap(2).text(&format!("line {line}"), fg(Token::Meta));
            }
            Pen::new(buf, area.x + 2, y + 1, right, g).text(&d.message, fg(Token::Fg2));
            y += 2;
        }
        if let Some(p) = &file.parsed {
            let manual: usize = p.coverage.iter().map(|c| c.manual).sum();
            if manual > 0 && y < limit {
                let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x, y, right, g);
                let s = if manual == 1 { "" } else { "s" };
                pen.glyph(Glyph::Manual, fg(Token::Warn))
                    .gap(1)
                    .text(&format!("{manual} manual action{s}"), fg(Token::Fg));
                Pen::new(buf, area.x + 2, y + 1, right, g)
                    .text("planrun waits for you there", fg(Token::Fg2));
                y += 2;
            }
        }
        y + 1
    }
}

/// The labels the views show for paths.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Labels {
    /// The plans directory, as the footer shows it.
    pub plans: String,
    /// The repository root, to show paths relative to it.
    pub root: PathBuf,
}

impl Labels {
    /// `path` relative to the root with forward slashes, or as it is.
    pub fn relative(&self, path: &std::path::Path) -> String {
        let canonical = std::fs::canonicalize(path).unwrap_or_else(|_| path.to_path_buf());
        match canonical.strip_prefix(&self.root) {
            Ok(rel) => rel.to_string_lossy().replace('\\', "/"),
            Err(_) => path.file_name().map_or_else(
                || path.display().to_string(),
                |n| n.to_string_lossy().into_owned(),
            ),
        }
    }
}

fn draw_outline(buf: &mut Buffer, area: Rect, y: u16, p: &Parsed, g: Glyphs) -> u16 {
    let right = area.right().saturating_sub(1);
    let width = Rect::new(area.x, area.y, right - area.x, area.height);
    section(
        buf,
        width,
        y,
        "OUTLINE",
        &[("coverage".into(), fg(Token::Fg3))],
        g,
    );
    let mut y = y + 1;
    let strip_x = right.saturating_sub(16);
    let rows = usize::from(area.bottom().saturating_sub(y + 16)).max(2);
    for (task, cov) in p.plan.tasks.iter().zip(&p.coverage).take(rows) {
        let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x, y, strip_x.saturating_sub(1), g);
        pen.text(&task.id.raw, fg(Token::Meta))
            .gap(2)
            .text(p.plan.text(&task.title), fg(Token::Fg));
        let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, strip_x, y, right, g);
        for (cell, glyph, token) in strip(cov) {
            pen.text(&g.get(glyph).repeat(cell), fg(token));
        }
        y += 1;
    }
    let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x, y, area.right(), g);
    let dim = fg(Token::Fg3);
    pen.glyph(Glyph::CovAction, fg(Token::Fg2))
        .text(" action ", dim)
        .glyph(Glyph::CovAbsorbed, fg(Token::Meta))
        .text(" absorbed ", dim)
        .glyph(Glyph::CovManifest, dim)
        .text(" manifest ", dim)
        .glyph(Glyph::CovNote, dim)
        .text(" note ", dim)
        .glyph(Glyph::CovAction, fg(Token::Warn))
        .text(" manual", dim);
    y + 2
}

/// The coverage strip of a task: 16 cells shared out by block count (largest remainder), in the
/// order action, absorbed, manifest, note, manual; a non-zero manual count is at least one cell.
pub fn strip(cov: &TaskCoverage) -> Vec<(usize, Glyph, Token)> {
    let parts = [
        (cov.actions, Glyph::CovAction, Token::Fg2),
        (cov.absorbed.len(), Glyph::CovAbsorbed, Token::Meta),
        (cov.manifest, Glyph::CovManifest, Token::Fg3),
        (cov.notes, Glyph::CovNote, Token::Fg3),
        (cov.manual, Glyph::CovAction, Token::Warn),
    ];
    let total: usize = parts.iter().map(|p| p.0).sum();
    if total == 0 {
        return vec![(16, Glyph::CovNote, Token::Rule)];
    }
    let reserved = usize::from(cov.manual > 0);
    let cells = 16 - reserved;
    let mut shares: Vec<(usize, usize)> = parts
        .iter()
        .map(|p| (p.0 * cells / total, p.0 * cells % total))
        .collect();
    let mut left = cells - shares.iter().map(|s| s.0).sum::<usize>();
    while left > 0 {
        let Some(best) = (0..shares.len())
            .filter(|&i| parts[i].0 > 0)
            .max_by_key(|&i| (shares[i].1, std::cmp::Reverse(i)))
        else {
            break;
        };
        shares[best].0 += 1;
        shares[best].1 = 0;
        left -= 1;
    }
    shares[4].0 += reserved;
    parts
        .iter()
        .zip(shares)
        .filter(|(_, s)| s.0 > 0)
        .map(|(p, s)| (s.0, p.1, p.2))
        .collect()
}

fn draw_status(
    buf: &mut Buffer,
    area: Rect,
    y: u16,
    run: Option<&RunState>,
    labels: &Labels,
    g: Glyphs,
) -> u16 {
    let right = area.right().saturating_sub(1);
    let width = Rect::new(area.x, area.y, right - area.x, area.height);
    let (glyph, word, style) = run_cell(run, g);
    let mut tail = Vec::new();
    if let Some(glyph) = glyph {
        tail.push((format!("{} ", g.get(glyph)), fg(style)));
    }
    tail.push((word, fg(Token::Fg2)));
    section(buf, width, y, "STATUS", &tail, g);
    let text = match run {
        None | Some(RunState::NotRun) => {
            format!("no journal in {} for this plan", labels_journal(labels))
        }
        Some(RunState::Done(_)) => "the last run completed".to_owned(),
        Some(RunState::Stopped) => "the last run stopped: resume goes on (F4)".to_owned(),
        Some(RunState::Interrupted) => "no run-end: killed, or running now (F4)".to_owned(),
        Some(RunState::Foreign(other)) => format!("its journal belongs to {other}"),
        Some(RunState::Unreadable(e)) => e.clone(),
    };
    Pen::new(buf, area.x, y + 1, right, g).text(&text, fg(Token::Fg2));
    y + 3
}

fn labels_journal(_labels: &Labels) -> &'static str {
    ".git/planrun"
}

/// The CHECK column: glyph, word and the glyph's colour.
fn check_cell(check: &Bg<PlanFile>) -> (Option<Glyph>, String, Token) {
    match check {
        Bg::Running => (Some(Glyph::Background), "checking".into(), Token::Fg2),
        Bg::Stale(_) => (Some(Glyph::Done), "stale".into(), Token::Fg3),
        Bg::Unavailable(e) | Bg::Failed(e) => (Some(Glyph::Failed), fit_word(e), Token::Err),
        Bg::Current(f) if f.not_a_plan => (None, "not a plan".into(), Token::Fg3),
        Bg::Current(f) => match f.counts() {
            (0, 0) => (Some(Glyph::Done), "valid".into(), Token::Ok),
            (0, w) => (Some(Glyph::Done), format!("{w} warn"), Token::Ok),
            (1, _) => (Some(Glyph::Failed), "1 error".into(), Token::Err),
            (e, _) => (Some(Glyph::Failed), format!("{e} errors"), Token::Err),
        },
    }
}

fn fit_word(e: &str) -> String {
    if e.len() > 9 {
        "unreadable".into()
    } else {
        e.to_owned()
    }
}

/// The RUN column: glyph, word and the glyph's colour.
fn run_cell(run: Option<&RunState>, g: Glyphs) -> (Option<Glyph>, String, Token) {
    match run {
        None => (None, g.get(Glyph::HRule).to_owned(), Token::Fg3),
        Some(RunState::NotRun) => (Some(Glyph::Pending), "not run".into(), Token::Fg3),
        Some(RunState::Done(ms)) => (
            Some(Glyph::Done),
            format!("done {}", date_utc(*ms)),
            Token::Ok,
        ),
        Some(RunState::Stopped) => (Some(Glyph::Failed), "stopped".into(), Token::Err),
        Some(RunState::Interrupted) => (Some(Glyph::Waiting), "interrupted".into(), Token::Warn),
        Some(RunState::Foreign(_)) => (Some(Glyph::Failed), "foreign".into(), Token::Err),
        Some(RunState::Unreadable(_)) => (Some(Glyph::Failed), "unreadable".into(), Token::Err),
    }
}

/// `HH:MM` in UTC (std has no time zone; decision D94).
pub fn clock_utc(ms: u64) -> String {
    let secs = ms / 1000;
    format!("{:02}:{:02}", (secs / 3600) % 24, (secs / 60) % 60)
}

/// `25 Nov` in UTC.
pub fn date_utc(ms: u64) -> String {
    let (_, month, day) = civil(ms);
    format!("{day} {}", MONTHS[month - 1])
}

/// The months, short.
pub const MONTHS: [&str; 12] = [
    "Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec",
];

/// The UTC date of `ms`: year, month (1–12), day (1–31). Howard Hinnant's days-to-civil.
pub fn civil(ms: u64) -> (i64, usize, u64) {
    let days = i64::try_from(ms / 86_400_000).unwrap_or(0);
    let z = days + 719_468;
    let era = z.div_euclid(146_097);
    let doe = z.rem_euclid(146_097);
    let yoe = (doe - doe / 1460 + doe / 36_524 - doe / 146_096) / 365;
    let doy = doe - (365 * yoe + yoe / 4 - yoe / 100);
    let mp = (5 * doy + 2) / 153;
    let day = doy - (153 * mp + 2) / 5 + 1;
    let month = if mp < 10 { mp + 3 } else { mp - 9 };
    let year = yoe + era * 400 + i64::from(month <= 2);
    (
        year,
        usize::try_from(month).unwrap_or(1),
        u64::try_from(day).unwrap_or(1),
    )
}

/// A style for `token` on the selection's background when `selected`.
pub fn row_style(token: Token, selected: bool) -> Style {
    let bg = if selected { Token::Bg2 } else { Token::Bg0 };
    fg(token).bg(bg.color())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn cov(actions: usize, manifest: usize, notes: usize, manual: usize) -> TaskCoverage {
        TaskCoverage {
            task: planrun_model::TaskId {
                raw: "P1-T1".into(),
                ordinal: 1,
            },
            actions,
            absorbed: Vec::new(),
            manifest,
            notes,
            manual,
            dropped: 0,
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_strip_has_sixteen_cells_and_a_manual_cell_when_there_is_one() {
        for c in [
            cov(10, 3, 2, 0),
            cov(30, 1, 1, 1),
            cov(1, 0, 0, 0),
            cov(5, 5, 5, 5),
        ] {
            let cells: usize = strip(&c).iter().map(|s| s.0).sum();
            assert_eq!(cells, 16);
        }
        let s = strip(&cov(30, 1, 1, 1));
        assert_eq!(s.last().map(|p| (p.0 >= 1, p.2)), Some((true, Token::Warn)));
        assert_eq!(
            strip(&cov(1, 0, 0, 0)),
            vec![(16, Glyph::CovAction, Token::Fg2)]
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn dates_and_times_are_utc() {
        assert_eq!(civil(0), (1970, 1, 1));
        // 2026-11-27 12:34:56 UTC
        let ms = 1_795_782_896_000;
        assert_eq!(civil(ms), (2026, 11, 27));
        assert_eq!(date_utc(ms), "27 Nov");
        assert_eq!(clock_utc(ms), "12:34");
    }
}
```

File `crates/planrun-tui/src/terminal.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! `planrun tui` on a real terminal (decision D94): stdin and stdout must be terminals; the
//! repository is the one of the current directory (D88); the plans directory defaults to
//! `docs/plans` under its root. One loop polls crossterm's events and drains the app's channel
//! between polls; there is no input thread, which would read the keys meant for the `[o]` shell.
//! Interrupts are installed once, before the terminal is set up; a panic restores the terminal
//! only when it is the render thread's.

use std::io::IsTerminal;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::thread::ThreadId;
use std::time::Duration;

use ratatui::crossterm::event::{self, Event};

use planrun_exec::shell::install_interrupt_handler;
use planrun_exec::workspace::Workspace;

use crate::app::{App, system_clock};
use crate::driver::Core;
use crate::glyph::Glyphs;
use crate::jobs::{Context, UpdateCheck};
use crate::keys;

/// How long one poll for a key waits before the app's channel is drained again.
const POLL: Duration = Duration::from_millis(50);

/// What planrun-cli hands the TUI.
pub struct Host {
    /// The current directory: its repository is the target.
    pub dir: PathBuf,
    /// The plans directory as given (relative to `dir`), or `None` for `docs/plans` under the
    /// repository's root.
    pub plans: Option<PathBuf>,
    /// planrun's version.
    pub version: String,
    /// The update check.
    pub update: Option<UpdateCheck>,
}

/// The default plans directory, under the repository's root (D94).
pub const DEFAULT_PLANS: &str = "docs/plans";

/// Builds the context for the repository of `dir`.
pub fn context(
    dir: &Path,
    plans: Option<&Path>,
    version: &str,
    env: Vec<(std::ffi::OsString, std::ffi::OsString)>,
) -> Result<Context, String> {
    let ws = Workspace::open(dir, env.clone()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let git_dir = ws.git().git_dir().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let root = ws.root().to_path_buf();
    let plans_dir = match plans {
        Some(p) if p.is_absolute() => p.to_path_buf(),
        Some(p) => dir.join(p),
        None => root.join(DEFAULT_PLANS),
    };
    let canonical = std::fs::canonicalize(&plans_dir).unwrap_or_else(|_| plans_dir.clone());
    let plans_label = match canonical.strip_prefix(&root) {
        Ok(rel) if rel.as_os_str().is_empty() => ".".to_owned(),
        Ok(rel) => rel.to_string_lossy().replace('\\', "/"),
        Err(_) => plans_dir.display().to_string(),
    };
    let repo = root.file_name().map_or_else(
        || root.display().to_string(),
        |n| n.to_string_lossy().into_owned(),
    );
    Ok(Context {
        target: root.display().to_string(),
        repo,
        root,
        git_dir,
        plans_dir,
        plans_label,
        version: version.to_owned(),
        env,
    })
}

/// Runs the TUI until it quits. `Err` is the message for a usage problem: not a terminal, not a
/// repository, or a terminal that could not be set up.
pub fn run(host: Host) -> Result<(), String> {
    if !std::io::stdin().is_terminal() || !std::io::stdout().is_terminal() {
        return Err(
            "planrun tui needs a terminal on stdin and stdout; `planrun run` is the line version"
                .to_owned(),
        );
    }
    let ctx = context(&host.dir, host.plans.as_deref(), &host.version, Vec::new())?;
    let app = App::new(ctx, Glyphs::from_env(), system_clock(), host.update);
    install_interrupt_handler();
    let report = std::panic::take_hook();
    let terminal = ratatui::init();
    install_panic_hook(report);
    let result = drive(Core::new(app, terminal));
    ratatui::restore();
    result
}

/// What a panic does to the terminal (D94).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum OnPanic {
    /// The render thread's: restore the terminal, then report as Rust does.
    Restore,
    /// Another thread's (the runner's, a job's): leave the terminal and print nothing; the
    /// thread catches the panic and the app shows it as that work's failure.
    Leave,
}

/// What a panic on `current` does, when `render` owns the terminal.
pub fn on_panic(render: ThreadId, current: ThreadId) -> OnPanic {
    if render == current {
        OnPanic::Restore
    } else {
        OnPanic::Leave
    }
}

/// The hook a panic report goes through.
type Report = Box<dyn Fn(&std::panic::PanicHookInfo<'_>) + Sync + Send>;

/// Replaces ratatui's hook, which would restore the terminal on every thread's panic, caught or
/// not: `report` (the hook before `ratatui::init`) runs only for the render thread's.
fn install_panic_hook(report: Report) {
    let render = std::thread::current().id();
    std::panic::set_hook(Box::new(move |info| {
        if on_panic(render, std::thread::current().id()) == OnPanic::Restore {
            ratatui::restore();
            report(info);
        }
    }));
}

fn drive<B: ratatui::backend::Backend>(mut core: Core<B>) -> Result<(), String> {
    let term = |e: B::Error| format!("the terminal: {e}");
    let io = |e: std::io::Error| format!("the terminal: {e}");
    core.start();
    core.draw().map_err(term)?;
    while !core.done() {
        core.pump();
        if event::poll(POLL).map_err(io)? {
            match event::read().map_err(io)? {
                Event::Key(ev) => {
                    if let Some(key) = keys::key(&ev) {
                        core.key(key);
                    }
                }
                Event::Resize(..) => core.redraw().map_err(term)?,
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        core.tick();
        core.pump();
        core.draw().map_err(term)?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn only_the_render_thread_s_panic_restores_the_terminal() {
        let render = std::thread::current().id();
        let other = std::thread::spawn(|| std::thread::current().id())
            .join()
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| panic!("the thread panicked"));
        assert_eq!(on_panic(render, render), OnPanic::Restore);
        assert_eq!(on_panic(render, other), OnPanic::Leave);
    }
}
```

```rust
//! Text measured in cells (`unicode-width`), cut and padded to a width.

use unicode_width::{UnicodeWidthChar, UnicodeWidthStr};

/// The width of `s` in cells.
pub fn width(s: &str) -> usize {
    s.width()
}

/// `s` cut to at most `cells` cells; when cut, its last cell is `ellipsis`.
pub fn fit(s: &str, cells: usize, ellipsis: &str) -> String {
    if s.width() <= cells {
        return s.to_owned();
    }
    if cells == 0 {
        return String::new();
    }
    let mut out = String::new();
    let mut used = 0;
    for c in s.chars() {
        let w = c.width().unwrap_or(0);
        if used + w > cells - 1 {
            break;
        }
        out.push(c);
        used += w;
    }
    out.push_str(ellipsis);
    out
}

/// `s` cut to `cells` cells and padded with spaces to exactly that width.
pub fn pad(s: &str, cells: usize, ellipsis: &str) -> String {
    let mut out = fit(s, cells, ellipsis);
    let w = out.width();
    out.extend(std::iter::repeat_n(' ', cells.saturating_sub(w)));
    out
}

/// `s` padded on the left to `cells` cells (never cut).
pub fn right(s: &str, cells: usize) -> String {
    let w = s.width();
    let mut out: String = std::iter::repeat_n(' ', cells.saturating_sub(w)).collect();
    out.push_str(s);
    out
}

/// A duration for the tree and the console: `0.1s`, `12.4s`, `3m05s`, `1h02m`.
pub fn duration(ms: u64) -> String {
    let secs = ms / 1000;
    if secs < 60 {
        return format!("{}.{}s", secs, (ms % 1000) / 100);
    }
    if secs < 3600 {
        return format!("{}m{:02}s", secs / 60, secs % 60);
    }
    format!("{}h{:02}m", secs / 3600, (secs % 3600) / 60)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn fitting_cuts_with_an_ellipsis_and_pads() {
        assert_eq!(fit("src/lib.rs", 20, "…"), "src/lib.rs");
        assert_eq!(fit("New-Item docs/modules.txt", 12, "…"), "New-Item do…");
        assert_eq!(width(&fit("New-Item docs/modules.txt", 12, "…")), 12);
        assert_eq!(pad("ab", 4, "…"), "ab  ");
        assert_eq!(right("7", 3), "  7");
        assert_eq!(fit("abc", 0, "…"), "");
    }

    #[test]
    fn durations_read_at_a_glance() {
        assert_eq!(duration(140), "0.1s");
        assert_eq!(duration(12_400), "12.4s");
        assert_eq!(duration(185_000), "3m05s");
        assert_eq!(duration(3_720_000), "1h02m");
    }
}
```

```rust
//! The colour tokens (docs/tui-design.md §4, decision D89). v1 renders 24-bit colour only; each
//! token keeps its 256-colour index for the fallback that comes later.
//!
//! The rules: green only after verification, amber only when planrun waits for you (or for a
//! warning), red for failure and the Destructive class, blue for focus and the running action,
//! purple for identifiers. Bold and reverse are the only modifiers.

use ratatui::style::{Color, Modifier, Style};

/// One colour token.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
pub enum Token {
    /// App background.
    Bg0,
    /// Header, footer, panel titles, the dock's band.
    Bg1,
    /// Selection, key chips, inputs, overlays.
    Bg2,
    /// Hairlines between panels.
    Rule,
    /// The overlay frame.
    Border,
    /// Primary text.
    Fg,
    /// Secondary text.
    Fg2,
    /// Tertiary text: line numbers, timestamps.
    Fg3,
    /// Verified, done.
    Ok,
    /// planrun waits for you; warnings.
    Warn,
    /// Failure, error, the Destructive class.
    Err,
    /// Focus, the running action.
    Act,
    /// Hashes, ids, versions, absorbed blocks.
    Meta,
    /// A diff's added row.
    AddBg,
    /// A diff's deleted row.
    DelBg,
}

impl Token {
    /// Every token, in the design's order.
    pub const ALL: [Token; 15] = [
        Token::Bg0,
        Token::Bg1,
        Token::Bg2,
        Token::Rule,
        Token::Border,
        Token::Fg,
        Token::Fg2,
        Token::Fg3,
        Token::Ok,
        Token::Warn,
        Token::Err,
        Token::Act,
        Token::Meta,
        Token::AddBg,
        Token::DelBg,
    ];

    /// Its 24-bit value.
    pub fn rgb(self) -> (u8, u8, u8) {
        match self {
            Token::Bg0 => (0x11, 0x18, 0x20),
            Token::Bg1 => (0x19, 0x23, 0x2E),
            Token::Bg2 => (0x26, 0x34, 0x42),
            Token::Rule => (0x2E, 0x3D, 0x4C),
            Token::Border => (0x52, 0x65, 0x79),
            Token::Fg => (0xD8, 0xE1, 0xE9),
            Token::Fg2 => (0x91, 0xA3, 0xB5),
            Token::Fg3 => (0x6C, 0x80, 0x94),
            Token::Ok => (0x69, 0xB9, 0x95),
            Token::Warn => (0xD9, 0xA8, 0x5F),
            Token::Err => (0xD8, 0x75, 0x75),
            Token::Act => (0x75, 0xA9, 0xD5),
            Token::Meta => (0xB6, 0x9B, 0xD9),
            Token::AddBg => (0x1B, 0x32, 0x29),
            Token::DelBg => (0x3A, 0x22, 0x28),
        }
    }

    /// Its index in the 256-colour palette, for the fallback mode (not used in v1).
    pub fn index256(self) -> u8 {
        match self {
            Token::Bg0 => 234,
            Token::Bg1 | Token::AddBg => 235,
            Token::Bg2 | Token::DelBg => 236,
            Token::Rule => 237,
            Token::Border => 60,
            Token::Fg => 254,
            Token::Fg2 => 109,
            Token::Fg3 => 66,
            Token::Ok => 72,
            Token::Warn => 179,
            Token::Err => 174,
            Token::Act => 110,
            Token::Meta => 140,
        }
    }

    /// Its name in the design (`bg0`, `fg2`, …).
    pub fn name(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Token::Bg0 => "bg0",
            Token::Bg1 => "bg1",
            Token::Bg2 => "bg2",
            Token::Rule => "rule",
            Token::Border => "border",
            Token::Fg => "fg",
            Token::Fg2 => "fg2",
            Token::Fg3 => "fg3",
            Token::Ok => "ok",
            Token::Warn => "warn",
            Token::Err => "err",
            Token::Act => "act",
            Token::Meta => "meta",
            Token::AddBg => "addbg",
            Token::DelBg => "delbg",
        }
    }

    /// The ratatui colour.
    pub fn color(self) -> Color {
        let (r, g, b) = self.rgb();
        Color::Rgb(r, g, b)
    }

    /// The token whose colour is `color`, if any.
    pub fn of(color: Color) -> Option<Token> {
        Token::ALL.into_iter().find(|t| t.color() == color)
    }
}

/// Text in `fg` on the background of whatever is under it.
pub fn fg(fg: Token) -> Style {
    Style::new().fg(fg.color())
}

/// Text in `fg` on `bg`.
pub fn on(fg: Token, bg: Token) -> Style {
    Style::new().fg(fg.color()).bg(bg.color())
}

/// Bold text in `fg`.
pub fn bold(fg: Token) -> Style {
    Style::new().fg(fg.color()).add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD)
}

/// The app's background with primary text.
pub fn base() -> Style {
    on(Token::Fg, Token::Bg0)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn every_token_has_its_own_colour_and_name() {
        for (i, a) in Token::ALL.iter().enumerate() {
            for b in &Token::ALL[i + 1..] {
                assert_ne!(a.rgb(), b.rgb(), "{} and {}", a.name(), b.name());
                assert_ne!(a.name(), b.name());
            }
            assert_eq!(Token::of(a.color()), Some(*a));
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_256_indices_are_the_design_s() {
        let indices: Vec<u8> = Token::ALL.iter().map(|t| t.index256()).collect();
        assert_eq!(
            indices,
            [
                234, 235, 236, 237, 60, 254, 109, 66, 72, 179, 174, 110, 140, 235, 236
            ]
        );
    }
}
```

```rust
//! The Plans view (docs/tui-design.md §6.A) on the greeter fixture with the synthetic plans.

mod support;

use planrun_tui::keys::Key;
use support::{PLANS, screen, setup};

#[test]
fn the_plans_view_lists_every_file_with_its_check_and_run() {
    let setup = setup(&PLANS);
    let mut core = setup.core();
    core.key(Key::Down);
    insta::assert_snapshot!(screen(&mut core));
}

#[test]
fn the_filter_narrows_the_table_and_esc_clears_it() {
    let setup = setup(&PLANS);
    let mut core = setup.core();
    for key in [
        Key::Char('/'),
        Key::Char('d'),
        Key::Char('e'),
        Key::Char('v'),
        Key::Enter,
    ] {
        core.key(key);
    }
    let names: Vec<String> = core
        .app()
        .plans()
        .shown()
        .iter()
        .map(|e| e.name.clone())
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(names, ["devready-style.md"]);
    core.key(Key::Esc);
    assert_eq!(core.app().plans().shown().len(), PLANS.len());
}

#[test]
fn below_100_by_30_the_tui_says_it_is_too_small() {
    let setup = setup(&PLANS[..1]);
    let mut core = setup.core();
    let backend = ratatui::backend::TestBackend::new(80, 24);
    let mut small = planrun_tui::driver::Core::new(
        setup.app(),
        ratatui::Terminal::new(backend).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}")),
    );
    let text = screen(&mut small);
    assert!(text.contains("terminal too small"), "{text}");
    assert!(text.contains("needs 100 × 30"), "{text}");
    let _ = screen(&mut core);
}

#[test]
fn a_job_that_panics_comes_back_in_its_failed_state() {
    let setup = setup(&PLANS[..1]);
    let app = setup.app_with(Some(Box::new(|| panic!("the update check broke"))));
    let terminal = ratatui::Terminal::new(ratatui::backend::TestBackend::new(120, 36))
        .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
    let mut core = planrun_tui::driver::Core::new(app, terminal);
    core.start();
    let failed = core.wait(std::time::Duration::from_secs(30), |app| {
        app.update() == &planrun_tui::jobs::UpdateState::Failed
    });
    assert!(failed, "the panicked job never reported");
    assert!(screen(&mut core).contains("update check failed"));
}

#[test]
fn q_quits_from_plans() {
    let setup = setup(&PLANS[..1]);
    let mut core = setup.core();
    core.key(Key::Char('q'));
    assert!(core.done());
}
```

File `crates/planrun-tui/tests/support/mod.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! The TUI's test harness (decision D93): a greeter fixture copy, the synthetic plans in a
//! plans directory beside it, an app with a fixed clock and fixed labels, and a core over a
//! 120 × 36 `TestBackend`.

#![allow(dead_code)]

#[path = "../../../../tests/support/fixture.rs"]
pub mod fixture;

use std::fs::File;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::{Duration, SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

use ratatui::Terminal;
use ratatui::backend::TestBackend;

use fixture::Fixture;
use planrun_tui::app::App;
use planrun_tui::driver::Core;
use planrun_tui::glyph::Glyphs;
use planrun_tui::grid::grid;
use planrun_tui::jobs::Bg;
use planrun_tui::terminal::context;

/// 2026-11-27 12:34:56 UTC.
pub const NOW: u64 = 1_795_782_896_000;

/// The synthetic plans, and the corpus README, which is not a plan.
pub const PLANS: [&str; 5] = [
    "README.md",
    "assay-style.md",
    "before-after-style.md",
    "devready-style.md",
    "superpowers-style.md",
];

/// A fixture with its plans directory.
pub struct Setup {
    /// The repository.
    pub fixture: Fixture,
    /// The plans directory, outside the repository.
    pub plans: PathBuf,
}

/// The greeter fixture and a plans directory with `plans`, each file's time set to [`NOW`].
pub fn setup(plans: &[&str]) -> Setup {
    let fixture = Fixture::greeter(false);
    let dir = fixture.outside().join("plans");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
    let corpus =
        std::path::Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../../tests/corpus/synthetic");
    for name in plans {
        let target = dir.join(name);
        std::fs::copy(corpus.join(name), &target).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{name}: {e}"));
        let file = File::options()
            .write(true)
            .open(&target)
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
        file.set_modified(UNIX_EPOCH + Duration::from_millis(NOW))
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
    }
    Setup {
        fixture,
        plans: dir,
    }
}

impl Setup {
    /// The app on this setup, with the labels of the design's artboards.
    pub fn app(&self) -> App {
        self.app_with(None)
    }

    /// The app with `update` as its update check.
    pub fn app_with(&self, update: Option<planrun_tui::jobs::UpdateCheck>) -> App {
        let mut ctx = context(
            self.fixture.path(),
            Some(&self.plans),
            "0.1.0",
            self.fixture.env(),
        )
        .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
        ctx.repo = "greeter".to_owned();
        ctx.target = r"C:\src\greeter".to_owned();
        ctx.plans_label = "./plans".to_owned();
        App::new(ctx, Glyphs::unicode(), Arc::new(|| NOW), update)
    }

    /// A started core whose plans are all checked.
    pub fn core(&self) -> Core<TestBackend> {
        let terminal = Terminal::new(TestBackend::new(120, 36)).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
        let mut core = Core::new(self.app(), terminal);
        core.start();
        let n = std::fs::read_dir(&self.plans).map_or(0, |d| d.count());
        let checked = core.wait(Duration::from_secs(60), |app| {
            let entries = &app.plans().entries;
            entries.len() == n && entries.iter().all(|e| matches!(e.check, Bg::Current(_)))
        });
        assert!(checked, "the plans were not checked in time");
        core
    }
}

/// The screen as text, for a snapshot.
pub fn screen(core: &mut Core<TestBackend>) -> String {
    core.draw().unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
    grid(core.terminal().backend().buffer())
}

/// The current time, for tests that wait.
pub fn now() -> SystemTime {
    SystemTime::now()
}
```

**Step 2 — verify**

### P6c-T3: the plan view in Inspect

**Goal:** `enter` on a plan opens the plan view in Inspect: the navigator (Task → Step → Action, folding), ACTION (kind, class and reasons, the policy's decision under the assumed policy, the dry run's verdict, the plan line, the planned diff), DRY RUN (every verdict, advisory) and the policy picker `a`, with the dry run as a background job (D84).
