File `crates/planrun-tui/src/diff.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! Unified diffs as rows (docs/tui-design.md §6.B/C): the planned diff of a file action in
//! Inspect, and `git diff HEAD` of a task's files in Run. Split view and syntax highlighting are
//! not in v1.

use ratatui::buffer::Buffer;
use ratatui::layout::Rect;

use planrun_model::{ActionKind, Plan, WriteMode};

use crate::draw::{Pen, fill};
use crate::glyph::{Glyph, Glyphs};
use crate::text;
use crate::theme::{Token, bold, fg, on};

/// What a row of a diff is.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Kind {
    /// An unchanged line.
    Context,
    /// An added line.
    Add,
    /// A deleted line.
    Del,
    /// A note about the diff (`@@`, `deleted`, `overwrites the file on disk`).
    Note,
}

/// One row: the old and new line numbers, what it is, its text.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Row {
    /// The line in the old file.
    pub old: Option<u32>,
    /// The line in the new file.
    pub new: Option<u32>,
    /// What it is.
    pub kind: Kind,
    /// The text without its sign.
    pub text: String,
}

/// A file's diff.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct FileDiff {
    /// The path.
    pub path: String,
    /// Its rows.
    pub rows: Vec<Row>,
}

impl FileDiff {
    /// Added and deleted lines.
    pub fn counts(&self) -> (usize, usize) {
        let add = self.rows.iter().filter(|r| r.kind == Kind::Add).count();
        let del = self.rows.iter().filter(|r| r.kind == Kind::Del).count();
        (add, del)
    }
}

fn row(old: Option<u32>, new: Option<u32>, kind: Kind, text: &str) -> Row {
    Row {
        old,
        new,
        kind,
        text: text.to_owned(),
    }
}

/// Parses unified diff text (a plan's patch, or `git diff` output) into one diff per file.
pub fn parse(patch: &str) -> Vec<FileDiff> {
    let mut files: Vec<FileDiff> = Vec::new();
    let (mut old, mut new) = (0u32, 0u32);
    for line in patch.lines() {
        if let Some(rest) = line.strip_prefix("diff --git ") {
            let path = rest.rsplit(" b/").next().unwrap_or(rest).to_owned();
            files.push(FileDiff {
                path,
                rows: Vec::new(),
            });
            continue;
        }
        if line.starts_with("+++ ") || line.starts_with("--- ") || line.starts_with("index ") {
            continue;
        }
        let Some(file) = files.last_mut() else {
            continue;
        };
        if let Some(hunk) = line.strip_prefix("@@ ") {
            (old, new) = hunk_starts(hunk);
            file.rows.push(row(None, None, Kind::Note, line));
            continue;
        }
        if let Some(text) = line.strip_prefix('+') {
            file.rows.push(row(None, Some(new), Kind::Add, text));
            new += 1;
        } else if let Some(text) = line.strip_prefix('-') {
            file.rows.push(row(Some(old), None, Kind::Del, text));
            old += 1;
        } else if let Some(text) = line.strip_prefix(' ').or(line.is_empty().then_some("")) {
            file.rows
                .push(row(Some(old), Some(new), Kind::Context, text));
            old += 1;
            new += 1;
        } else {
            file.rows.push(row(None, None, Kind::Note, line));
        }
    }
    files
}

/// `-a,b +c,d @@` → (a, c).
fn hunk_starts(hunk: &str) -> (u32, u32) {
    let mut parts = hunk.split_whitespace();
    let start = |p: Option<&str>| {
        p.and_then(|p| p[1..].split(',').next().and_then(|n| n.parse().ok()))
            .unwrap_or(1)
    };
    let old = start(parts.next());
    let new = start(parts.next());
    (old, new)
}

/// The planned diff of a file action, from the plan alone.
pub fn planned(plan: &Plan, kind: &ActionKind) -> Option<FileDiff> {
    let file = |path: &dyn ToString, rows| FileDiff {
        path: path.to_string(),
        rows,
    };
    match kind {
        ActionKind::WriteFile { path, body, mode } => {
            let mut rows: Vec<Row> = plan
                .text(body)
                .lines()
                .enumerate()
                .map(|(i, l)| row(None, u32::try_from(i + 1).ok(), Kind::Add, l))
                .collect();
            if *mode == WriteMode::Overwrite {
                rows.insert(
                    0,
                    row(None, None, Kind::Note, "overwrites the whole file on disk"),
                );
            }
            Some(file(path, rows))
        }
        ActionKind::ApplyPatch { patch, path, .. } => {
            let mut parsed = parse(plan.text(patch));
            let rows = if parsed.is_empty() {
                Vec::new()
            } else {
                parsed.remove(0).rows
            };
            Some(file(path, rows))
        }
        ActionKind::ReplaceExact {
            path,
            before,
            after,
        } => {
            let mut rows: Vec<Row> = plan
                .text(before)
                .lines()
                .map(|l| row(None, None, Kind::Del, l))
                .collect();
            rows.extend(
                plan.text(after)
                    .lines()
                    .map(|l| row(None, None, Kind::Add, l)),
            );
            Some(file(path, rows))
        }
        ActionKind::Delete { path, via_git } => {
            let what = if *via_git {
                "deleted (git rm)"
            } else {
                "deleted"
            };
            Some(file(path, vec![row(None, None, Kind::Note, what)]))
        }
        _ => None,
    }
}

/// The diff's band: the path in bold, `+n −m`, the change-state chip, and `unified` on the
/// right (the split view, `s` at 160 columns, is not in v1).
pub fn header(
    buf: &mut Buffer,
    area: Rect,
    diff: &FileDiff,
    chip: (&str, Token),
    detail: &str,
    g: Glyphs,
) {
    let rowr = Rect::new(area.x, area.y, area.width, 1);
    fill(buf, rowr, on(Token::Fg, Token::Bg1));
    let (add, del) = diff.counts();
    let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x + 1, area.y, area.right(), g);
    pen.text(&diff.path, bold(Token::Fg).bg(Token::Bg1.color()))
        .gap(2);
    pen.text(
        &format!("+{add} {}{del}", g.get(Glyph::Delete)),
        on(Token::Fg2, Token::Bg1),
    )
    .gap(4);
    pen.text(
        &format!(" {} ", chip.0),
        bold(chip.1).bg(Token::Bg2.color()),
    );
    if !detail.is_empty() {
        pen.gap(2).text(detail, on(Token::Fg3, Token::Bg1));
    }
    let x = pen.x;
    let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, x, area.y, area.right().saturating_sub(2), g);
    pen.right("unified", on(Token::Fg3, Token::Bg1));
}

/// Draws `rows` from `skip` in `area`: old and new line numbers, the sign, the text; added and
/// deleted rows tinted.
pub fn draw_rows(buf: &mut Buffer, area: Rect, rows: &[Row], skip: usize, g: Glyphs) {
    for (i, r) in rows
        .iter()
        .skip(skip)
        .take(usize::from(area.height))
        .enumerate()
    {
        let y = area.y + u16::try_from(i).unwrap_or(0);
        let (bg, sign, sign_style, text_color) = match r.kind {
            Kind::Context => (Token::Bg0, " ", fg(Token::Fg3), Token::Fg2),
            Kind::Add => (Token::AddBg, "+", bold(Token::Ok), Token::Fg),
            Kind::Del => (Token::DelBg, "-", bold(Token::Err), Token::Fg),
            Kind::Note => (Token::Bg0, " ", fg(Token::Fg3), Token::Fg3),
        };
        if bg != Token::Bg0 {
            fill(buf, Rect::new(area.x, y, area.width, 1), on(Token::Fg, bg));
        }
        let num = |n: Option<u32>| {
            n.map_or_else(|| "    ".to_owned(), |n| text::right(&n.to_string(), 4))
        };
        let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x + 1, y, area.right(), g);
        let numbers = on(Token::Fg3, bg);
        if r.kind == Kind::Note {
            pen.gap(10).text(&r.text, numbers);
            continue;
        }
        pen.text(&num(r.old), numbers)
            .gap(1)
            .text(&num(r.new), numbers)
            .gap(1);
        pen.text(sign, sign_style.bg(bg.color())).gap(1);
        pen.text(&r.text, on(text_color, bg));
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn a_patch_becomes_numbered_rows() {
        let patch = "diff --git a/src/lib.rs b/src/lib.rs\nindex aa80cf1..97bd92a 100644\n--- a/src/lib.rs\n+++ b/src/lib.rs\n@@ -1,3 +1,3 @@\n a\n-b\n+c\n d\n";
        let files = parse(patch);
        assert_eq!(files.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(files[0].path, "src/lib.rs");
        let rows: Vec<(Option<u32>, Option<u32>, Kind)> = files[0]
            .rows
            .iter()
            .map(|r| (r.old, r.new, r.kind))
            .collect();
        assert_eq!(
            rows,
            [
                (None, None, Kind::Note),
                (Some(1), Some(1), Kind::Context),
                (Some(2), None, Kind::Del),
                (None, Some(2), Kind::Add),
                (Some(3), Some(3), Kind::Context),
            ]
        );
        assert_eq!(files[0].counts(), (1, 1));
    }
}
```

File `crates/planrun-tui/src/inspect.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! The plan view in Inspect mode (docs/tui-design.md §6.B, decision D84): filled from a dry run
//! (D55) and the assumed policy. ACTION shows the selected action's class and reasons, the
//! policy's decision, the dry run's verdict, its plan line and its planned diff; DRY RUN lists
//! every verdict, marked advisory: the real preflight decides at run time. `a` changes the
//! assumed policy, and `R` runs with exactly that policy.

use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::Arc;

use ratatui::buffer::Buffer;
use ratatui::layout::Rect;
use ratatui::style::Style;

use planrun_exec::finding::{Finding, Severity};
use planrun_exec::policy::{Decision, DefaultClassifier, Policy, PolicyClass};
use planrun_exec::preflight::{ActionReport, DryRun, Outcome};
use planrun_model::{Action, ActionKind, PathOrigin, Plan};
use planrun_parse::Parsed;

use crate::diff::{self, FileDiff};
use crate::draw::{Pen, band};
use crate::glyph::{Glyph, Glyphs};
use crate::jobs::Bg;
use crate::keys::Key;
use crate::theme::{Token, bold, fg, on};
use crate::tree::{self, Mark, Node, Tree};

/// The panel with focus.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Default)]
pub enum Focus {
    /// The navigator.
    #[default]
    Tree,
    /// ACTION.
    Action,
    /// DRY RUN.
    DryRun,
}

/// The policy Inspect assumes, and `R` runs with (D84).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Assumed {
    /// `--auto`.
    pub auto: Vec<PolicyClass>,
    /// `--run-expected-failures`.
    pub expected: bool,
}

impl Assumed {
    /// The policy; Destructive is never among `auto`, so it always builds.
    pub fn policy(&self) -> Policy {
        Policy::new(&self.auto, self.expected).unwrap_or_default()
    }

    /// `default`, or `auto read,build` and `expected failures run`.
    pub fn describe(&self) -> String {
        if self.auto.is_empty() && !self.expected {
            return "default".to_owned();
        }
        let mut parts = Vec::new();
        if !self.auto.is_empty() {
            parts.push(format!("auto {}", self.auto_words()));
        }
        if self.expected {
            parts.push("expected failures run".to_owned());
        }
        parts.join(" · ")
    }

    /// `read,build`, as `--auto` takes it.
    pub fn auto_words(&self) -> String {
        let words: Vec<&str> = self.auto.iter().map(|c| class_word(*c)).collect();
        words.join(",")
    }

    fn toggle(&mut self, class: PolicyClass) {
        if let Some(i) = self.auto.iter().position(|c| *c == class) {
            self.auto.remove(i);
        } else {
            self.auto.push(class);
            self.auto.sort();
        }
    }
}

/// A class as `--auto` spells it.
pub fn class_word(class: PolicyClass) -> &'static str {
    match class {
        PolicyClass::Read => "read",
        PolicyClass::Build => "build",
        PolicyClass::Modify => "modify",
        PolicyClass::Destructive => "destructive",
    }
}

/// A class as the panels name it.
pub fn class_name(class: PolicyClass) -> &'static str {
    match class {
        PolicyClass::Read => "Read",
        PolicyClass::Build => "Build",
        PolicyClass::Modify => "Modify",
        PolicyClass::Destructive => "Destructive",
    }
}

/// What a key did in Inspect.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Did {
    /// Nothing for the app.
    None,
    /// Run with the assumed policy, from this task when `Some`.
    Run(Option<usize>),
    /// Back to Plans.
    Back,
    /// Not Inspect's key.
    Unused,
}

/// The Inspect state of one plan.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Inspect {
    /// The plan file.
    pub path: PathBuf,
    /// The parsed plan.
    pub parsed: Arc<Parsed>,
    /// The assumed policy.
    pub assumed: Assumed,
    /// The dry run.
    pub dry: Bg<DryRun>,
    /// The navigator.
    pub tree: Tree,
    /// The panel with focus.
    pub focus: Focus,
    /// The first row of ACTION's body shown.
    pub action_top: usize,
    /// The first row of DRY RUN shown.
    pub dry_top: usize,
    /// The policy picker is open.
    pub picking: bool,
}

impl Inspect {
    /// Inspect of `parsed`, its dry run still running.
    pub fn new(path: PathBuf, parsed: Arc<Parsed>) -> Inspect {
        Inspect {
            path,
            parsed,
            assumed: Assumed::default(),
            dry: Bg::Running,
            tree: Tree::default(),
            focus: Focus::Tree,
            action_top: 0,
            dry_top: 0,
            picking: false,
        }
    }

    /// The plan.
    pub fn plan(&self) -> &Plan {
        &self.parsed.plan
    }

    /// Handles `key`.
    pub fn key(&mut self, key: Key) -> Did {
        if self.picking {
            match key {
                Key::Char('r') => self.assumed.toggle(PolicyClass::Read),
                Key::Char('b') => self.assumed.toggle(PolicyClass::Build),
                Key::Char('m') => self.assumed.toggle(PolicyClass::Modify),
                Key::Char('e') => self.assumed.expected = !self.assumed.expected,
                Key::Enter | Key::Esc | Key::Char('a') => self.picking = false,
                _ => {}
            }
            return Did::None;
        }
        match key {
            Key::Tab => {
                self.focus = match self.focus {
                    Focus::Tree => Focus::Action,
                    Focus::Action => Focus::DryRun,
                    Focus::DryRun => Focus::Tree,
                };
                return Did::None;
            }
            Key::BackTab => {
                self.focus = match self.focus {
                    Focus::Tree => Focus::DryRun,
                    Focus::Action => Focus::Tree,
                    Focus::DryRun => Focus::Action,
                };
                return Did::None;
            }
            Key::Char('a') => {
                self.picking = true;
                return Did::None;
            }
            Key::Char('R') if self.dry.value().is_some() => {
                let from = self.tree.current(self.plan()).map(Node::task);
                return Did::Run(from.filter(|t| *t > 0));
            }
            Key::Esc => return Did::Back,
            _ => {}
        }
        let scroll = |top: &mut usize| match key {
            Key::Up | Key::Char('k') => *top = top.saturating_sub(1),
            Key::Down | Key::Char('j') => *top += 1,
            Key::PageUp => *top = top.saturating_sub(10),
            Key::PageDown => *top += 10,
            Key::Home => *top = 0,
            _ => {}
        };
        match self.focus {
            Focus::Tree => {
                let plan = self.parsed.plan.clone();
                if self.tree.key(key, &plan) {
                    self.action_top = 0;
                    return Did::None;
                }
                Did::Unused
            }
            Focus::Action => {
                scroll(&mut self.action_top);
                Did::None
            }
            Focus::DryRun => {
                scroll(&mut self.dry_top);
                Did::None
            }
        }
    }

    /// The report of the action at (`t`, `s`, `a`).
    fn report(&self, t: usize, s: usize, a: usize) -> Option<&ActionReport> {
        let dry = self.dry.value()?;
        let action = &self.plan().tasks.get(t)?.steps.get(s)?.actions.get(a)?;
        dry.tasks
            .get(t)?
            .actions
            .iter()
            .find(|r| r.step == s && r.action.span == action.span)
    }

    /// The decision for the action at (`t`, `s`, `a`) under the assumed policy.
    fn decision(&self, t: usize, s: usize, a: usize) -> Decision {
        let plan = self.plan();
        let task = &plan.tasks[t];
        self.assumed.policy().decide(
            task,
            &task.steps[s].actions[a],
            &plan.source,
            &DefaultClassifier,
        )
    }

    /// The navigator's mark of `node`.
    pub fn mark(&self, node: Node) -> Mark {
        match node {
            Node::Action(t, s, a) => {
                let (glyph, color) = self.status(t, s, a);
                let action = &self.plan().tasks[t].steps[s].actions[a];
                let (tag, tag_color) = tag(action, self.decision(t, s, a));
                Mark {
                    glyph,
                    color,
                    tag,
                    tag_color,
                }
            }
            Node::Step(t, s) => {
                let n = self.plan().tasks[t].steps[s].actions.len();
                let all: Vec<(Glyph, Token)> = (0..n).map(|a| self.status(t, s, a)).collect();
                let (glyph, color) = worst(&all);
                Mark::plain(glyph, color)
            }
            Node::Task(t) => {
                let task = &self.plan().tasks[t];
                let mut all: Vec<(Glyph, Token)> = Vec::new();
                for (s, step) in task.steps.iter().enumerate() {
                    all.extend((0..step.actions.len()).map(|a| self.status(t, s, a)));
                }
                if let Some(report) = self.dry.value().and_then(|d| d.tasks.get(t)) {
                    all.extend(report.manifest.iter().map(finding_mark));
                }
                let (glyph, color) = worst(&all);
                Mark::plain(glyph, color)
            }
        }
    }

    /// The status glyph of an action: the dry run's verdict, or ⊘ when the policy skips it.
    fn status(&self, t: usize, s: usize, a: usize) -> (Glyph, Token) {
        if matches!(self.decision(t, s, a), Decision::Skip(_)) {
            return (Glyph::Skipped, Token::Fg3);
        }
        match self.report(t, s, a).map(|r| &r.outcome) {
            None if self.dry.value().is_none() => (Glyph::Background, Token::Fg2),
            Some(Outcome::Ok(_)) => (Glyph::Done, Token::Ok),
            Some(Outcome::Failed(_)) => (Glyph::Failed, Token::Err),
            Some(Outcome::Depends(_)) => (Glyph::Warning, Token::Warn),
            _ => (Glyph::Dot, Token::Fg3),
        }
    }

    /// The header's right side.
    pub fn state(&self, g: Glyphs) -> Vec<(String, Style)> {
        let bar = |t: Token| on(t, Token::Bg1);
        let mut out = vec![
            (
                " INSPECT ".to_owned(),
                bold(Token::Meta).bg(Token::Bg1.color()),
            ),
            ("  policy ".to_owned(), bar(Token::Fg3)),
            (
                self.assumed.describe(),
                bold(Token::Fg).bg(Token::Bg1.color()),
            ),
            ("  dry run ".to_owned(), bar(Token::Fg3)),
        ];
        let Some(dry) = self.dry.value() else {
            out.push((
                format!("{} running", g.get(Glyph::Background)),
                bar(Token::Fg2),
            ));
            return out;
        };
        let (ok, warn, err) = counts(dry);
        out.push((format!("{} ", g.get(Glyph::Done)), bar(Token::Ok)));
        out.push((format!("{ok} ok"), bar(Token::Fg2)));
        if warn > 0 {
            out.push((format!("  {} ", g.get(Glyph::Warning)), bar(Token::Warn)));
            out.push((format!("{warn} warn"), bar(Token::Warn)));
        }
        if err > 0 {
            out.push((format!("  {} ", g.get(Glyph::Failed)), bar(Token::Err)));
            out.push((format!("{err} failed"), bar(Token::Err)));
        }
        out
    }

    /// The crumbs after the repository: the plan file, the task, the selected step or action.
    pub fn crumbs(&self, g: Glyphs) -> Vec<String> {
        let name = self
            .path
            .file_name()
            .map_or_else(String::new, |n| n.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
        let mut out = vec![name];
        let plan = self.plan();
        let Some(node) = self.tree.current(plan) else {
            return out;
        };
        out.push(plan.tasks[node.task()].id.raw.to_string());
        match node {
            Node::Task(_) => {}
            Node::Step(t, s) => out.push(step_name(plan, t, s, g)),
            Node::Action(t, s, a) => {
                let action = &plan.tasks[t].steps[s].actions[a];
                out.push(format!(
                    "{} {}",
                    g.get(tree::kind_glyph(action)),
                    crumb_label(plan, action)
                ));
            }
        }
        out
    }

    /// The keys the footer shows.
    pub fn footer(&self) -> Vec<(String, String)> {
        if self.picking {
            return vec![
                ("r b m".into(), "auto classes".into()),
                ("e".into(), "expected failures".into()),
                ("enter".into(), "done".into()),
            ];
        }
        vec![
            ("R".into(), "run with this policy".into()),
            ("shift+R".into(), "from this task".into()),
            ("a".into(), "policy…".into()),
            ("enter".into(), "fold".into()),
            ("tab".into(), "panel".into()),
            ("esc".into(), "plans".into()),
        ]
    }

    /// Draws the plan view in `area`.
    pub fn draw(&self, buf: &mut Buffer, area: Rect, rel: &str, g: Glyphs) {
        let plan = self.plan();
        let nav = Rect::new(area.x, area.y, tree::WIDTH, area.height);
        let n = plan.tasks.len();
        let title = format!("{n} task{}", if n == 1 { "" } else { "s" });
        self.tree.draw(
            buf,
            nav,
            plan,
            &title,
            self.focus == Focus::Tree,
            &|node| self.mark(node),
            g,
        );
        let right = Rect::new(
            area.x + tree::WIDTH,
            area.y,
            area.width - tree::WIDTH,
            area.height,
        );
        let upper_h = right.height * 55 / 100;
        let upper = Rect::new(right.x, right.y, right.width, upper_h);
        let lower = Rect::new(
            right.x,
            right.y + upper_h,
            right.width,
            right.height - upper_h,
        );
        self.draw_action(buf, upper, rel, g);
        if self.picking {
            self.draw_picker(buf, lower, g);
        } else {
            self.draw_dry(buf, lower, g);
        }
    }

    fn draw_action(&self, buf: &mut Buffer, area: Rect, rel: &str, g: Glyphs) {
        let plan = self.plan();
        let Some(node) = self.tree.current(plan) else {
            band(buf, area, false, "ACTION", "", g);
            return;
        };
        let line = |span: planrun_model::Span| plan.source.line_col(span.start).line;
        let (title, detail, place) = match node {
            Node::Task(t) => (
                "TASK",
                plan.tasks[t].id.raw.to_string(),
                line(plan.tasks[t].span),
            ),
            Node::Step(t, s) => (
                "STEP",
                step_name(plan, t, s, g),
                line(plan.tasks[t].steps[s].span),
            ),
            Node::Action(t, s, a) => {
                let step = &plan.tasks[t].steps[s];
                let number = step
                    .number
                    .map_or_else(|| g.get(Glyph::Dot).to_owned(), |n| n.to_string());
                let detail = format!("{} of {} in step {number}", a + 1, step.actions.len());
                ("ACTION", detail, line(step.actions[a].span))
            }
        };
        band(buf, area, self.focus == Focus::Action, title, &detail, g);
        Pen::new(buf, area.x, area.y, area.right().saturating_sub(1), g)
            .right(&format!("{rel}:{place}"), on(Token::Fg3, Token::Bg1));
        let body = Rect::new(
            area.x + 2,
            area.y + 2,
            area.width.saturating_sub(3),
            area.height.saturating_sub(2),
        );
        match node {
            Node::Action(t, s, a) => self.draw_action_body(buf, body, t, s, a, g),
            Node::Step(t, s) => self.draw_step_body(buf, body, t, s, g),
            Node::Task(t) => self.draw_task_body(buf, body, t, g),
        }
    }

    fn draw_action_body(
        &self,
        buf: &mut Buffer,
        area: Rect,
        t: usize,
        s: usize,
        a: usize,
        g: Glyphs,
    ) {
        let plan = self.plan();
        let task = &plan.tasks[t];
        let action = &task.steps[s].actions[a];
        let right = area.right();
        let mut y = area.y;
        let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x, y, right, g);
        pen.glyph(tree::kind_glyph(action), bold(tree::kind_color(action)))
            .gap(1);
        pen.text(kind_name(action), bold(Token::Fg));
        if let Some(path) = planrun_exec::handler::file_path(&action.kind) {
            pen.gap(2).text(&path.to_string(), bold(Token::Fg));
        }
        y += 2;
        let label = |buf: &mut Buffer, y: u16, name: &str| -> u16 {
            let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x, y, right, g);
            pen.cell(name, 9, fg(Token::Fg3));
            pen.x
        };
        let decision = self.decision(t, s, a);
        let x = label(buf, y, "class");
        let (class_text, class_color) = class_line(plan, task, action);
        Pen::new(buf, x, y, right, g).text(&class_text, fg(class_color));
        y += 1;
        let x = label(buf, y, "policy");
        Pen::new(buf, x, y, right, g).text(&policy_line(action, decision), fg(Token::Fg2));
        y += 1;
        let x = label(buf, y, "dry run");
        let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, x, y, right, g);
        match self.report(t, s, a) {
            Some(report) => verdict(&mut pen, &report.outcome, g),
            None => {
                pen.glyph(Glyph::Background, fg(Token::Fg2))
                    .text(" running", fg(Token::Fg2));
            }
        }
        y += 2;
        let rest = Rect::new(
            area.x.saturating_sub(1),
            y,
            area.width + 1,
            area.bottom().saturating_sub(y),
        );
        if rest.height == 0 {
            return;
        }
        if let Some(diff) = diff::planned(plan, &action.kind) {
            self.draw_diff(buf, rest, &diff, g);
            return;
        }
        let body = match &action.kind {
            ActionKind::RunScript(script) | ActionKind::Commit(script) => plan.text(&script.body),
            ActionKind::Manual { text, .. } | ActionKind::Note { text } => plan.text(text),
            _ => "",
        };
        for (i, l) in body
            .lines()
            .skip(self.action_top)
            .take(usize::from(rest.height))
            .enumerate()
        {
            let y = rest.y + u16::try_from(i).unwrap_or(0);
            let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, rest.x + 1, y, rest.right(), g);
            pen.text(
                &crate::text::right(&(i + 1 + self.action_top).to_string(), 4),
                fg(Token::Fg3),
            );
            pen.gap(2).text(l, fg(Token::Fg));
        }
    }

    fn draw_diff(&self, buf: &mut Buffer, area: Rect, diff: &FileDiff, g: Glyphs) {
        diff::header(buf, area, diff, ("PLANNED", Token::Fg2), "", g);
        let rows = Rect::new(
            area.x,
            area.y + 1,
            area.width,
            area.height.saturating_sub(1),
        );
        diff::draw_rows(buf, rows, &diff.rows, self.action_top, g);
    }

    fn draw_step_body(&self, buf: &mut Buffer, area: Rect, t: usize, s: usize, g: Glyphs) {
        let plan = self.plan();
        let step = &plan.tasks[t].steps[s];
        let mut y = area.y;
        let title = step.title.as_ref().map_or("", |t| plan.text(t));
        Pen::new(buf, area.x, y, area.right(), g).text(title, bold(Token::Fg));
        y += 2;
        for (a, action) in step.actions.iter().enumerate() {
            if y >= area.bottom() {
                break;
            }
            let mark = self.mark(Node::Action(t, s, a));
            let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x, y, area.right(), g);
            pen.glyph(mark.glyph, bold(mark.color)).gap(1);
            pen.glyph(tree::kind_glyph(action), bold(tree::kind_color(action)))
                .gap(1);
            pen.cell(&tree::label(plan, action), 48, fg(Token::Fg2))
                .gap(1);
            pen.text(&mark.tag, fg(mark.tag_color));
            y += 1;
        }
    }

    fn draw_task_body(&self, buf: &mut Buffer, area: Rect, t: usize, g: Glyphs) {
        let plan = self.plan();
        let task = &plan.tasks[t];
        let mut y = area.y;
        Pen::new(buf, area.x, y, area.right(), g).text(plan.text(&task.title), bold(Token::Fg));
        y += 2;
        Pen::new(buf, area.x, y, area.right(), g).text("FILES", bold(Token::Fg3));
        y += 1;
        if task.manifest.entries.is_empty() {
            Pen::new(buf, area.x, y, area.right(), g)
                .text("no manifest: every file action asks (D50)", fg(Token::Fg2));
            y += 1;
        }
        for entry in &task.manifest.entries {
            if y >= area.bottom() {
                return;
            }
            let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x, y, area.right(), g);
            pen.cell(
                &format!("{:?}", entry.change).to_lowercase(),
                10,
                fg(Token::Fg3),
            );
            pen.text(&entry.path.to_string(), fg(Token::Fg));
            if entry.origin == PathOrigin::Bare {
                pen.gap(2).text("bare: never in scope", fg(Token::Warn));
            }
            y += 1;
        }
        let Some(report) = self.dry.value().and_then(|d| d.tasks.get(t)) else {
            return;
        };
        y += 1;
        for f in &report.manifest {
            if y >= area.bottom() {
                return;
            }
            let (glyph, color) = finding_mark(f);
            let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x, y, area.right(), g);
            pen.glyph(glyph, bold(color))
                .gap(1)
                .text(f.code, fg(Token::Fg))
                .gap(2)
                .text(&f.message, fg(Token::Fg2));
            y += 1;
        }
    }

    fn draw_dry(&self, buf: &mut Buffer, area: Rect, g: Glyphs) {
        band(
            buf,
            area,
            self.focus == Focus::DryRun,
            "DRY RUN",
            &format!(
                "advisory {} preflight decides at run time",
                g.get(Glyph::Dot)
            ),
            g,
        );
        let Some(dry) = self.dry.value() else {
            let text = match &self.dry {
                Bg::Failed(e) | Bg::Unavailable(e) => e.clone(),
                _ => format!("{} running", g.get(Glyph::Background)),
            };
            Pen::new(buf, area.x + 2, area.y + 1, area.right(), g).text(&text, fg(Token::Fg2));
            return;
        };
        let (exit, color) = if dry.has_errors() {
            ("exit 1", Token::Err)
        } else {
            ("exit 0", Token::Ok)
        };
        Pen::new(buf, area.x, area.y, area.right().saturating_sub(1), g)
            .right(exit, on(color, Token::Bg1));
        let lines = self.dry_lines(dry, g);
        for (i, line) in lines
            .iter()
            .skip(self.dry_top)
            .take(usize::from(area.height.saturating_sub(1)))
            .enumerate()
        {
            let y = area.y + 1 + u16::try_from(i).unwrap_or(0);
            let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x + 2, y, area.right(), g);
            pen.glyph(line.glyph, bold(line.color)).gap(1);
            pen.cell(&line.task, 7, fg(Token::Meta));
            pen.cell(&line.place, 11, fg(Token::Fg3));
            pen.text(&line.text, fg(line.text_color));
        }
    }

    fn dry_lines(&self, dry: &DryRun, g: Glyphs) -> Vec<DryLine> {
        let plan = self.plan();
        let mut lines = Vec::new();
        for f in &dry.start {
            let (glyph, color) = finding_mark(f);
            lines.push(DryLine {
                glyph,
                color,
                task: String::new(),
                place: f.code.to_owned(),
                text: f.message.clone(),
                text_color: Token::Fg2,
            });
        }
        if dry.start.is_empty() {
            lines.push(DryLine {
                glyph: Glyph::Done,
                color: Token::Ok,
                task: String::new(),
                place: "dirt".to_owned(),
                text: "none in the paths the plan touches".to_owned(),
                text_color: Token::Fg2,
            });
        }
        for report in &dry.tasks {
            let task = &plan.tasks[report.task];
            for f in &report.manifest {
                let (glyph, color) = finding_mark(f);
                lines.push(DryLine {
                    glyph,
                    color,
                    task: task.id.raw.to_string(),
                    place: "manifest".to_owned(),
                    text: f.message.clone(),
                    text_color: color,
                });
            }
            for r in &report.actions {
                if matches!(r.action.kind, ActionKind::Note { .. }) {
                    continue;
                }
                let step = &task.steps[r.step];
                let number = step
                    .number
                    .map_or_else(|| g.get(Glyph::Dot).to_owned(), |n| n.to_string());
                let a = step
                    .actions
                    .iter()
                    .position(|x| x.span == r.action.span)
                    .unwrap_or(0);
                let decision = self.decision(report.task, r.step, a);
                let (glyph, color) = if matches!(decision, Decision::Skip(_)) {
                    (Glyph::Skipped, Token::Fg3)
                } else {
                    self.status(report.task, r.step, a)
                };
                let glyph = if matches!(r.action.kind, ActionKind::Manual { .. }) {
                    Glyph::Manual
                } else {
                    glyph
                };
                let color = if glyph == Glyph::Manual {
                    Token::Warn
                } else {
                    color
                };
                let (what, text_color) = dry_text(&r.outcome, decision);
                lines.push(DryLine {
                    glyph,
                    color,
                    task: task.id.raw.to_string(),
                    place: format!("step {number}"),
                    text: format!(
                        "{} {}  {what}",
                        g.get(tree::kind_glyph(&r.action)),
                        crate::text::fit(&tree::label(plan, &r.action), 28, g.get(Glyph::Ellipsis))
                    ),
                    text_color,
                });
            }
        }
        lines
    }

    fn draw_picker(&self, buf: &mut Buffer, area: Rect, g: Glyphs) {
        band(
            buf,
            area,
            true,
            "POLICY",
            "assumed for the tags above, and for R",
            g,
        );
        let mut y = area.y + 2;
        let rows = [
            ("r", "read", self.assumed.auto.contains(&PolicyClass::Read)),
            (
                "b",
                "build",
                self.assumed.auto.contains(&PolicyClass::Build),
            ),
            (
                "m",
                "modify",
                self.assumed.auto.contains(&PolicyClass::Modify),
            ),
            ("e", "run expected failures", self.assumed.expected),
        ];
        for (key, name, on_) in rows {
            let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x + 2, y, area.right(), g);
            pen.chip(key, "", Token::Bg0).gap(1);
            let (glyph, color) = if on_ {
                (Glyph::Done, Token::Act)
            } else {
                (Glyph::Pending, Token::Fg3)
            };
            pen.glyph(glyph, bold(color))
                .gap(1)
                .text(name, fg(Token::Fg));
            if key != "e" {
                pen.gap(2).text("runs without asking", fg(Token::Fg3));
            }
            y += 1;
        }
        y += 1;
        Pen::new(buf, area.x + 2, y, area.right(), g).text(
            "Destructive scripts always need a typed yes (D69).",
            fg(Token::Fg2),
        );
        Pen::new(buf, area.x + 2, y + 1, area.right(), g).text(
            &format!(
                "--auto {}",
                if self.assumed.auto.is_empty() {
                    "(none)".to_owned()
                } else {
                    self.assumed.auto_words()
                }
            ),
            fg(Token::Meta),
        );
    }
}

struct DryLine {
    glyph: Glyph,
    color: Token,
    task: String,
    place: String,
    text: String,
    text_color: Token,
}

fn counts(dry: &DryRun) -> (usize, usize, usize) {
    let mut ok = 0;
    for t in &dry.tasks {
        ok += t
            .actions
            .iter()
            .filter(|a| matches!(a.outcome, Outcome::Ok(_) | Outcome::WouldAsk(_)))
            .count();
    }
    let warn = dry
        .findings()
        .filter(|f| f.severity == Severity::Warning)
        .count();
    let err = dry.findings().filter(|f| f.is_error()).count();
    (ok, warn, err)
}

fn finding_mark(f: &Finding) -> (Glyph, Token) {
    if f.is_error() {
        (Glyph::Failed, Token::Err)
    } else {
        (Glyph::Warning, Token::Warn)
    }
}

/// The worst of the marks: failed, then a warning, then done, then all skipped, else not
/// simulated.
fn worst(all: &[(Glyph, Token)]) -> (Glyph, Token) {
    for glyph in [Glyph::Failed, Glyph::Warning, Glyph::Background] {
        if let Some(m) = all.iter().find(|m| m.0 == glyph) {
            return *m;
        }
    }
    if all.iter().any(|m| m.0 == Glyph::Done) {
        return (Glyph::Done, Token::Ok);
    }
    if !all.is_empty() && all.iter().all(|m| m.0 == Glyph::Skipped) {
        return (Glyph::Skipped, Token::Fg3);
    }
    (Glyph::Dot, Token::Fg3)
}

/// The tag of an action in Inspect: what the policy will do.
pub fn tag(action: &Action, decision: Decision) -> (String, Token) {
    if matches!(action.kind, ActionKind::Commit(_)) && decision != Decision::TypedYes {
        return ("gate".into(), Token::Fg2);
    }
    match decision {
        Decision::Auto => ("auto".into(), Token::Fg3),
        Decision::Ask(_) | Decision::AskUnscoped => ("ask".into(), Token::Fg2),
        Decision::TypedYes => ("type yes".into(), Token::Err),
        Decision::Wait => ("waits".into(), Token::Warn),
        Decision::Show => (String::new(), Token::Fg3),
        Decision::Skip(_) => ("skip".into(), Token::Fg3),
    }
}

fn kind_name(action: &Action) -> &'static str {
    match action.kind {
        ActionKind::WriteFile { .. } => "WRITE FILE",
        ActionKind::ApplyPatch { .. } => "APPLY PATCH",
        ActionKind::ReplaceExact { .. } => "REPLACE EXACT",
        ActionKind::Delete { .. } => "DELETE",
        ActionKind::Generated { .. } => "GENERATED",
        ActionKind::RunScript(_) => "RUN SCRIPT",
        ActionKind::Commit(_) => "COMMIT",
        ActionKind::Note { .. } => "NOTE",
        ActionKind::Manual { .. } => "MANUAL",
    }
}

fn class_line(plan: &Plan, task: &planrun_model::Task, action: &Action) -> (String, Token) {
    match &action.kind {
        ActionKind::RunScript(s) | ActionKind::Commit(s) => {
            let c = DefaultClassifier.explain(s.shell, plan.text(&s.body));
            let color = if c.class == PolicyClass::Destructive {
                Token::Err
            } else {
                Token::Fg2
            };
            let mut text = class_name(c.class).to_owned();
            if !c.reasons.is_empty() {
                text.push_str(": ");
                text.push_str(&c.reasons.join("; "));
            }
            (text, color)
        }
        ActionKind::Manual { reason, .. } => (format!("Manual: {reason:?}"), Token::Warn),
        ActionKind::Note { .. } => ("Note: shown, never run".into(), Token::Fg2),
        ActionKind::Generated { .. } => ("checked to exist before the commit".into(), Token::Fg2),
        _ => {
            let Some(path) = planrun_exec::handler::file_path(&action.kind) else {
                return (String::new(), Token::Fg2);
            };
            let entry = task
                .manifest
                .entries
                .iter()
                .find(|e| e.path.file() == Some(path));
            match entry {
                Some(e) if task.manifest.scopes(path) => (
                    format!(
                        "Modify · in task scope (manifest: {} {path})",
                        format!("{:?}", e.change).to_lowercase()
                    ),
                    Token::Fg2,
                ),
                _ => (
                    "Modify · outside the task's files (D50)".into(),
                    Token::Warn,
                ),
            }
        }
    }
}

fn policy_line(action: &Action, decision: Decision) -> String {
    let file = planrun_exec::handler::file_path(&action.kind).is_some()
        && !matches!(action.kind, ActionKind::Generated { .. });
    let what = match decision {
        Decision::Auto if file => {
            "runs without asking: a file action inside the task's scope".to_owned()
        }
        Decision::Auto => "runs without asking".to_owned(),
        Decision::Ask(c) => format!("asks first: {} is not in --auto", class_name(c)),
        Decision::AskUnscoped => "asks first: outside the task's files (D50)".to_owned(),
        Decision::TypedYes => "needs a typed yes: Destructive".to_owned(),
        Decision::Wait => "waits for you: planrun cannot do it".to_owned(),
        Decision::Show => "shown in place".to_owned(),
        Decision::Skip(_) => "skipped: an expected failure (a = run expected failures)".to_owned(),
    };
    if matches!(action.kind, ActionKind::Commit(_)) {
        return format!("behind the commit gate; {what}");
    }
    what
}

fn verdict(pen: &mut Pen<'_>, outcome: &Outcome, _g: Glyphs) {
    match outcome {
        Outcome::Ok(effect) => {
            pen.glyph(Glyph::Done, fg(Token::Ok)).gap(1).text(
                &format!("{effect} {} simulates cleanly", _g.get(Glyph::Dot)),
                fg(Token::Fg2),
            );
        }
        Outcome::WouldAsk(effect) => {
            pen.glyph(Glyph::Dot, fg(Token::Fg3)).gap(1).text(
                &format!("{effect} {} would ask (unscoped)", _g.get(Glyph::Dot)),
                fg(Token::Fg2),
            );
        }
        Outcome::Failed(f) => {
            pen.glyph(Glyph::Failed, fg(Token::Err))
                .gap(1)
                .text(&format!("{}: {}", f.code, f.message), fg(Token::Err));
        }
        Outcome::Depends(f) => {
            pen.glyph(Glyph::Warning, fg(Token::Warn))
                .gap(1)
                .text(&f.message, fg(Token::Warn));
        }
        Outcome::NotRun => {
            pen.glyph(Glyph::Dot, fg(Token::Fg3))
                .gap(1)
                .text("not run in a dry run", fg(Token::Fg2));
        }
        Outcome::NotChecked => {
            pen.glyph(Glyph::Dot, fg(Token::Fg3))
                .gap(1)
                .text("not checked: its script did not run", fg(Token::Fg2));
        }
        Outcome::Waits => {
            pen.glyph(Glyph::Dot, fg(Token::Fg3))
                .gap(1)
                .text("waits for you", fg(Token::Fg2));
        }
        Outcome::Shown => {
            pen.glyph(Glyph::Dot, fg(Token::Fg3))
                .gap(1)
                .text("shown", fg(Token::Fg2));
        }
    }
}

fn dry_text(outcome: &Outcome, decision: Decision) -> (String, Token) {
    if let Decision::Skip(_) = decision {
        return ("skipped: expected failure".into(), Token::Fg2);
    }
    match outcome {
        Outcome::Ok(e) => (format!("{e}, simulates"), Token::Fg2),
        Outcome::WouldAsk(e) => (format!("{e}, would ask"), Token::Fg2),
        Outcome::Failed(f) => (format!("{}: {}", f.code, f.message), Token::Err),
        Outcome::Depends(f) => (f.message.clone(), Token::Warn),
        Outcome::NotRun => match decision {
            Decision::TypedYes => ("typed yes (Destructive)".into(), Token::Fg2),
            Decision::Ask(c) => (format!("would ask ({})", class_name(c)), Token::Fg2),
            _ => ("not run in a dry run".into(), Token::Fg2),
        },
        Outcome::NotChecked => ("not checked".into(), Token::Fg2),
        Outcome::Waits => ("manual: waits for you".into(), Token::Fg2),
        Outcome::Shown => ("shown".into(), Token::Fg2),
    }
}

fn step_name(plan: &Plan, t: usize, s: usize, g: Glyphs) -> String {
    let step = &plan.tasks[t].steps[s];
    match step.number {
        Some(n) => format!("step {n}"),
        None => format!("step {}", g.get(Glyph::Dot)),
    }
}

fn crumb_label(plan: &Plan, action: &Action) -> String {
    let label = tree::label(plan, action);
    match &action.kind {
        ActionKind::RunScript(_) => label.split_whitespace().next().unwrap_or("").to_owned(),
        _ => crate::text::fit(&label, 24, "…"),
    }
}
```

//! The planrun TUI (docs/tui-design.md; decisions D84–D94): a second [`Ui`](planrun_exec::Ui)
//! over the same crates as the line prompts, composed by planrun-cli as `planrun tui`. It shows
//! the domain model and adds no model of its own: resume, policy and preflight stay
//! authoritative; the TUI explains and asks, and never decides.

#![forbid(unsafe_code)]

pub mod app;
pub mod chrome;
pub mod diff;
pub mod draw;
pub mod driver;
pub mod glyph;
pub mod grid;
pub mod inspect;
pub mod jobs;
pub mod keys;
pub mod plans;

pub use terminal::{Host, run};

File `crates/planrun-tui/src/tree.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! The navigator (docs/tui-design.md §6.B/C, decision D87): Task → Step → Action, 36 columns. A
//! row is a status glyph, a kind glyph, a label and a tag; tasks fold. The mode decides each
//! row's status and tag: Inspect from the dry run and the assumed policy, Run from the journal.

use std::collections::BTreeSet;

use ratatui::buffer::Buffer;
use ratatui::layout::Rect;
use ratatui::style::Style;

use planrun_model::{Action, ActionKind, Plan};

use crate::draw::{Pen, band, select_row};
use crate::glyph::{Glyph, Glyphs};
use crate::keys::Key;
use crate::text;
use crate::theme::{Token, bold, fg};

/// The navigator's width, its hairline included.
pub const WIDTH: u16 = 37;

/// Where a row points.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Hash)]
pub enum Node {
    /// A task, by position.
    Task(usize),
    /// A step of a task.
    Step(usize, usize),
    /// An action of a step.
    Action(usize, usize, usize),
}

impl Node {
    /// The task's position.
    pub fn task(self) -> usize {
        match self {
            Node::Task(t) | Node::Step(t, _) | Node::Action(t, _, _) => t,
        }
    }
}

/// What a mode says about a row: its status glyph and colour, and its tag and colour.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Mark {
    /// The status glyph.
    pub glyph: Glyph,
    /// Its colour.
    pub color: Token,
    /// The tag on the right (`auto`, `0.1s`, a hash).
    pub tag: String,
    /// Its colour.
    pub tag_color: Token,
}

impl Mark {
    /// A mark without a tag.
    pub fn plain(glyph: Glyph, color: Token) -> Mark {
        Mark {
            glyph,
            color,
            tag: String::new(),
            tag_color: Token::Fg3,
        }
    }
}

/// The navigator's state: the folded tasks and the selected row.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct Tree {
    /// Folded tasks, by position.
    pub folded: BTreeSet<usize>,
    /// The selected node.
    pub selected: Option<Node>,
}

impl Tree {
    /// The visible nodes of `plan`, in order. Notes are shown in their step's panel, not as
    /// rows, and a step of notes only has no row.
    pub fn rows(&self, plan: &Plan) -> Vec<Node> {
        let mut rows = Vec::new();
        for (t, task) in plan.tasks.iter().enumerate() {
            rows.push(Node::Task(t));
            if self.folded.contains(&t) {
                continue;
            }
            for (s, step) in task.steps.iter().enumerate() {
                let shown: Vec<usize> = (0..step.actions.len())
                    .filter(|&a| !matches!(step.actions[a].kind, ActionKind::Note { .. }))
                    .collect();
                if shown.is_empty() {
                    continue;
                }
                rows.push(Node::Step(t, s));
                rows.extend(shown.into_iter().map(|a| Node::Action(t, s, a)));
            }
        }
        rows
    }

    /// The selected node, or the first row.
    pub fn current(&self, plan: &Plan) -> Option<Node> {
        let rows = self.rows(plan);
        match self.selected {
            Some(n) if rows.contains(&n) => Some(n),
            Some(n) => rows
                .iter()
                .rev()
                .find(|r| **r <= n)
                .copied()
                .or(rows.first().copied()),
            None => rows.first().copied(),
        }
    }

    /// Selects `node`, unfolding its task.
    pub fn select(&mut self, node: Node) {
        self.folded.remove(&node.task());
        self.selected = Some(node);
    }

    /// Moves, folds and unfolds; false when the key is not the navigator's.
    pub fn key(&mut self, key: Key, plan: &Plan) -> bool {
        let rows = self.rows(plan);
        let Some(current) = self.current(plan) else {
            return false;
        };
        let at = rows.iter().position(|r| *r == current).unwrap_or(0);
        let last = rows.len().saturating_sub(1);
        let next = match key {
            Key::Up | Key::Char('k') => rows[at.saturating_sub(1)],
            Key::Down | Key::Char('j') => rows[(at + 1).min(last)],
            Key::PageUp => rows[at.saturating_sub(10)],
            Key::PageDown => rows[(at + 10).min(last)],
            Key::Home => rows[0],
            Key::End => rows[last],
            Key::Left | Key::Char('h') => {
                let t = current.task();
                if current == Node::Task(t) {
                    self.folded.insert(t);
                }
                Node::Task(t)
            }
            Key::Right | Key::Char('l') => {
                self.folded.remove(&current.task());
                current
            }
            Key::Enter => {
                if let Node::Task(t) = current
                    && !self.folded.remove(&t)
                {
                    self.folded.insert(t);
                }
                current
            }
            _ => return false,
        };
        self.selected = Some(next);
        true
    }

    /// Draws the navigator in `area`; `mark` gives each row's status and tag.
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub fn draw(
        &self,
        buf: &mut Buffer,
        area: Rect,
        plan: &Plan,
        title: &str,
        focused: bool,
        mark: &dyn Fn(Node) -> Mark,
        g: Glyphs,
    ) {
        let inner = Rect::new(area.x, area.y, area.width.saturating_sub(1), area.height);
        crate::draw::vrule(
            buf,
            area.right().saturating_sub(1),
            area.y,
            area.bottom(),
            g,
        );
        band(buf, inner, focused, "PLAN", title, g);
        let rows = self.rows(plan);
        let current = self.current(plan);
        let height = usize::from(area.height.saturating_sub(1));
        let at = current
            .and_then(|c| rows.iter().position(|r| *r == c))
            .unwrap_or(0);
        let top = (at + 1).saturating_sub(height);
        for (i, node) in rows.iter().enumerate().skip(top).take(height) {
            let y = area.y + 1 + u16::try_from(i - top).unwrap_or(0);
            let selected = Some(*node) == current;
            if selected {
                select_row(buf, inner, y, g);
            }
            draw_row(
                buf,
                inner,
                y,
                plan,
                *node,
                &mark(*node),
                self.folded.contains(&node.task()),
                selected,
                g,
            );
        }
    }
}

#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
fn draw_row(
    buf: &mut Buffer,
    area: Rect,
    y: u16,
    plan: &Plan,
    node: Node,
    mark: &Mark,
    folded: bool,
    selected: bool,
    g: Glyphs,
) {
    let bg = if selected { Token::Bg2 } else { Token::Bg0 };
    let on = |t: Token| -> Style { fg(t).bg(bg.color()) };
    let tag_w = u16::try_from(text::width(&mark.tag)).unwrap_or(0);
    let label_end = area.right().saturating_sub(tag_w + 2);
    let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x + 1, y, label_end, g);
    pen.glyph(mark.glyph, bold(mark.color).bg(bg.color()))
        .gap(1);
    match node {
        Node::Task(t) => {
            let task = &plan.tasks[t];
            let fold = if folded { Glyph::Closed } else { Glyph::Open };
            pen.glyph(fold, bold(Token::Fg3).bg(bg.color())).gap(1);
            let style = bold(Token::Fg).bg(bg.color());
            pen.text(
                &format!("{} {}", task.id.raw, plan.text(&task.title)),
                style,
            );
        }
        Node::Step(t, s) => {
            let step = &plan.tasks[t].steps[s];
            pen.gap(2);
            let number = step
                .number
                .map_or_else(|| g.get(Glyph::Dot).to_owned(), |n| n.to_string());
            let title = step.title.as_ref().map_or("", |t| plan.text(t));
            pen.text(&number, bold(Token::Fg3).bg(bg.color())).gap(1);
            pen.text(title, on(Token::Fg2));
        }
        Node::Action(t, s, a) => {
            let action = &plan.tasks[t].steps[s].actions[a];
            pen.gap(4);
            pen.glyph(kind_glyph(action), bold(kind_color(action)).bg(bg.color()))
                .gap(1);
            pen.text(&label(plan, action), on(Token::Fg2));
        }
    }
    let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, label_end, y, area.right().saturating_sub(1), g);
    pen.right(&mark.tag, on(mark.tag_color));
}

/// The kind glyph of an action.
pub fn kind_glyph(action: &Action) -> Glyph {
    match &action.kind {
        ActionKind::WriteFile { .. } => Glyph::Write,
        ActionKind::ApplyPatch { .. } => Glyph::Patch,
        ActionKind::ReplaceExact { .. } => Glyph::Replace,
        ActionKind::Delete { .. } => Glyph::Delete,
        ActionKind::Generated { .. } => Glyph::Generated,
        ActionKind::RunScript(_) => Glyph::Script,
        ActionKind::Commit(_) => Glyph::Commit,
        ActionKind::Note { .. } => Glyph::Note,
        ActionKind::Manual { .. } => Glyph::Manual,
    }
}

/// The kind glyph's colour: fg2, amber for a Manual.
pub fn kind_color(action: &Action) -> Token {
    match action.kind {
        ActionKind::Manual { .. } => Token::Warn,
        _ => Token::Fg2,
    }
}

/// An action's label: its path, a script's first line, `commit`, a Manual's or Note's first
/// words.
pub fn label(plan: &Plan, action: &Action) -> String {
    match &action.kind {
        ActionKind::WriteFile { path, .. }
        | ActionKind::ApplyPatch { path, .. }
        | ActionKind::ReplaceExact { path, .. }
        | ActionKind::Delete { path, .. }
        | ActionKind::Generated { path } => path.to_string(),
        ActionKind::RunScript(s) => first_line(plan.text(&s.body)),
        ActionKind::Commit(_) => "commit".to_owned(),
        ActionKind::Note { text } | ActionKind::Manual { text, .. } => first_line(plan.text(text)),
    }
}

/// The first non-blank line of `s`, trimmed.
pub fn first_line(s: &str) -> String {
    s.lines()
        .map(str::trim)
        .find(|l| !l.is_empty())
        .unwrap_or("")
        .to_owned()
}
```

File `crates/planrun-tui/tests/inspect.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! The plan view in Inspect (docs/tui-design.md §6.B, D84) on the synthetic assay plan.

mod support;

use std::time::Duration;

use planrun_tui::jobs::Bg;
use planrun_tui::keys::Key;
use planrun_tui::tree::Node;
use support::{screen, setup};

/// A core with `assay-style.md` open in Inspect and its dry run done.
fn inspected(setup: &support::Setup) -> planrun_tui::driver::Core<ratatui::backend::TestBackend> {
    let mut core = setup.core();
    core.key(Key::Enter);
    let done = core.wait(Duration::from_secs(60), |app| {
        app.inspect()
            .is_some_and(|i| matches!(i.dry, Bg::Current(_)))
    });
    assert!(done, "the dry run did not finish");
    core
}

#[test]
fn inspect_shows_the_selected_patch_its_verdict_and_the_dry_run() {
    let setup = setup(&["assay-style.md"]);
    let mut core = inspected(&setup);
    for _ in 0..3 {
        core.key(Key::Down);
    }
    let node = core.app().inspect().and_then(|i| i.tree.current(i.plan()));
    assert_eq!(node, Some(Node::Action(0, 1, 1)));
    insta::assert_snapshot!(screen(&mut core));
}

#[test]
fn the_assumed_policy_changes_a_script_s_tag() {
    let setup = setup(&["assay-style.md"]);
    let mut core = inspected(&setup);
    let tag = |core: &planrun_tui::driver::Core<_>| {
        let inspect = core.app().inspect().unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("no inspect"));
        inspect.mark(Node::Action(0, 2, 1)).tag
    };
    assert_eq!(tag(&core), "ask");
    for key in [Key::Char('a'), Key::Char('b'), Key::Enter] {
        core.key(key);
    }
    assert_eq!(tag(&core), "auto");
    let described = core.app().inspect().map(|i| i.assumed.describe());
    assert_eq!(described.as_deref(), Some("auto build"));
}

#[test]
fn esc_goes_back_to_plans_and_f3_returns() {
    let setup = setup(&["assay-style.md"]);
    let mut core = inspected(&setup);
    core.key(Key::Esc);
    assert!(screen(&mut core).contains("PLANS"));
    core.key(Key::F(3));
    assert!(screen(&mut core).contains("INSPECT"));
}
```

### P6c-T4: Run mode, the worker and the dock

**Goal:** `R` runs the plan: the runner on its worker thread with a `Ui` that forwards to the render thread, `ask` a blocking round trip; a journal observer forwards every record, and the plan view in Run is filled from them (states, durations, outcome tags, change-state chips), CHANGES from `git diff HEAD` of the task's paths, the console from `output` and `status`; a question opens the dock over the console, with one layout per `QuestionKind` (D85, D86, D90).

**Files**

- Modify `crates/planrun-tui/src/app.rs`
- Create `crates/planrun-tui/src/dock.rs`
- Modify `crates/planrun-tui/src/driver.rs`
- Modify `crates/planrun-tui/src/jobs.rs`
- Modify `crates/planrun-tui/src/lib.rs`
- Create `crates/planrun-tui/src/run.rs`
- Create `crates/planrun-tui/src/worker.rs`
- Create `crates/planrun-tui/tests/run.rs`

Over 300 lines, so they come as exact diffs: `crates/planrun-tui/src/app.rs`, `crates/planrun-tui/src/jobs.rs`.

**Step 1 — write the files**
