```rust
//! The decision dock (docs/tui-design.md §6.D, decisions D85, D86): a question opens over the
//! console's region and leaves the navigator and CHANGES visible. One layout per
//! [`QuestionKind`], matched exhaustively, so the compiler finds a question the TUI does not
//! show. The answers are `Question.choices` and `Question.typed`, every one the runner offers and
//! no other; a typed question takes the whole word and enter, never a single key (D74).

use std::sync::mpsc::Sender;

use ratatui::buffer::Buffer;
use ratatui::layout::Rect;
use ratatui::style::Style;

use planrun_exec::done::Seen;
use planrun_exec::expect::Mismatch;
use planrun_exec::journal::At;
use planrun_exec::shell::RunStatus;
use planrun_exec::ui::{Choice, Question, QuestionKind, Reply};
use planrun_model::{Action, ActionKind, Plan, Shell};

use crate::draw::{Pen, fill};
use crate::glyph::{Glyph, Glyphs};
use crate::inspect::class_name;
use crate::keys::Key;
use crate::text;
use crate::theme::{Token, bold, fg, on};

/// One line of the dock's body: styled pieces.
type Spans = Vec<(String, Style)>;

/// An open question.
pub struct Dock {
    /// The question.
    pub question: Question,
    reply: Option<Sender<Reply>>,
    /// The typed answer so far.
    pub input: String,
    /// A typed word that is not one of the answers was entered.
    pub refused: Option<String>,
    /// The first body line shown.
    pub top: usize,
}

impl Dock {
    /// The dock for `question`, answered on `reply`.
    pub fn new(question: Question, reply: Sender<Reply>) -> Dock {
        Dock {
            question,
            reply: Some(reply),
            input: String::new(),
            refused: None,
            top: 0,
        }
    }

    /// Sends `reply` to the runner; the dock is done.
    pub fn answer(&mut self, reply: Reply) {
        if let Some(tx) = self.reply.take() {
            let _ = tx.send(reply);
        }
    }

    /// Whether it was answered.
    pub fn answered(&self) -> bool {
        self.reply.is_none()
    }

    /// A key: the reply when it answers the question. A typed question gets the word on enter;
    /// a word it does not accept is shown as refused and nothing is sent (D74: it would ask
    /// again).
    pub fn key(&mut self, key: Key) -> Option<Reply> {
        if let Some(words) = self.question.typed {
            match key {
                Key::Char(c) => {
                    self.input.push(c);
                    self.refused = None;
                }
                Key::Backspace => {
                    self.input.pop();
                }
                Key::Enter => {
                    let word = std::mem::take(&mut self.input);
                    if words.contains(&word.as_str()) {
                        return Some(Reply::Typed(word));
                    }
                    self.refused = Some(word);
                }
                Key::Down => self.top += 1,
                Key::Up => self.top = self.top.saturating_sub(1),
                _ => {}
            }
            return None;
        }
        let pressed = match key {
            Key::Enter => "",
            Key::Char(c) => return self.choice(&c.to_string()),
            Key::Down => {
                self.top += 1;
                return None;
            }
            Key::Up => {
                self.top = self.top.saturating_sub(1);
                return None;
            }
            _ => return None,
        };
        self.choice(pressed)
    }

    fn choice(&self, key: &str) -> Option<Reply> {
        self.question
            .choices
            .iter()
            .find(|c| c.key() == key)
            .map(|c| Reply::Choice(*c))
    }

    /// The keys the footer shows: the answers, then `tab`.
    pub fn footer(&self) -> Vec<(String, String)> {
        let mut out: Vec<(String, String)> = match self.question.typed {
            Some(words) => words
                .iter()
                .map(|w| {
                    (
                        format!("{w}{}", Glyphs::unicode().get(Glyph::Enter)),
                        word_meaning(&self.question.kind, w).to_owned(),
                    )
                })
                .collect(),
            None => self
                .question
                .choices
                .iter()
                .map(|c| (key_name(*c).to_owned(), c.label().to_owned()))
                .collect(),
        };
        out.push(("tab".into(), "look around".into()));
        out
    }

    /// The band's title: glyph, words, colour.
    fn title(&self) -> (Glyph, &'static str, &'static str) {
        match &self.question.kind {
            QuestionKind::Run { .. } => (Glyph::Waiting, "DECISION", "script"),
            QuestionKind::TypedYes { .. } => (
                Glyph::Waiting,
                "DECISION",
                "Destructive script · typed answer",
            ),
            QuestionKind::Mismatch { dead: true, .. } => (
                Glyph::Warning,
                "SESSION ENDED",
                "the session is gone; no accept",
            ),
            QuestionKind::Mismatch { .. } => {
                (Glyph::Warning, "MISMATCH", "expected output did not match")
            }
            QuestionKind::Confirm { .. } => (
                Glyph::Waiting,
                "CONFIRM",
                "the plan says it in words; you judge",
            ),
            QuestionKind::Session { .. } => (
                Glyph::Waiting,
                "NEW SESSION",
                "acknowledged even with --auto",
            ),
            QuestionKind::Manual => (Glyph::Manual, "MANUAL", "the plan asks you to do this"),
            QuestionKind::Unscoped => (
                Glyph::Waiting,
                "OUTSIDE SCOPE",
                "a file action outside the task's files",
            ),
            QuestionKind::Dirt { .. } => (
                Glyph::Waiting,
                "OTHER CHANGES",
                "outside the task's paths, before the commit",
            ),
            QuestionKind::Gate { .. } => (Glyph::Waiting, "COMMIT GATE", "closed · typed answer"),
            QuestionKind::Outside { .. } => (
                Glyph::Waiting,
                "WORKING DIRECTORY",
                "outside the repository",
            ),
            QuestionKind::Changed { .. } => (
                Glyph::Waiting,
                "CHANGED SINCE PREPARE",
                "something else writes the tree",
            ),
            QuestionKind::Again { .. } => (
                Glyph::Waiting,
                "RAN BEFORE THE INTERRUPTION",
                "run again or keep",
            ),
            QuestionKind::HeadMoved { .. } => {
                (Glyph::Warning, "HEAD MOVED", "while an action was running")
            }
            QuestionKind::Unknown { .. } => (
                Glyph::Waiting,
                "UNKNOWN STEP STATE",
                "neither as before nor as planned",
            ),
            QuestionKind::AcceptDone { .. } => (
                Glyph::Waiting,
                "ACCEPT AS DONE",
                "overrides the disk check · typed answer",
            ),
            QuestionKind::ResumeDirt { .. } => (
                Glyph::Waiting,
                "YOUR CHANGES",
                "a remaining file action writes over them",
            ),
        }
    }

    /// The body: what the run found (the payload), then the plan's text where it matters, then
    /// the runner's own words.
    fn body(&self, plan: &Plan, g: Glyphs) -> Vec<Spans> {
        let q = &self.question;
        let action = q.at.and_then(|at| {
            plan.tasks
                .get(at.task)?
                .steps
                .get(at.step)?
                .actions
                .get(at.action)
        });
        let mut out: Vec<Spans> = Vec::new();
        let row = |label: &str, text: String, color: Token| -> Spans {
            vec![
                (text::pad(label, 9, "…"), fg(Token::Fg3)),
                (text, fg(color)),
            ]
        };
        match &q.kind {
            QuestionKind::Run { decision } => {
                let why = match decision {
                    planrun_exec::policy::Decision::Ask(c) => {
                        format!("{} is not in --auto", class_name(*c))
                    }
                    other => format!("{other:?}"),
                };
                out.push(row("why", why, Token::Fg2));
                out.push(Vec::new());
                out.extend(block(plan, action));
            }
            QuestionKind::TypedYes { reasons } => {
                out.push(row(
                    "why",
                    format!("Destructive: {}", reasons.join("; ")),
                    Token::Err,
                ));
                out.push(Vec::new());
                out.extend(block(plan, action));
                out.push(Vec::new());
                let shell = action.and_then(script_shell).map_or("pwsh", shell_name);
                out.push(row(
                    "if yes",
                    format!("runs in the {shell} session; journaled as your approval"),
                    Token::Fg2,
                ));
                out.push(row(
                    "if skip",
                    "the block is journaled as skipped by you".to_owned(),
                    Token::Fg2,
                ));
                out.push(row(
                    "if quit",
                    "the run stops before this block; resume asks again".to_owned(),
                    Token::Fg2,
                ));
            }
            QuestionKind::Mismatch {
                status,
                mismatches,
                output,
                aligned,
                commit,
                dead,
            } => {
                out.push(row(
                    "ended",
                    status_text(status),
                    if *dead { Token::Err } else { Token::Fg2 },
                ));
                for m in mismatches {
                    if let Mismatch::Exit { expected, got } = m {
                        out.push(row(
                            "exit",
                            format!("expected {expected:?}, got {}", status_text(got)),
                            Token::Err,
                        ));
                    }
                }
                if !aligned.is_empty() {
                    out.push(Vec::new());
                    out.push(vec![
                        (
                            text::pad("EXPECTED lines, in order", 42, "…"),
                            bold(Token::Fg3),
                        ),
                        ("GOT".into(), bold(Token::Fg3)),
                    ]);
                    let expected = action
                        .and_then(|a| expected_lines(plan, a))
                        .unwrap_or_default();
                    for al in aligned {
                        let want = expected.get(al.number - 1).cloned().unwrap_or_default();
                        let (glyph, color) = if al.output.is_some() {
                            (Glyph::Done, Token::Ok)
                        } else {
                            (Glyph::Failed, Token::Err)
                        };
                        let got = al
                            .output
                            .and_then(|i| output.get(i))
                            .map_or(String::new(), |l| l.text.trim_end_matches('\r').to_owned());
                        let mut line: Spans = vec![
                            (format!("{} ", g.get(glyph)), bold(color)),
                            (text::pad(&want, 40, g.get(Glyph::Ellipsis)), fg(Token::Fg)),
                        ];
                        match al.output {
                            Some(i) => {
                                line.push((
                                    text::pad(&got, 28, g.get(Glyph::Ellipsis)),
                                    fg(Token::Fg2),
                                ));
                                line.push((
                                    format!("{} {}", g.get(Glyph::Aligned), i + 1),
                                    fg(Token::Fg3),
                                ));
                            }
                            None => line.push((
                                format!("{} not found", g.get(Glyph::Left)),
                                fg(Token::Err),
                            )),
                        }
                        out.push(line);
                    }
                }
                if let Some(problem) = commit {
                    out.push(row("commit", problem.clone(), Token::Err));
                }
                if *dead {
                    out.push(row("dead", "accepting would send the commit into a session without its variables (D75)".to_owned(), Token::Fg2));
                }
            }
            QuestionKind::Confirm { reasons, errored } => {
                let words: Vec<String> = reasons
                    .iter()
                    .map(|r| format!("{r:?}").to_lowercase())
                    .collect();
                out.push(row(
                    "checked",
                    "the exit status and any summary lines held".to_owned(),
                    Token::Ok,
                ));
                out.push(row("asks", words.join(", "), Token::Fg2));
                if *errored {
                    out.push(row(
                        "errored",
                        "pwsh recorded an error".to_owned(),
                        Token::Warn,
                    ));
                }
                if let Some(prose) = action.and_then(|a| expectation_prose(plan, a)) {
                    out.push(Vec::new());
                    out.push(vec![("the plan says".into(), bold(Token::Fg3))]);
                    out.extend(quote(&prose));
                }
            }
            QuestionKind::Session { why, earlier } => {
                out.push(row("why", why.clone(), Token::Fg2));
                out.push(row(
                    "gone",
                    "variables, functions and the directory of earlier blocks".to_owned(),
                    Token::Fg2,
                ));
                if !earlier.is_empty() {
                    out.push(row("earlier", earlier.join(", "), Token::Meta));
                }
            }
            QuestionKind::Manual => {
                let text = action.map(|a| match &a.kind {
                    ActionKind::Manual { text, .. } => plan.text(text).to_owned(),
                    _ => String::new(),
                });
                out.extend(quote(&text.unwrap_or_default()));
            }
            QuestionKind::Unscoped => {
                if let Some(a) = action
                    && let Some(path) = planrun_exec::handler::file_path(&a.kind)
                {
                    out.push(row("path", path.to_string(), Token::Fg));
                    out.push(row(
                        "origin",
                        format!("{:?}", a.path_origin).to_lowercase(),
                        Token::Fg2,
                    ));
                    let line = plan.source.line_col(a.span.start).line;
                    out.push(row("plan", format!("line {line}"), Token::Meta));
                }
            }
            QuestionKind::Dirt { paths } => {
                for p in paths {
                    out.push(vec![
                        (format!("{} ", p.code), fg(Token::Warn)),
                        (p.path.clone(), fg(Token::Fg)),
                    ]);
                }
            }
            QuestionKind::Outside { shell, cwd } => {
                out.push(row("shell", shell_name(*shell).to_owned(), Token::Fg2));
                out.push(row("cwd", cwd.display().to_string(), Token::Warn));
            }
            QuestionKind::Gate { reasons } => {
                for r in reasons {
                    out.push(vec![
                        (format!("{} ", g.get(Glyph::Failed)), bold(Token::Err)),
                        (r.clone(), fg(Token::Fg)),
                    ]);
                }
            }
            QuestionKind::Changed { paths } | QuestionKind::ResumeDirt { paths } => {
                for p in paths {
                    out.push(vec![
                        (format!("{} ", g.get(Glyph::Warning)), bold(Token::Warn)),
                        (p.to_string(), fg(Token::Fg)),
                    ]);
                }
            }
            QuestionKind::Again { running } => {
                let what = if *running {
                    "was running when planrun was interrupted"
                } else {
                    "ran before the interruption"
                };
                out.push(row("it", what.to_owned(), Token::Fg2));
            }
            QuestionKind::Unknown {
                step,
                paths,
                restorable,
            } => {
                out.push(row("step", place(step), Token::Meta));
                for (path, seen) in paths {
                    let (word, color) = match seen {
                        Seen::Post => ("as planned", Token::Ok),
                        Seen::Pre => ("as before", Token::Fg2),
                        Seen::Neither => ("neither", Token::Warn),
                    };
                    out.push(vec![
                        (text::pad(&path.to_string(), 40, "…"), fg(Token::Fg)),
                        (word.into(), fg(color)),
                    ]);
                }
                let r = if *restorable {
                    "every pre-image is there: restore is offered"
                } else {
                    "a pre-image is gone: no restore (D57)"
                };
                out.push(row("restore", r.to_owned(), Token::Fg2));
            }
            QuestionKind::AcceptDone { step } => {
                out.push(row("step", place(step), Token::Meta));
                out.push(row(
                    "means",
                    "its paths count as written, without the disk check".to_owned(),
                    Token::Fg2,
                ));
            }
            QuestionKind::HeadMoved {
                action,
                recorded,
                now,
            } => {
                out.push(row("action", place(action), Token::Meta));
                out.push(row("recorded", head_text(recorded), Token::Meta));
                out.push(row("now", head_text(now), Token::Meta));
            }
        }
        if !q.text.is_empty() {
            out.push(Vec::new());
            for line in &q.text {
                out.push(vec![(line.clone(), fg(Token::Fg2))]);
            }
        }
        out
    }

    /// Draws the dock over `area`.
    pub fn draw(&self, buf: &mut Buffer, area: Rect, plan: &Plan, focused: bool, g: Glyphs) {
        fill(
            buf,
            Rect::new(area.x, area.y, area.width, 1),
            on(Token::Fg, Token::Bg1),
        );
        let (glyph, title, detail) = self.title();
        let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x, area.y, area.right(), g);
        pen.glyph(Glyph::Band, on(Token::Warn, Token::Bg1)).gap(1);
        let title_style = if focused {
            bold(Token::Warn)
        } else {
            fg(Token::Warn)
        };
        pen.glyph(glyph, title_style.bg(Token::Bg1.color())).gap(1);
        pen.text(title, title_style.bg(Token::Bg1.color())).gap(2);
        pen.text(detail, on(Token::Fg2, Token::Bg1));
        if let Some(at) = self.question.at
            && let Some(task) = plan.tasks.get(at.task)
        {
            let step = task
                .steps
                .get(at.step)
                .and_then(|s| s.number)
                .map_or_else(String::new, |n| format!(" {} step {n}", g.get(Glyph::Dot)));
            Pen::new(buf, area.x, area.y, area.right().saturating_sub(1), g).right(
                &format!("{}{step}", task.id.raw),
                on(Token::Fg3, Token::Bg1),
            );
        }
        let answers_h = 3;
        let body_area = Rect::new(
            area.x + 2,
            area.y + 2,
            area.width.saturating_sub(3),
            area.height.saturating_sub(2 + answers_h),
        );
        let body = self.body(plan, g);
        for (i, line) in body
            .iter()
            .skip(self.top)
            .take(usize::from(body_area.height))
            .enumerate()
        {
            let mut pen = Pen::new(
                buf,
                body_area.x,
                body_area.y + u16::try_from(i).unwrap_or(0),
                body_area.right(),
                g,
            );
            for (s, style) in line {
                pen.text(s, *style);
            }
        }
        let y = area.bottom().saturating_sub(answers_h);
        self.draw_answers(
            buf,
            Rect::new(area.x + 2, y, area.width.saturating_sub(3), answers_h),
            g,
        );
    }

    fn draw_answers(&self, buf: &mut Buffer, area: Rect, g: Glyphs) {
        let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x, area.y, area.right(), g);
        match self.question.typed {
            Some(words) => {
                pen.text("answer  ", fg(Token::Fg3));
                let field = format!("{}{}", self.input, g.get(Glyph::Caret));
                pen.cell(&field, 12, bold(Token::Fg).bg(Token::Bg2.color()))
                    .gap(2);
                for w in words {
                    pen.text(w, bold(Token::Fg)).gap(1);
                    pen.text(word_meaning(&self.question.kind, w), fg(Token::Fg2))
                        .gap(3);
                }
                let note = match &self.refused {
                    Some(w) => (
                        format!("`{w}` is not one of the answers: type the whole word, then enter"),
                        fg(Token::Warn),
                    ),
                    None => (
                        "typed: the whole word, then enter; a single key never answers".to_owned(),
                        fg(Token::Fg3),
                    ),
                };
                Pen::new(buf, area.x, area.y + 1, area.right(), g).text(&note.0, note.1);
            }
            None => {
                for (i, c) in self.question.choices.iter().enumerate() {
                    if i > 0 {
                        pen.gap(3);
                    }
                    pen.chip(key_name(*c), c.label(), Token::Bg0);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

/// The key a choice is pressed with, as the dock names it.
pub fn key_name(c: Choice) -> &'static str {
    match c.key() {
        "" => "enter",
        k => k,
    }
}

/// What a typed word does, for the answer row.
fn word_meaning(kind: &QuestionKind, word: &str) -> &'static str {
    match (kind, word) {
        (QuestionKind::Gate { .. }, "yes") => "open the gate",
        (QuestionKind::AcceptDone { .. }, "yes") => "accept as done",
        (_, "yes") => "run it",
        (_, "skip") => "skip this script",
        (_, "quit") => "stop the run",
        _ => "",
    }
}

/// The action's script block, numbered.
fn block(plan: &Plan, action: Option<&Action>) -> Vec<Spans> {
    let Some(script) = action.and_then(|a| match &a.kind {
        ActionKind::RunScript(s) | ActionKind::Commit(s) => Some(s),
        _ => None,
    }) else {
        return Vec::new();
    };
    plan.text(&script.body)
        .lines()
        .enumerate()
        .map(|(i, l)| {
            vec![
                (format!("{:>3}  ", i + 1), fg(Token::Fg3)),
                (l.to_owned(), fg(Token::Fg)),
            ]
        })
        .collect()
}

fn quote(text: &str) -> Vec<Spans> {
    text.lines()
        .map(|l| {
            vec![
                ("│ ".to_owned(), fg(Token::Warn)),
                (l.to_owned(), fg(Token::Fg)),
            ]
        })
        .collect()
}

fn script_shell(action: &Action) -> Option<Shell> {
    match &action.kind {
        ActionKind::RunScript(s) | ActionKind::Commit(s) => Some(s.shell),
        _ => None,
    }
}

fn shell_name(shell: Shell) -> &'static str {
    match shell {
        Shell::Pwsh => "pwsh",
        Shell::Bash => "bash",
    }
}

fn expected_lines(plan: &Plan, action: &Action) -> Option<Vec<String>> {
    let script = match &action.kind {
        ActionKind::RunScript(s) | ActionKind::Commit(s) => s,
        _ => return None,
    };
    let lines = script.expect.as_ref()?.lines.as_ref()?;
    Some(plan.text(lines).lines().map(str::to_owned).collect())
}

fn expectation_prose(plan: &Plan, action: &Action) -> Option<String> {
    let script = match &action.kind {
        ActionKind::RunScript(s) | ActionKind::Commit(s) => s,
        _ => return None,
    };
    let prose = script.expect.as_ref()?.prose.as_ref()?;
    Some(plan.text(prose).to_owned())
}

fn status_text(status: &RunStatus) -> String {
    match status {
        RunStatus::Exited(code) => format!("exit {code}"),
        RunStatus::Threw => "a terminating error".to_owned(),
        RunStatus::SessionEnded(Some(code)) => format!("the session ended (exit {code})"),
        RunStatus::SessionEnded(None) => "the session ended".to_owned(),
        RunStatus::TimedOut => "timed out".to_owned(),
        RunStatus::Broken(why) => format!("the session broke: {why}"),
    }
}

fn place(at: &At) -> String {
    format!("{} step {} action {}", at.task, at.step + 1, at.action + 1)
}

fn head_text(head: &planrun_exec::git::Head) -> String {
    let commit = head
        .commit
        .as_deref()
        .map_or("(none)", |c| &c[..c.len().min(7)]);
    match &head.reference {
        Some(r) => format!("{commit} on {r}"),
        None => commit.to_owned(),
    }
}
```

File `crates/planrun-tui/src/driver.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! The loop's core, shared by the terminal and the tests (decision D93): it feeds the app keys,
//! ticks and messages, carries out the effects the app asks for, and draws. The terminal adds
//! crossterm's events; the tests a `TestBackend` and scripted keys.

use std::panic::{AssertUnwindSafe, catch_unwind};
use std::sync::mpsc::{Receiver, Sender, channel};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use ratatui::Terminal;
use ratatui::backend::Backend;

use crate::app::{App, Effect, Msg};
use crate::jobs::panic_message;
use crate::keys::Key;

/// The app, its terminal, and the channel its threads answer on.
pub struct Core<B: Backend> {
    app: App,
    terminal: Terminal<B>,
    tx: Sender<Msg>,
    rx: Receiver<Msg>,
}

impl<B: Backend> Core<B> {
    /// The core over `terminal`.
    pub fn new(app: App, terminal: Terminal<B>) -> Core<B> {
        let (tx, rx) = channel();
        Core {
            app,
            terminal,
            tx,
            rx,
        }
    }

    /// The app.
    pub fn app(&self) -> &App {
        &self.app
    }

    /// The app, to change it (the tests add a crash observer, D93).
    pub fn app_mut(&mut self) -> &mut App {
        &mut self.app
    }

    /// The terminal.
    pub fn terminal(&self) -> &Terminal<B> {
        &self.terminal
    }

    /// Whether the app is done.
    pub fn done(&self) -> bool {
        self.app.done()
    }

    /// Starts the app's first jobs.
    pub fn start(&mut self) {
        let effects = self.app.start();
        self.apply(effects);
    }

    /// Feeds a key.
    pub fn key(&mut self, key: Key) {
        let effects = self.app.key(key);
        self.apply(effects);
    }

    /// Lets time pass.
    pub fn tick(&mut self) {
        let effects = self.app.tick();
        self.apply(effects);
    }

    /// Hands the app every message that has arrived; true when there was one.
    pub fn pump(&mut self) -> bool {
        let mut any = false;
        while let Ok(msg) = self.rx.try_recv() {
            any = true;
            let effects = self.app.msg(msg);
            self.apply(effects);
        }
        any
    }

    /// Waits up to `timeout` for `until` to hold, handing the app messages as they arrive.
    pub fn wait(&mut self, timeout: Duration, until: impl Fn(&App) -> bool) -> bool {
        let end = Instant::now() + timeout;
        loop {
            self.pump();
            if until(&self.app) {
                return true;
            }
            let left = end.saturating_duration_since(Instant::now());
            if left.is_zero() {
                return false;
            }
            if let Ok(msg) = self.rx.recv_timeout(left.min(Duration::from_millis(20))) {
                let effects = self.app.msg(msg);
                self.apply(effects);
            }
        }
    }

    /// Draws the app.
    pub fn draw(&mut self) -> Result<(), B::Error> {
        let app = &self.app;
        self.terminal.draw(|frame| app.draw(frame.buffer_mut()))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Forces a full redraw after the screen was left.
    pub fn redraw(&mut self) -> Result<(), B::Error> {
        self.terminal.clear()?;
        self.draw()
    }

    fn apply(&mut self, effects: Vec<Effect>) {
        for effect in effects {
            match effect {
                Effect::Job(job) => {
                    let tx = self.tx.clone();
                    let ctx = self.app.context();
                    let failed = job.on_failure();
                    std::thread::spawn(move || {
                        let done = match catch_unwind(AssertUnwindSafe(|| job.run(&ctx))) {
                            Ok(done) => done,
                            Err(payload) => failed(format!(
                                "the job panicked: {}",
                                panic_message(payload.as_ref())
                            )),
                        };
                        let _ = tx.send(Msg::Done(done));
                    });
                }
                Effect::Run(request) => {
                    crate::worker::start(request, self.app.context(), self.tx.clone());
                }
                Effect::Quit => self.app.quit = true,
            }
        }
```
```diff
    }
}

+/// `git --no-optional-locks diff HEAD -- paths`, read-only; empty when git fails.
+fn changes(ctx: &Context, paths: &[String]) -> Vec<crate::diff::FileDiff> {
+    if paths.is_empty() {
+        return Vec::new();
+    }
+    let out = std::process::Command::new("git")
+        .args(["--no-optional-locks", "diff", "--no-color", "HEAD", "--"])
+        .args(paths)
+        .current_dir(&ctx.root)
+        .envs(ctx.env.iter().map(|(k, v)| (k, v)))
+        .output();
+    match out {
+        Ok(out) if out.status.success() => {
+            crate::diff::parse(&String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout))
+        }
+        _ => Vec::new(),
+    }
+}
```

```rust
//! The planrun TUI (docs/tui-design.md; decisions D84–D94): a second [`Ui`](planrun_exec::Ui)
//! over the same crates as the line prompts, composed by planrun-cli as `planrun tui`. It shows
//! the domain model and adds no model of its own: resume, policy and preflight stay
//! authoritative; the TUI explains and asks, and never decides.

#![forbid(unsafe_code)]

pub mod app;
pub mod chrome;
pub mod diff;
pub mod dock;
pub mod draw;
pub mod driver;
```

File `crates/planrun-tui/src/run.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! The plan view in Run mode (docs/tui-design.md §6.C, decisions D84, D90): the same layout as
//! Inspect, filled from the live runner. The navigator's states, durations, outcome tags and
//! change-state chips come from the journal's records; the console from `Ui::output` and
//! `Ui::status`, shown as prose and never parsed. A question opens the dock over the console.

use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::sync::mpsc::Sender;

use ratatui::buffer::Buffer;
use ratatui::layout::Rect;
use ratatui::style::Style;

use planrun_exec::journal::{At, Outcome, Record};
use planrun_exec::runner::Finished;
use planrun_exec::shell::{OutLine, Stream};
use planrun_exec::ui::{Question, Reply};
use planrun_model::{ActionKind, ManifestPath, Plan};
use planrun_parse::Parsed;

use crate::diff::{self, FileDiff};
use crate::dock::Dock;
use crate::draw::{Pen, band, hrule};
use crate::glyph::{Glyph, Glyphs};
use crate::inspect::{Assumed, tag};
use crate::keys::Key;
use crate::text;
use crate::theme::{Token, bold, fg, on};
use crate::tree::{self, Mark, Node, Tree};

/// A console line's label (§6.B/C).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Label {
    /// A task starts.
    Task,
    /// Preflight.
    Pre,
    /// A step's files.
    File,
    /// A script starts.
    Run,
    /// Output on stdout.
    Out,
    /// Output on stderr.
    Err,
    /// A script's verdict.
    Verify,
    /// A commit.
    Commit,
    /// Status prose, answers.
    Event,
}

impl Label {
    fn word(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Label::Task => "TASK",
            Label::Pre => "PRE",
            Label::File => "FILE",
            Label::Run => "RUN",
            Label::Out => "out",
            Label::Err => "err",
            Label::Verify => "VERIFY",
            Label::Commit => "COMMIT",
            Label::Event => "EVENT",
        }
    }

    fn style(self) -> Style {
        match self {
            Label::Task => bold(Token::Fg),
            Label::Run => bold(Token::Act),
            Label::Out => fg(Token::Fg3),
            Label::Err => fg(Token::Warn),
            Label::Verify | Label::Commit => bold(Token::Fg2),
            Label::Pre | Label::File | Label::Event => fg(Token::Fg2),
        }
    }

    /// Whether filter `n` (1 all, 2 events, 3 output, 4 verify, 5 warnings) shows it.
    fn shown(self, filter: u8, warning: bool) -> bool {
        match filter {
            2 => !matches!(self, Label::Out | Label::Err),
            3 => matches!(self, Label::Out | Label::Err),
            4 => matches!(self, Label::Verify | Label::Commit),
            5 => self == Label::Err || warning,
            _ => true,
        }
    }
}

/// One console line.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Line {
    /// Milliseconds since the run started.
    pub at: u64,
    /// Its label.
    pub label: Label,
    /// Its text.
    pub text: String,
    /// A warning (a mismatch, a failure).
    pub warning: bool,
}

/// How far the run is.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Phase {
    /// Taking the lock, opening the journal.
    Starting,
    /// Running.
    Executing,
    /// It ended.
    Ended(Result<Finished, String>),
}

/// One action's journaled state.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Act {
    /// `action-start` without its end.
    Running(u64),
    /// `action-end`, with its duration and status.
    Ended {
        /// How.
        outcome: Outcome,
        /// Milliseconds it took.
        took: u64,
    },
}

/// The live run.
pub struct Run {
    /// The plan file.
    pub path: PathBuf,
    /// The plan.
    pub parsed: Arc<Parsed>,
    /// The policy it runs with.
    pub assumed: Assumed,
    /// A resume.
    pub resume: bool,
    /// When it started.
    pub started: u64,
    /// How far it is.
    pub phase: Phase,
    /// Tasks started, by position.
    pub tasks_started: BTreeSet<usize>,
    /// Tasks ended, by position, with their HEAD.
    pub tasks_done: BTreeMap<usize, Option<String>>,
    /// The current task.
    pub current: Option<usize>,
    /// Actions by (task, step, action).
    pub acts: BTreeMap<(usize, usize, usize), Act>,
    /// Written file groups (their first action).
    pub written: BTreeSet<(usize, usize, usize)>,
    /// The console.
    pub console: Vec<Line>,
    /// The console filter, 1–5.
    pub filter: u8,
    /// Following the console's end.
    pub follow: bool,
    /// The console's first line when not following.
    pub scroll: usize,
    /// The navigator.
    pub tree: Tree,
    /// The open question.
    pub dock: Option<Dock>,
    /// The CHANGES files of the current task, and the one shown.
    pub changes: Vec<FileDiff>,
    /// The file shown in CHANGES.
    pub file: usize,
    /// The diff `git diff HEAD` gave for the current task's paths.
    pub on_disk: Vec<FileDiff>,
    /// Focus: 0 the navigator, 1 CHANGES, 2 the console or the dock.
    pub focus: u8,
    /// The action shown in ACTION, for `[v]`.
    pub viewing: Option<(usize, usize, usize)>,
}

/// What the app should do after a run message.
pub enum Follow {
    /// Nothing.
    None,
    /// Read the current task's diff again.
    Changes(usize, Vec<String>),
}

impl Run {
    /// A run of `parsed` with `assumed`, starting at `now`.
    pub fn new(
        path: PathBuf,
        parsed: Arc<Parsed>,
        assumed: Assumed,
        resume: bool,
        now: u64,
    ) -> Run {
        Run {
            path,
            parsed,
            assumed,
            resume,
            started: now,
            phase: Phase::Starting,
            tasks_started: BTreeSet::new(),
            tasks_done: BTreeMap::new(),
            current: None,
            acts: BTreeMap::new(),
            written: BTreeSet::new(),
            console: Vec::new(),
            filter: 1,
            follow: true,
            scroll: 0,
            tree: Tree::default(),
            dock: None,
            changes: Vec::new(),
            file: 0,
            on_disk: Vec::new(),
            focus: 0,
            viewing: None,
        }
    }

    /// The plan.
    pub fn plan(&self) -> &Plan {
        &self.parsed.plan
    }

    /// Whether the runner still runs.
    pub fn active(&self) -> bool {
        !matches!(self.phase, Phase::Ended(_))
    }

    fn task_of(&self, id: &str) -> Option<usize> {
        self.plan().tasks.iter().position(|t| &*t.id.raw == id)
    }

    fn place(&self, at: &At) -> Option<(usize, usize, usize)> {
        Some((self.task_of(&at.task)?, at.step, at.action))
    }

    fn log(&mut self, now: u64, label: Label, text: String, warning: bool) {
        self.console.push(Line {
            at: now.saturating_sub(self.started),
            label,
            text,
            warning,
        });
    }

    /// The run started.
    pub fn started(&mut self) {
        self.phase = Phase::Executing;
    }

    /// The run ended.
    pub fn ended(&mut self, now: u64, result: Result<Finished, String>) {
        let (text, warning) = match &result {
            Ok(f) if f.reason.is_none() => ("the run completed".to_owned(), false),
            Ok(f) => (
                format!("stopped: {}", f.reason.clone().unwrap_or_default()),
                true,
            ),
            Err(e) => (e.clone(), true),
        };
        self.log(now, Label::Event, text, warning);
        self.phase = Phase::Ended(result);
        self.dock = None;
    }

    /// `Ui::status`.
    pub fn status(&mut self, now: u64, line: &str) {
        let label = if line.starts_with("preflight") {
            Label::Pre
        } else {
            Label::Event
        };
        for l in line.lines() {
            self.log(now, label, l.to_owned(), false);
        }
    }

    /// `Ui::output`.
    pub fn output(&mut self, now: u64, line: &OutLine) {
        let label = match line.stream {
            Stream::Stdout => Label::Out,
            Stream::Stderr => Label::Err,
        };
        self.log(
            now,
            label,
            line.text.trim_end_matches('\r').to_owned(),
            false,
        );
    }

    /// `Ui::ask`.
    pub fn ask(&mut self, question: Question, reply: Sender<Reply>) {
        if let Some(at) = question.at {
            self.tree.select(Node::Action(at.task, at.step, at.action));
        }
        self.dock = Some(Dock::new(question, reply));
        self.focus = 2;
    }

    /// A journal record.
    pub fn record(&mut self, now: u64, record: &Record) -> Follow {
        match record {
            Record::TaskStart { task, .. } => {
                let Some(t) = self.task_of(task) else {
                    return Follow::None;
                };
                self.tasks_started.insert(t);
                self.current = Some(t);
                let title = self.plan().text(&self.plan().tasks[t].title).to_owned();
                self.log(now, Label::Task, format!("{task} {title}"), false);
                self.file = 0;
                self.on_disk.clear();
                self.changes = self.files(t);
                return self.refresh();
            }
            Record::StepPrepared { at, .. } => {
                self.log(
                    now,
                    Label::File,
                    format!("step {} prepared", at.step),
                    false,
                );
            }
            Record::StepWritten { at }
            | Record::StepVerified { at }
            | Record::StepAccepted { at } => {
                if let Some(p) = self.place(at) {
                    self.written.insert(p);
                }
                let what = if matches!(record, Record::StepWritten { .. }) {
                    "written"
                } else {
                    "found done"
                };
                let paths = self.group_paths(at);
                self.log(
                    now,
                    Label::File,
                    format!("step {what} {} {paths}", Glyphs::unicode().get(Glyph::Dot)),
                    false,
                );
                return self.refresh();
            }
            Record::StepFailed { error, .. } => {
                self.log(
                    now,
                    Label::File,
                    format!("step failed, rolled back: {error}"),
                    true,
                );
            }
            Record::ActionStart { at, kind, .. } => {
                if let Some(p) = self.place(at) {
                    self.acts.insert(p, Act::Running(now));
                    self.tree.select(Node::Action(p.0, p.1, p.2));
                    if kind == "script" {
                        let label = self.action_label(p);
                        self.log(now, Label::Run, format!("$ {label}"), false);
                    }
                }
            }
            Record::ActionEnd {
                at,
                outcome,
                status,
                head,
                ..
            } => {
                let Some(p) = self.place(at) else {
                    return Follow::None;
                };
                let started = match self.acts.get(&p) {
                    Some(Act::Running(s)) => *s,
                    _ => now,
                };
                self.acts.insert(
                    p,
                    Act::Ended {
                        outcome: *outcome,
                        took: now.saturating_sub(started),
                    },
                );
                let commit = matches!(self.action(p).map(|a| &a.kind), Some(ActionKind::Commit(_)));
                let warning = matches!(
                    outcome,
                    Outcome::Failed | Outcome::Refused | Outcome::Accepted | Outcome::Missing
                );
                let status = status.clone().unwrap_or_default();
                if commit {
                    let hash = head
                        .commit
                        .as_deref()
                        .map_or("", |c| &c[..c.len().min(7)])
                        .to_owned();
                    self.log(
                        now,
                        Label::Commit,
                        format!("{hash} {}", outcome_word(*outcome)),
                        warning,
                    );
                } else {
                    let glyph = if warning { Glyph::Failed } else { Glyph::Done };
                    self.log(
                        now,
                        Label::Verify,
                        format!(
                            "{} {} {status}",
                            Glyphs::unicode().get(glyph),
                            outcome_word(*outcome)
                        ),
                        warning,
                    );
                }
                return self.refresh();
            }
            Record::Answer {
                question, answer, ..
            } => {
                self.log(
                    now,
                    Label::Event,
                    format!("answered {question}: {answer}"),
                    false,
                );
            }
            Record::GateOverride { .. } => {
                self.log(
                    now,
                    Label::Event,
                    "the commit gate was opened with a typed yes".to_owned(),
                    true,
                );
            }
            Record::TaskEnd { task, head } => {
                if let Some(t) = self.task_of(task) {
                    self.tasks_done.insert(t, head.commit.clone());
                    self.tree.folded.insert(t);
                    return self.refresh();
                }
            }
            _ => {}
        }
        Follow::None
    }

    /// The paths of the file group at `at`.
    fn group_paths(&self, at: &At) -> String {
        let Some((t, s, a)) = self.place(at) else {
            return String::new();
        };
        let step = &self.plan().tasks[t].steps[s];
        let g = Glyphs::unicode();
        step.actions[a..]
            .iter()
            .take_while(|x| {
                planrun_exec::handler::file_path(&x.kind).is_some()
                    && !matches!(x.kind, ActionKind::Generated { .. })
            })
            .map(|x| {
                format!(
                    "{} {}",
                    g.get(tree::kind_glyph(x)),
                    tree::label(self.plan(), x)
                )
            })
            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            .join("  ")
    }

    fn action(&self, (t, s, a): (usize, usize, usize)) -> Option<&planrun_model::Action> {
        self.plan().tasks.get(t)?.steps.get(s)?.actions.get(a)
    }

    fn action_label(&self, p: (usize, usize, usize)) -> String {
        self.action(p)
            .map_or_else(String::new, |a| tree::label(self.plan(), a))
    }

    /// The current task's files: its file actions' paths and its manifest's file entries.
    fn files(&self, t: usize) -> Vec<FileDiff> {
        let plan = self.plan();
        let task = &plan.tasks[t];
        let mut out: Vec<FileDiff> = Vec::new();
        for action in task.steps.iter().flat_map(|s| s.actions.iter()) {
            if matches!(action.kind, ActionKind::Generated { .. }) {
                continue;
            }
            let Some(planned) = diff::planned(plan, &action.kind) else {
                continue;
            };
            match out.iter_mut().find(|f| f.path == planned.path) {
                Some(f) => f.rows.extend(planned.rows),
                None => out.push(planned),
            }
        }
        for entry in &task.manifest.entries {
            if let ManifestPath::File(p) = &entry.path
                && !out.iter().any(|f| f.path == p.to_string())
            {
                out.push(FileDiff {
                    path: p.to_string(),
                    rows: Vec::new(),
                });
            }
        }
        out
    }

    fn refresh(&self) -> Follow {
        match self.current {
            Some(t) => Follow::Changes(t, self.changes.iter().map(|f| f.path.clone()).collect()),
            None => Follow::None,
        }
    }

    /// `git diff HEAD` (or the commit's own diff) of task `t`'s paths.
    pub fn diffed(&mut self, t: usize, files: Vec<FileDiff>) {
        if self.current == Some(t) {
            self.on_disk = files;
        }
    }

    /// Whether the file group containing (`t`, `s`, `a`) was written.
    fn applied(&self, t: usize, s: usize, a: usize) -> bool {
        let step = &self.plan().tasks[t].steps[s];
        (0..=a)
            .rev()
            .take_while(|&i| planrun_exec::handler::file_path(&step.actions[i].kind).is_some())
            .any(|i| self.written.contains(&(t, s, i)))
    }

    /// The change state of `path` in the current task: committed, applied or planned.
    fn chip(&self, path: &str) -> (&'static str, Token) {
        let Some(t) = self.current else {
            return ("PLANNED", Token::Fg2);
        };
        if self.tasks_done.contains_key(&t) {
            return ("COMMITTED", Token::Ok);
        }
        let task = &self.plan().tasks[t];
        for (s, step) in task.steps.iter().enumerate() {
            for (a, action) in step.actions.iter().enumerate() {
                let same = planrun_exec::handler::file_path(&action.kind)
                    .is_some_and(|p| p.to_string() == path);
                if same && self.applied(t, s, a) {
                    return ("APPLIED", Token::Act);
                }
            }
        }
        ("PLANNED", Token::Fg2)
    }

    /// The navigator's mark of `node`.
    pub fn mark(&self, node: Node) -> Mark {
        match node {
            Node::Task(t) => {
                if let Some(head) = self.tasks_done.get(&t) {
                    let hash = head
                        .as_deref()
                        .map_or(String::new(), |c| c[..c.len().min(7)].to_owned());
                    return Mark {
                        glyph: Glyph::Done,
                        color: Token::Ok,
                        tag: hash,
                        tag_color: Token::Meta,
                    };
                }
                if self.current == Some(t) && self.active() {
                    let glyph = if self.dock.is_some() {
                        Glyph::Waiting
                    } else {
                        Glyph::Running
                    };
                    let color = if self.dock.is_some() {
                        Token::Warn
                    } else {
                        Token::Act
                    };
                    return Mark::plain(glyph, color);
                }
                if self.current == Some(t) {
                    return Mark::plain(Glyph::Failed, Token::Err);
                }
                let manual = self.plan().tasks[t]
                    .steps
                    .iter()
                    .flat_map(|s| s.actions.iter())
                    .filter(|a| matches!(a.kind, ActionKind::Manual { .. }))
                    .count();
                let tag = if manual > 0 {
                    format!("{} {manual}", Glyphs::unicode().get(Glyph::Manual))
                } else {
                    String::new()
                };
                Mark {
                    glyph: Glyph::Pending,
                    color: Token::Fg3,
                    tag,
                    tag_color: Token::Warn,
                }
            }
            Node::Step(t, s) => {
                let n = self.plan().tasks[t].steps[s].actions.len();
                let marks: Vec<Mark> = (0..n).map(|a| self.mark(Node::Action(t, s, a))).collect();
                let pick = [
                    Glyph::Waiting,
                    Glyph::Running,
                    Glyph::Failed,
                    Glyph::Warning,
                ]
                .into_iter()
                .find_map(|g| marks.iter().find(|m| m.glyph == g));
                if let Some(m) = pick {
                    return Mark::plain(m.glyph, m.color);
                }
                if !marks.is_empty()
                    && marks.iter().all(|m| {
                        m.glyph == Glyph::Done || m.glyph == Glyph::Skipped || m.glyph == Glyph::Dot
                    })
                    && marks.iter().any(|m| m.glyph == Glyph::Done)
                {
                    return Mark::plain(Glyph::Done, Token::Ok);
                }
                if !marks.is_empty() && marks.iter().all(|m| m.glyph == Glyph::Skipped) {
                    return Mark::plain(Glyph::Skipped, Token::Fg3);
                }
                Mark::plain(Glyph::Pending, Token::Fg3)
            }
            Node::Action(t, s, a) => self.action_mark(t, s, a),
        }
    }

    fn action_mark(&self, t: usize, s: usize, a: usize) -> Mark {
        let plan = self.plan();
        let task = &plan.tasks[t];
        let action = &task.steps[s].actions[a];
        let waiting = self
            .dock
            .as_ref()
            .and_then(|d| d.question.at)
            .is_some_and(|p| (p.task, p.step, p.action) == (t, s, a));
        if waiting {
            let tag = if self
                .dock
                .as_ref()
                .is_some_and(|d| d.question.typed.is_some())
            {
                "yes?"
            } else {
                "waiting"
            };
            return Mark {
                glyph: Glyph::Waiting,
                color: Token::Warn,
                tag: tag.into(),
                tag_color: Token::Warn,
            };
        }
        if matches!(action.kind, ActionKind::Note { .. }) {
            return Mark::plain(Glyph::Dot, Token::Fg3);
        }
        let file = planrun_exec::handler::file_path(&action.kind).is_some()
            && !matches!(action.kind, ActionKind::Generated { .. });
        if file && self.applied(t, s, a) {
            return Mark {
                glyph: Glyph::Done,
                color: Token::Ok,
                tag: "applied".into(),
                tag_color: Token::Fg3,
            };
        }
        match self.acts.get(&(t, s, a)) {
            Some(Act::Running(_)) => Mark {
                glyph: Glyph::Running,
                color: Token::Act,
                tag: "running".into(),
                tag_color: Token::Act,
            },
            Some(Act::Ended { outcome, took }) => {
                let (glyph, color) = match outcome {
                    Outcome::Passed
                    | Outcome::Confirmed
                    | Outcome::Acknowledged
                    | Outcome::Present
                    | Outcome::Kept => (Glyph::Done, Token::Ok),
                    Outcome::Accepted => (Glyph::Warning, Token::Warn),
                    Outcome::SkippedPolicy | Outcome::SkippedUser => (Glyph::Skipped, Token::Fg3),
                    Outcome::Failed | Outcome::Refused | Outcome::Missing => {
                        (Glyph::Failed, Token::Err)
                    }
                };
                let tag = match outcome {
                    Outcome::Passed | Outcome::Confirmed => text::duration(*took),
                    other => outcome_word(*other).to_owned(),
                };
                Mark {
                    glyph,
                    color,
                    tag,
                    tag_color: Token::Fg3,
                }
            }
            None => {
                let decision = self.assumed.policy().decide(
                    task,
                    action,
                    &plan.source,
                    &planrun_exec::policy::DefaultClassifier,
                );
                let (tag, tag_color) = tag(action, decision);
                Mark {
                    glyph: Glyph::Pending,
                    color: Token::Fg3,
                    tag,
                    tag_color,
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /// Handles `key` outside the dock; false when it is not the run view's.
    pub fn key(&mut self, key: Key) -> bool {
        match key {
            Key::Tab => self.focus = (self.focus + 1) % 3,
            Key::BackTab => self.focus = (self.focus + 2) % 3,
            Key::Char('f') => {
                self.follow = true;
            }
            Key::Char(c @ '1'..='5') => self.filter = c as u8 - b'0',
            _ => return self.panel_key(key),
        }
        true
    }

    fn panel_key(&mut self, key: Key) -> bool {
        match self.focus {
            0 => {
                let plan = self.parsed.plan.clone();
                self.tree.key(key, &plan)
            }
            1 => {
                let n = self.changes.len().max(1);
                match key {
                    Key::Right | Key::Char('l') => self.file = (self.file + 1) % n,
                    Key::Left | Key::Char('h') => self.file = (self.file + n - 1) % n,
                    _ => return false,
                }
                true
            }
            _ => {
                let shown = self.shown().len();
                let at = if self.follow {
                    shown.saturating_sub(1)
                } else {
                    self.scroll
                };
                match key {
                    Key::Up | Key::Char('k') => {
                        self.follow = false;
                        self.scroll = at.saturating_sub(1);
                    }
                    Key::Down | Key::Char('j') => {
                        self.scroll = (at + 1).min(shown.saturating_sub(1));
                        self.follow = self.scroll + 1 >= shown;
                    }
                    Key::PageUp => {
                        self.follow = false;
                        self.scroll = at.saturating_sub(10);
                    }
                    Key::End => self.follow = true,
                    _ => return false,
                }
                true
            }
        }
    }

    fn shown(&self) -> Vec<&Line> {
        self.console
            .iter()
            .filter(|l| l.label.shown(self.filter, l.warning))
            .collect()
    }

    /// The header's right side.
    pub fn state(&self, g: Glyphs) -> Vec<(String, Style)> {
        let bar = |t: Token| on(t, Token::Bg1);
        let mut out = vec![
            (" RUN ".to_owned(), bold(Token::Act).bg(Token::Bg1.color())),
            ("  ".to_owned(), bar(Token::Fg)),
        ];
        match (&self.phase, &self.dock) {
            (Phase::Ended(Ok(f)), _) if f.reason.is_none() => {
                out.push((format!("{} done", g.get(Glyph::Done)), bar(Token::Ok)));
            }
            (Phase::Ended(_), _) => {
                out.push((format!("{} stopped", g.get(Glyph::Failed)), bar(Token::Err)))
            }
            (_, Some(_)) => out.push((
                format!("{} waiting for you", g.get(Glyph::Waiting)),
                bold(Token::Warn).bg(Token::Bg1.color()),
            )),
            (Phase::Starting, None) => out.push((
                format!("{} starting", g.get(Glyph::Background)),
                bar(Token::Fg2),
            )),
            (Phase::Executing, None) => {
                out.push((format!("{} ", g.get(Glyph::Running)), bar(Token::Act)));
                out.push(("executing".to_owned(), bar(Token::Fg)));
            }
        }
        if self.dock.is_none() || !self.active() {
            out.push((
                format!("  {}  {}", g.get(Glyph::Dot), self.assumed.describe()),
                bar(Token::Fg3),
            ));
        }
        out
    }

    /// The crumbs after the repository.
    pub fn crumbs(&self, g: Glyphs) -> Vec<String> {
        let name = self
            .path
            .file_name()
            .map_or_else(String::new, |n| n.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
        let mut out = vec![name];
        let plan = self.plan();
        let Some(node) = self.tree.current(plan) else {
            return out;
        };
        out.push(plan.tasks[node.task()].id.raw.to_string());
        if let Node::Action(t, s, a) = node {
            let step = &plan.tasks[t].steps[s];
            if let Some(n) = step.number {
                out.push(format!("step {n}"));
            }
            let action = &step.actions[a];
            let label = tree::label(plan, action);
            let short = match action.kind {
                ActionKind::RunScript(_) => {
                    label.split_whitespace().next().unwrap_or("").to_owned()
                }
                _ => text::fit(&label, 24, g.get(Glyph::Ellipsis)),
            };
            out.push(format!("{} {short}", g.get(tree::kind_glyph(action))));
        }
        out
    }

    /// The keys the footer shows.
    pub fn footer(&self) -> Vec<(String, String)> {
        if let Some(dock) = &self.dock {
            return dock.footer();
        }
        if !self.active() {
            return vec![
                ("esc".into(), "plans".into()),
                ("F4".into(), "history".into()),
                ("tab".into(), "next panel".into()),
            ];
        }
        vec![
            ("f".into(), "follow".into()),
            ("1–5".into(), "filter".into()),
            ("tab".into(), "next panel".into()),
            ("ctrl+c".into(), "interrupt…".into()),
        ]
    }

    /// Draws the run view in `area`.
    pub fn draw(&self, buf: &mut Buffer, area: Rect, g: Glyphs) {
        let plan = self.plan();
        let nav = Rect::new(area.x, area.y, tree::WIDTH, area.height);
        let n = plan.tasks.len();
        let at = self.current.map_or(0, |t| t + 1);
        let title = format!("task {at} of {n}");
        self.tree.draw(
            buf,
            nav,
            plan,
            &title,
            self.focus == 0,
            &|node| self.mark(node),
            g,
        );
        let right = Rect::new(
            area.x + tree::WIDTH,
            area.y,
            area.width - tree::WIDTH,
            area.height,
        );
        let upper_h = right.height * 55 / 100;
        let upper = Rect::new(right.x, right.y, right.width, upper_h);
        let lower = Rect::new(
            right.x,
            right.y + upper_h,
            right.width,
            right.height - upper_h,
        );
        self.draw_changes(buf, upper, g);
        match &self.dock {
            Some(dock) => dock.draw(buf, lower, plan, self.focus == 2, g),
            None => self.draw_console(buf, lower, g),
        }
    }

    fn draw_changes(&self, buf: &mut Buffer, area: Rect, g: Glyphs) {
        let task = self
            .current
            .map(|t| self.plan().tasks[t].id.raw.to_string());
        let n = self.changes.len();
        let detail = match &task {
            Some(id) => format!(
                "task {id} {} {n} file{}",
                g.get(Glyph::Dot),
                if n == 1 { "" } else { "s" }
            ),
            None => String::new(),
        };
        band(buf, area, self.focus == 1, "CHANGES", &detail, g);
        if n == 0 {
            return;
        }
        let shown: Vec<FileDiff> = self
            .changes
            .iter()
            .map(|planned| {
                self.on_disk
                    .iter()
                    .find(|d| d.path == planned.path)
                    .cloned()
                    .unwrap_or_else(|| planned.clone())
            })
            .collect();
        let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x + 2, area.y + 1, area.right(), g);
        for (i, f) in shown.iter().enumerate() {
            let (add, del) = f.counts();
            let style = if i == self.file {
                bold(Token::Fg)
            } else {
                fg(Token::Fg2)
            };
            pen.text(&f.path, style).gap(1);
            if add > 0 {
                pen.text(&format!("+{add}"), fg(Token::Fg2)).gap(1);
            }
            if del > 0 {
                pen.text(&format!("{}{del}", g.get(Glyph::Delete)), fg(Token::Fg2))
                    .gap(1);
            }
            pen.gap(2);
        }
        hrule(buf, area.x + 1, area.right(), area.y + 2, g);
        let file = &shown[self.file.min(n - 1)];
        let header = Rect::new(
            area.x + 1,
            area.y + 3,
            area.width - 1,
            area.height.saturating_sub(3),
        );
        let chip = self.chip(&file.path);
        let detail = match chip.0 {
            "APPLIED" => "on disk, not committed",
            "COMMITTED" => "in HEAD",
            _ => "not written yet",
        };
        diff::header(buf, header, file, chip, detail, g);
        let rows = Rect::new(
            header.x,
            header.y + 1,
            header.width,
            header.height.saturating_sub(1),
        );
        diff::draw_rows(buf, rows, &file.rows, 0, g);
    }

    fn draw_console(&self, buf: &mut Buffer, area: Rect, g: Glyphs) {
        band(buf, area, self.focus == 2, "CONSOLE", "", g);
        let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x + 13, area.y, area.right(), g);
        for (i, name) in ["all", "events", "output", "verify", "warnings"]
            .iter()
            .enumerate()
        {
            let on_ = usize::from(self.filter) == i + 1;
            let style = if on_ {
                bold(Token::Act).bg(Token::Bg1.color())
            } else {
                on(Token::Fg3, Token::Bg1)
            };
            pen.text(name, style).gap(3);
        }
        let follow = if self.follow {
            format!("{} following", g.get(Glyph::Follow))
        } else {
            "f follows".to_owned()
        };
        Pen::new(buf, area.x, area.y, area.right().saturating_sub(1), g)
            .right(&follow, on(Token::Fg3, Token::Bg1));
        let lines = self.shown();
        let live = self.active() && self.dock.is_none();
        let height = usize::from(area.height.saturating_sub(1 + u16::from(live)));
        let end = if self.follow {
            lines.len()
        } else {
            (self.scroll + 1).min(lines.len())
        };
        let start = end.saturating_sub(height);
        for (i, line) in lines[start..end].iter().enumerate() {
            let y = area.y + 1 + u16::try_from(i).unwrap_or(0);
            let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x + 2, y, area.right(), g);
            pen.text(&clock(line.at), fg(Token::Fg3));
            if line.label == Label::Err {
                pen.glyph(Glyph::Select, fg(Token::Warn));
            } else {
                pen.gap(1);
            }
            pen.cell(line.label.word(), 8, line.label.style());
            let color = if line.warning {
                Token::Warn
            } else if line.label == Label::Run {
                Token::Act
            } else {
                Token::Fg
            };
            pen.text(&line.text, fg(color));
        }
        if live {
            let y = area.y + 1 + u16::try_from(end - start).unwrap_or(0);
            let running = self
                .acts
                .values()
                .filter_map(|a| match a {
                    Act::Running(s) => Some(*s),
                    _ => None,
                })
                .max();
            let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x + 2, y, area.right(), g);
            pen.glyph(Glyph::Spin(0), bold(Token::Act)).gap(1);
            let what = match running {
                Some(_) => "running".to_owned(),
                None => "working".to_owned(),
            };
            pen.text(
                &format!("{what} {} {}", g.get(Glyph::Dot), self.assumed.describe()),
                fg(Token::Fg2),
            );
        }
    }
}

fn outcome_word(outcome: Outcome) -> &'static str {
    match outcome {
        Outcome::Passed => "passed",
        Outcome::Confirmed => "confirmed",
        Outcome::Refused => "refused",
        Outcome::Accepted => "accepted",
        Outcome::Failed => "failed",
        Outcome::SkippedPolicy => "skipped",
        Outcome::SkippedUser => "skipped by you",
        Outcome::Acknowledged => "done",
        Outcome::Present => "present",
        Outcome::Missing => "missing",
        Outcome::Kept => "kept",
    }
}

/// `mm:ss` since the run started (D94).
pub fn clock(ms: u64) -> String {
    let s = ms / 1000;
    format!("{:02}:{:02}", s / 60, s % 60)
}
```

File `crates/planrun-tui/src/worker.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! The runner on its worker thread (decision D90). The runner stays synchronous: it reaches the
//! terminal only through the [`Ui`] here, whose `ask`, `shell`, `view` and `diff` are blocking
//! round trips to the render thread; `status` and `output` are sent and not waited on. The live
//! view comes from the journal: an observer, built on this thread (it is not `Send`), forwards
//! every record over the same channel.

use std::panic::{AssertUnwindSafe, catch_unwind};
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::sync::mpsc::{Sender, channel};

use planrun_exec::journal::{self, Journal, Observer, Record, Seam};
use planrun_exec::lock;
use planrun_exec::policy::{Policy, PolicyClass};
use planrun_exec::runner::resume::analyse;
use planrun_exec::runner::{Finished, RunConfig, Runner};
use planrun_exec::shell::{OutLine, SessionConfig};
use planrun_exec::ui::{Position, Question, Reply, Ui};
use planrun_exec::workspace::Workspace;
use planrun_parse::Parsed;

use crate::app::Msg;
use crate::jobs::Context;

/// Builds an extra journal observer on the worker thread (the tests' crash seam, D93).
pub type ObserverFactory = Box<dyn FnOnce() -> Observer + Send>;

/// What to run.
pub struct RunRequest {
    /// The plan file.
    pub path: PathBuf,
    /// Its parsed plan.
    pub parsed: Arc<Parsed>,
    /// `--auto`.
    pub auto: Vec<PolicyClass>,
    /// `--run-expected-failures`.
    pub expected: bool,
    /// The first task, by position.
    pub from: Option<usize>,
    /// Resume the journal's unfinished run instead of starting one.
    pub resume: bool,
    /// More journal observers, after the TUI's own.
    pub observers: Vec<ObserverFactory>,
}

/// What the worker tells the render thread.
pub enum RunMsg {
    /// The run took the lock and opened its journal.
    Started,
    /// A journal record was appended.
    Record(Record),
    /// A step's commit wrote its n-th path.
    Wrote(usize),
    /// `Ui::status`.
    Status(String),
    /// `Ui::output`.
    Output(OutLine),
    /// `Ui::ask`: answer on the sender.
    Ask(Question, Sender<Reply>),
    /// `Ui::view`: show the action; reply when shown.
    View(Position, Sender<()>),
    /// `Ui::diff`: show the working tree's diff; reply when shown.
    Diff(Sender<()>),
    /// `Ui::shell`: run the `[o]` shell; reply when it exited.
    Shell(Sender<()>),
    /// The run ended, or could not start.
    Ended(Result<Finished, String>),
}

/// The [`Ui`] on the worker thread.
struct ChannelUi {
    tx: Sender<Msg>,
}

impl ChannelUi {
    fn send(&self, msg: RunMsg) {
        let _ = self.tx.send(Msg::Run(msg));
    }

    fn round_trip(&self, msg: impl FnOnce(Sender<()>) -> RunMsg) {
        let (tx, rx) = channel();
        self.send(msg(tx));
        let _ = rx.recv();
    }
}

impl Ui for ChannelUi {
    fn status(&mut self, line: &str) {
        self.send(RunMsg::Status(line.to_owned()));
    }

    fn output(&mut self, line: &OutLine) {
        self.send(RunMsg::Output(line.clone()));
    }

    fn ask(&mut self, question: &Question) -> Reply {
        let (tx, rx) = channel();
        self.send(RunMsg::Ask(question.clone(), tx));
        rx.recv()
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| Reply::Stop("the TUI closed before an answer".to_owned()))
    }

    fn view(&mut self, at: Position) {
        self.round_trip(|tx| RunMsg::View(at, tx));
    }

    fn diff(&mut self) {
        self.round_trip(RunMsg::Diff);
    }

    fn shell(&mut self) {
        self.round_trip(RunMsg::Shell);
    }
}

/// Starts `request` on a thread of its own; everything it has to say arrives as
/// [`Msg::Run`].
pub fn start(request: RunRequest, ctx: Arc<Context>, tx: Sender<Msg>) {
    std::thread::spawn(move || {
        let done = tx.clone();
        let result = catch_unwind(AssertUnwindSafe(|| run(request, &ctx, tx)));
        let ended = match result {
            Ok(ended) => ended,
            Err(payload) => Err(format!(
                "the run's thread panicked: {}",
                crate::jobs::panic_message(payload.as_ref())
            )),
        };
        let _ = done.send(Msg::Run(RunMsg::Ended(ended)));
    });
}

fn run(request: RunRequest, ctx: &Context, tx: Sender<Msg>) -> Result<Finished, String> {
    let ws = Workspace::open(&ctx.root, ctx.env.clone()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let _lock = lock::acquire(&ctx.journal_dir()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let stem = request
        .path
        .file_stem()
        .map_or_else(|| "plan".to_owned(), |s| s.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
    let path = journal::path_for(&ctx.git_dir, &stem);
    let plan_path = ctx.plan_path(&request.path);
    let lines = journal::read(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if let Some(other) = lines.iter().find_map(|l| match &l.record {
        Record::Run { plan, .. } if *plan != plan_path => Some(plan.clone()),
        _ => None,
    }) {
        return Err(format!(
            "the journal {} belongs to the plan {other}: two plans share a file name",
            path.display()
        ));
    }
    let analysis = analyse(&lines).filter(|a| a.resumable());
    if analysis.is_some() && !request.resume {
        return Err("the plan has an unfinished run: resume it from History (F4)".to_owned());
    }
    if analysis.is_none() && request.resume {
        return Err("there is no unfinished run to resume".to_owned());
    }
    let mut journal = Journal::open(&path).map_err(|e| format!("{}: {e}", path.display()))?;
    journal.observe(forward(tx.clone()));
    for factory in request.observers {
        journal.observe(factory());
    }
    let policy = Policy::new(&request.auto, request.expected).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let config = RunConfig {
        plan_path,
        policy,
        from: request.from,
        to: None,
        session: SessionConfig {
            root: ws.root().to_path_buf(),
            env: ctx.env.clone(),
            timeout: None,
            temp: std::env::temp_dir(),
        },
    };
    let _ = tx.send(Msg::Run(RunMsg::Started));
    let mut ui = ChannelUi { tx };
    let plan = &request.parsed.plan;
    let mut runner = Runner::new(plan, &ws, &mut journal, &mut ui, config);
    let finished = match &analysis {
        Some(analysis) => runner.resume(analysis),
        None => runner.run(),
    };
    finished.map_err(|e| e.to_string())
}

/// The TUI's observer: every record and write, forwarded.
fn forward(tx: Sender<Msg>) -> Observer {
    Box::new(move |seam| {
        let msg = match seam {
            Seam::Appended(record) => RunMsg::Record(record.clone()),
            Seam::Wrote(n) => RunMsg::Wrote(n),
        };
        let _ = tx.send(Msg::Run(msg));
    })
}
```

File `crates/planrun-tui/tests/run.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! A run in the TUI (decisions D90, D93): the synthetic assay plan is inspected, run with the
//! policy Inspect assumes, answered in the dock, and committed, on a copy of the greeter fixture,
//! with the real runner on its worker thread.

mod support;

use std::time::Duration;

use planrun_exec::shell::{ShellError, locate};
use planrun_exec::ui::{Choice, Reply};
use planrun_model::Shell;
use planrun_tui::driver::Core;
use planrun_tui::jobs::Bg;
use planrun_tui::keys::Key;
use planrun_tui::run::Phase;
use ratatui::backend::TestBackend;
use support::{Setup, screen, setup};

/// Whether pwsh is there; without it the test is skipped unless shells are required (D63).
pub fn pwsh() -> bool {
    match locate(Shell::Pwsh) {
        Ok(_) => true,
        Err(e @ ShellError::Unavailable { .. })
            if std::env::var_os("PLANRUN_REQUIRE_SHELLS").is_none() =>
        {
            eprintln!("skipped: {e}");
            false
        }
        Err(e) => panic!("{e}"),
    }
}

/// Opens the assay plan, assumes `--auto read,build,modify` and presses `R`.
fn started(setup: &Setup) -> Core<TestBackend> {
    let mut core = setup.core();
    core.key(Key::Enter);
    let inspected = core.wait(Duration::from_secs(60), |app| {
        app.inspect()
            .is_some_and(|i| matches!(i.dry, Bg::Current(_)))
    });
    assert!(inspected, "the dry run did not finish");
    for key in [
        Key::Char('a'),
        Key::Char('r'),
        Key::Char('b'),
        Key::Char('m'),
        Key::Enter,
        Key::Char('R'),
    ] {
        core.key(key);
    }
    core
}

/// Waits for the next question or the end; `None` at the end.
fn next_question(core: &mut Core<TestBackend>) -> Option<String> {
    let came = core.wait(Duration::from_secs(300), |app| {
        app.run()
            .is_some_and(|r| r.dock.is_some() || matches!(r.phase, Phase::Ended(_)))
    });
    assert!(came, "the run neither asked nor ended in time");
    let run = core.app().run()?;
    run.dock.as_ref().map(|d| d.question.kind.name().to_owned())
}

/// Answers the open question as the plans' replays do: a typed question `yes`, a confirmation
/// `y`, anything else enter.
fn answer(core: &mut Core<TestBackend>) {
    let Some(dock) = core.app().run().and_then(|r| r.dock.as_ref()) else {
        return;
    };
    let keys: Vec<Key> = match dock.question.typed {
        Some(_) => "yes".chars().map(Key::Char).chain([Key::Enter]).collect(),
        None if dock.question.choices.contains(&Choice::Yes) => vec![Key::Char('y')],
        None => vec![Key::Enter],
    };
    for key in keys {
        core.key(key);
    }
}

#[test]
fn the_assay_plan_runs_to_two_commits_through_the_dock() {
    if !pwsh() {
        return;
    }
    let setup = setup(&["assay-style.md"]);
    let mut core = started(&setup);
    let mut asked = Vec::new();
    while let Some(kind) = next_question(&mut core) {
        if asked.is_empty() {
            let text = screen(&mut core);
            assert!(text.contains("waiting for you"), "{text}");
            assert!(text.contains("CHANGES"), "{text}");
        }
        asked.push(kind);
        assert!(asked.len() < 20, "too many questions: {asked:?}");
        answer(&mut core);
    }
    assert_eq!(
        asked,
        ["confirm", "confirm"],
        "the commits' prose confirmations"
    );
    let run = core.app().run().unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("no run"));
    match &run.phase {
        Phase::Ended(Ok(f)) => assert_eq!(f.reason, None, "{f:?}"),
        other => panic!("{other:?}"),
    }
    assert_eq!(run.tasks_done.len(), 2);
    let log = setup.fixture.git(&["log", "--format=%s"]);
    assert_eq!(log.lines().count(), 3, "{log}");
    assert_eq!(
        setup.fixture.status(),
        "",
        "the tree is clean after the run"
    );
    let text = screen(&mut core);
    assert!(text.contains("done"), "{text}");
}

#[test]
fn a_typed_question_refuses_a_word_it_does_not_take() {
    let (tx, rx) = std::sync::mpsc::channel();
    let question = planrun_exec::ui::Question {
        kind: planrun_exec::ui::QuestionKind::Gate {
            reasons: vec!["a skip by you".to_owned()],
        },
        at: None,
        text: Vec::new(),
        choices: Vec::new(),
        typed: Some(planrun_exec::ui::YES_OR_QUIT),
    };
    let mut dock = planrun_tui::dock::Dock::new(question, tx);
    for c in "y".chars() {
        assert_eq!(dock.key(Key::Char(c)), None);
    }
    assert_eq!(dock.key(Key::Enter), None, "a single key never answers");
    assert_eq!(dock.refused.as_deref(), Some("y"));
    for c in "yes".chars() {
        dock.key(Key::Char(c));
    }
    let reply = dock.key(Key::Enter).unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("no reply"));
    assert_eq!(reply, Reply::Typed("yes".to_owned()));
    dock.answer(reply);
    assert_eq!(rx.recv().ok(), Some(Reply::Typed("yes".to_owned())));
}
```

**Step 2 — verify**

Targeted: a run through the dock, on a fixture copy:



**Step 3 — commit**

Only the task's paths are staged, only when the gate's exit code above was 0, and only when every staged file is the verified one:

```powershell

  }
  $differ = @($verified.Keys | Where-Object { (git rev-parse ":$_") -ne $verified[$_] })
  if ($differ.Count -eq 0) { git commit -m "P6c-T4: Run mode, the worker and the dock" }
  else { "staged files differ from the verified tree: $($differ -join ', ')" }

git status --porcelain
```
