Expected: the commit, then no output from `git status --porcelain`. A line naming files instead of the commit means those files differ from the verified tree.

### P6c-T5: the round trips and Ctrl-C

**Goal:** The dock's round trips and Ctrl-C: `[o]` runs the shell through the driver's hooks (D73, D90), `[v]` and `[d]` are shown with the dock open, Ctrl-C asks in the dock and then ends planrun with `shell::end_now` (D91), and `q` at a commit's question needs pressing twice.

**Files**

- Modify `crates/planrun-tui/src/app.rs`
- Modify `crates/planrun-tui/src/dock.rs`
- Modify `crates/planrun-tui/src/driver.rs`
- Modify `crates/planrun-tui/src/jobs.rs`
- Modify `crates/planrun-tui/src/run.rs`
- Modify `crates/planrun-tui/src/terminal.rs`
- Modify `crates/planrun-tui/tests/plans.rs`
- Create `crates/planrun-tui/tests/round_trips.rs`
- Modify `crates/planrun-tui/tests/support/mod.rs`

Over 300 lines, so they come as exact diffs: `crates/planrun-tui/src/app.rs`, `crates/planrun-tui/src/dock.rs`, `crates/planrun-tui/src/jobs.rs`, `crates/planrun-tui/src/run.rs`.

```rust
//! The loop's core, shared by the terminal and the tests (decision D93): it feeds the app keys,
//! ticks and messages, carries out the effects the app asks for, and draws. The terminal adds
//! crossterm's events; the tests a `TestBackend` and scripted keys.

use std::panic::{AssertUnwindSafe, catch_unwind};
use std::path::Path;
use std::sync::mpsc::{Receiver, Sender, channel};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use ratatui::Terminal;
use ratatui::backend::Backend;

use crate::app::{App, Effect, Msg};
use crate::jobs::panic_message;
use crate::keys::Key;

/// What the driver cannot do on its own: the terminal's half of the `[o]` round trip, and
/// ending planrun on a confirmed Ctrl-C.
pub trait Hooks {
    /// Leaves the screen, runs an interactive shell at `root` until it exits, and comes back;
    /// Ctrl-C reaches the shell, not planrun (D73).
    fn shell(&mut self, root: &Path);
    /// Ends planrun the way an interrupt does (D91). The terminal's never returns.
    fn end_now(&mut self);
}

/// The app, its terminal, and the channel its threads answer on.
pub struct Core<B: Backend> {
    app: App,
    terminal: Terminal<B>,
    tx: Sender<Msg>,
    rx: Receiver<Msg>,
    hooks: Box<dyn Hooks>,
}

impl<B: Backend> Core<B> {
    /// The core over `terminal`, with `hooks` for the shell and Ctrl-C.
    pub fn new(app: App, terminal: Terminal<B>, hooks: Box<dyn Hooks>) -> Core<B> {
        let (tx, rx) = channel();
        Core {
            app,
            terminal,
            tx,
            rx,
            hooks,
        }
    }

    /// The app.
    pub fn app(&self) -> &App {
        &self.app
    }

    /// The app, to change it (the tests add a crash observer, D93).
    pub fn app_mut(&mut self) -> &mut App {
        &mut self.app
    }

    /// The terminal.
    pub fn terminal(&self) -> &Terminal<B> {
        &self.terminal
    }

    /// Whether the app is done.
    pub fn done(&self) -> bool {
        self.app.done()
    }

    /// Starts the app's first jobs.
    pub fn start(&mut self) {
        let effects = self.app.start();
        self.apply(effects);
    }

    /// Feeds a key.
    pub fn key(&mut self, key: Key) {
        let effects = self.app.key(key);
        self.apply(effects);
    }

    /// Lets time pass.
    pub fn tick(&mut self) {
        let effects = self.app.tick();
        self.apply(effects);
    }

    /// Hands the app every message that has arrived; true when there was one.
    pub fn pump(&mut self) -> bool {
        let mut any = false;
        while let Ok(msg) = self.rx.try_recv() {
            any = true;
            let effects = self.app.msg(msg);
            self.apply(effects);
        }
        any
    }

    /// Waits up to `timeout` for `until` to hold, handing the app messages as they arrive.
    pub fn wait(&mut self, timeout: Duration, until: impl Fn(&App) -> bool) -> bool {
        let end = Instant::now() + timeout;
        loop {
            self.pump();
            if until(&self.app) {
                return true;
            }
            let left = end.saturating_duration_since(Instant::now());
            if left.is_zero() {
                return false;
            }
            if let Ok(msg) = self.rx.recv_timeout(left.min(Duration::from_millis(20))) {
                let effects = self.app.msg(msg);
                self.apply(effects);
            }
        }
    }

    /// Draws the app.
    pub fn draw(&mut self) -> Result<(), B::Error> {
        let app = &self.app;
        self.terminal.draw(|frame| app.draw(frame.buffer_mut()))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Forces a full redraw after the screen was left.
    pub fn redraw(&mut self) -> Result<(), B::Error> {
        self.terminal.clear()?;
        self.draw()
    }

    fn apply(&mut self, effects: Vec<Effect>) {
        for effect in effects {
            match effect {
                Effect::Job(job) => {
                    let tx = self.tx.clone();
                    let ctx = self.app.context();
                    let failed = job.on_failure();
                    std::thread::spawn(move || {
                        let done = match catch_unwind(AssertUnwindSafe(|| job.run(&ctx))) {
                            Ok(done) => done,
                            Err(payload) => failed(format!(
                                "the job panicked: {}",
                                panic_message(payload.as_ref())
                            )),
                        };
                        let _ = tx.send(Msg::Done(done));
                    });
                }
                Effect::Run(request) => {
                    crate::worker::start(request, self.app.context(), self.tx.clone());
                }
                Effect::Shell(reply) => {
                    let root = self.app.context().root.clone();
                    self.hooks.shell(&root);
                    let _ = self.redraw();
                    let _ = reply.send(());
                }
                Effect::EndNow => {
                    self.hooks.end_now();
                    self.app.quit = true;
                }
                Effect::Quit => self.app.quit = true,
            }
        }
    }
}
```

```diff
+/// The output of `git --no-optional-locks args…`; empty when git fails.
+fn git_out(ctx: &Context, args: &[&str]) -> String {
+    let out = std::process::Command::new("git")
+        .arg("--no-optional-locks")
+        .args(args)
+        .current_dir(&ctx.root)
+        .envs(ctx.env.iter().map(|(k, v)| (k, v)))
+        .output();
+    match out {
+        Ok(out) if out.status.success() => String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout).into_owned(),
+        _ => String::new(),
+    }
+}
```

File `crates/planrun-tui/src/terminal.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! `planrun tui` on a real terminal (decision D94): stdin and stdout must be terminals; the
//! repository is the one of the current directory (D88); the plans directory defaults to
//! `docs/plans` under its root. One loop polls crossterm's events and drains the app's channel
//! between polls; there is no input thread, which would read the keys meant for the `[o]` shell.
//! Interrupts are installed once, before the terminal is set up; a panic restores the terminal
//! only when it is the render thread's.

use std::io::IsTerminal;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::thread::ThreadId;
use std::time::Duration;

use ratatui::crossterm::event::{self, Event};
use ratatui::crossterm::execute;
use ratatui::crossterm::terminal::{self, EnterAlternateScreen};

use planrun_exec::shell::{InterruptsIgnored, install_interrupt_handler};
use planrun_exec::workspace::Workspace;

use crate::app::{App, system_clock};
use crate::driver::{Core, Hooks};
use crate::glyph::Glyphs;
use crate::jobs::{Context, UpdateCheck};
use crate::keys;

/// How long one poll for a key waits before the app's channel is drained again.
const POLL: Duration = Duration::from_millis(50);

/// What planrun-cli hands the TUI.
pub struct Host {
    /// The current directory: its repository is the target.
    pub dir: PathBuf,
    /// The plans directory as given (relative to `dir`), or `None` for `docs/plans` under the
    /// repository's root.
    pub plans: Option<PathBuf>,
    /// planrun's version.
    pub version: String,
    /// The update check.
    pub update: Option<UpdateCheck>,
}

/// The default plans directory, under the repository's root (D94).
pub const DEFAULT_PLANS: &str = "docs/plans";

/// Builds the context for the repository of `dir`.
pub fn context(
    dir: &Path,
    plans: Option<&Path>,
    version: &str,
    env: Vec<(std::ffi::OsString, std::ffi::OsString)>,
) -> Result<Context, String> {
    let ws = Workspace::open(dir, env.clone()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let git_dir = ws.git().git_dir().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let root = ws.root().to_path_buf();
    let plans_dir = match plans {
        Some(p) if p.is_absolute() => p.to_path_buf(),
        Some(p) => dir.join(p),
        None => root.join(DEFAULT_PLANS),
    };
    let canonical = std::fs::canonicalize(&plans_dir).unwrap_or_else(|_| plans_dir.clone());
    let plans_label = match canonical.strip_prefix(&root) {
        Ok(rel) if rel.as_os_str().is_empty() => ".".to_owned(),
        Ok(rel) => rel.to_string_lossy().replace('\\', "/"),
        Err(_) => plans_dir.display().to_string(),
    };
    let repo = root.file_name().map_or_else(
        || root.display().to_string(),
        |n| n.to_string_lossy().into_owned(),
    );
    Ok(Context {
        target: root.display().to_string(),
        repo,
        root,
        git_dir,
        plans_dir,
        plans_label,
        version: version.to_owned(),
        env,
    })
}

/// Runs the TUI until it quits. `Err` is the message for a usage problem: not a terminal, not a
/// repository, or a terminal that could not be set up.
pub fn run(host: Host) -> Result<(), String> {
    if !std::io::stdin().is_terminal() || !std::io::stdout().is_terminal() {
        return Err(
            "planrun tui needs a terminal on stdin and stdout; `planrun run` is the line version"
                .to_owned(),
        );
    }
    let ctx = context(&host.dir, host.plans.as_deref(), &host.version, Vec::new())?;
    let app = App::new(ctx, Glyphs::from_env(), system_clock(), host.update);
    install_interrupt_handler();
    let report = std::panic::take_hook();
    let terminal = ratatui::init();
    install_panic_hook(report);
    let result = drive(Core::new(app, terminal, Box::new(Crossterm)));
    ratatui::restore();
    result
}

/// What a panic does to the terminal (D94).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum OnPanic {
    /// The render thread's: restore the terminal, then report as Rust does.
    Restore,
    /// Another thread's (the runner's, a job's): leave the terminal and print nothing; the
    /// thread catches the panic and the app shows it as that work's failure.
    Leave,
}

/// What a panic on `current` does, when `render` owns the terminal.
pub fn on_panic(render: ThreadId, current: ThreadId) -> OnPanic {
    if render == current {
        OnPanic::Restore
    } else {
        OnPanic::Leave
    }
}

/// The hook a panic report goes through.
type Report = Box<dyn Fn(&std::panic::PanicHookInfo<'_>) + Sync + Send>;

/// Replaces ratatui's hook, which would restore the terminal on every thread's panic, caught or
/// not: `report` (the hook before `ratatui::init`) runs only for the render thread's.
fn install_panic_hook(report: Report) {
    let render = std::thread::current().id();
    std::panic::set_hook(Box::new(move |info| {
        if on_panic(render, std::thread::current().id()) == OnPanic::Restore {
            ratatui::restore();
            report(info);
        }
    }));
}

/// The terminal's hooks.
struct Crossterm;

impl Hooks for Crossterm {
    fn shell(&mut self, root: &Path) {
        ratatui::restore();
        let program = if cfg!(windows) { "pwsh" } else { "bash" };
        println!(
            "{program} at {}; this is not the plan's session (its variables are not here). Exit to return.",
            root.display()
        );
        {
            let _quiet = InterruptsIgnored::new();
            if let Err(e) = std::process::Command::new(program)
                .current_dir(root)
                .status()
            {
                println!("{program} did not start: {e}");
            }
        }
        let _ = terminal::enable_raw_mode();
        let _ = execute!(std::io::stdout(), EnterAlternateScreen);
        while event::poll(Duration::ZERO).unwrap_or(false) {
            let _ = event::read();
        }
    }

    fn end_now(&mut self) {
        ratatui::restore();
        planrun_exec::shell::end_now(130);
    }
}

fn drive<B: ratatui::backend::Backend>(mut core: Core<B>) -> Result<(), String> {
    let term = |e: B::Error| format!("the terminal: {e}");
    let io = |e: std::io::Error| format!("the terminal: {e}");
    core.start();
    core.draw().map_err(term)?;
    while !core.done() {
        core.pump();
        if event::poll(POLL).map_err(io)? {
            match event::read().map_err(io)? {
                Event::Key(ev) => {
                    if let Some(key) = keys::key(&ev) {
                        core.key(key);
                    }
                }
                Event::Resize(..) => core.redraw().map_err(term)?,
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        core.tick();
        core.pump();
        core.draw().map_err(term)?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn only_the_render_thread_s_panic_restores_the_terminal() {
        let render = std::thread::current().id();
        let other = std::thread::spawn(|| std::thread::current().id())
            .join()
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| panic!("the thread panicked"));
        assert_eq!(on_panic(render, render), OnPanic::Restore);
        assert_eq!(on_panic(render, other), OnPanic::Leave);
    }
}
```

```rust
//! The Plans view (docs/tui-design.md §6.A) on the greeter fixture with the synthetic plans.

mod support;

use planrun_tui::keys::Key;
use support::{PLANS, screen, setup};

#[test]
fn the_plans_view_lists_every_file_with_its_check_and_run() {
    let setup = setup(&PLANS);
    let mut core = setup.core();
    core.key(Key::Down);
    insta::assert_snapshot!(screen(&mut core));
}

#[test]
fn the_filter_narrows_the_table_and_esc_clears_it() {
    let setup = setup(&PLANS);
    let mut core = setup.core();
    for key in [
        Key::Char('/'),
        Key::Char('d'),
        Key::Char('e'),
        Key::Char('v'),
        Key::Enter,
    ] {
        core.key(key);
    }
    let names: Vec<String> = core
        .app()
        .plans()
        .shown()
        .iter()
        .map(|e| e.name.clone())
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(names, ["devready-style.md"]);
    core.key(Key::Esc);
    assert_eq!(core.app().plans().shown().len(), PLANS.len());
}

#[test]
fn below_100_by_30_the_tui_says_it_is_too_small() {
    let setup = setup(&PLANS[..1]);
    let mut core = setup.core();
    let backend = ratatui::backend::TestBackend::new(80, 24);
    let mut small = planrun_tui::driver::Core::new(
        setup.app(),
        ratatui::Terminal::new(backend).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}")),
        Box::new(support::Counted::default()),
    );
    let text = screen(&mut small);
    assert!(text.contains("terminal too small"), "{text}");
    assert!(text.contains("needs 100 × 30"), "{text}");
    let _ = screen(&mut core);
}

    let setup = setup(&PLANS[..1]);
    let app = setup.app_with(Some(Box::new(|| panic!("the update check broke"))));
    let terminal = ratatui::Terminal::new(ratatui::backend::TestBackend::new(120, 36))
        .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
    let mut core =
        planrun_tui::driver::Core::new(app, terminal, Box::new(support::Counted::default()));
    core.start();
    let failed = core.wait(std::time::Duration::from_secs(30), |app| {
        app.update() == &planrun_tui::jobs::UpdateState::Failed
    });

}
```

File `crates/planrun-tui/tests/round_trips.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! The dock's round trips and Ctrl-C (decisions D73, D90, D91): `[o]` runs the shell through
//! the hooks and the runner asks again, `[v]` and `[d]` are answered in the TUI with the dock
//! left open, and Ctrl-C ends planrun only after its confirm.

mod support;

use std::sync::atomic::Ordering;
use std::time::Duration;

use planrun_exec::ui::{Choice, Question, QuestionKind, Reply};
use planrun_tui::dock::Dock;
use planrun_tui::driver::Core;
use planrun_tui::jobs::Bg;
use planrun_tui::keys::Key;
use planrun_tui::run::{Inspector, Phase};
use ratatui::backend::TestBackend;
use support::{Counted, screen, setup};

/// The journal name of the next question, once it is asked.
fn question(core: &mut Core<TestBackend>) -> String {
    let asked = core.wait(Duration::from_secs(300), |app| {
        app.run()
            .is_some_and(|r| r.dock.is_some() || matches!(r.phase, Phase::Ended(_)))
    });
    assert!(asked, "no question in time");
    let run = core.app().run().unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("no run"));
    let dock = run
        .dock
        .as_ref()
        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("ended: {:?}", run.phase));
    dock.question.kind.name().to_owned()
}

#[test]
fn shell_view_and_diff_come_back_to_the_question_and_ctrl_c_ends_after_its_confirm() {
    if std::env::var_os("PLANRUN_REQUIRE_SHELLS").is_none()
        && planrun_exec::shell::locate(planrun_model::Shell::Pwsh).is_err()
    {
        eprintln!("skipped: no pwsh");
        return;
    }
    let setup = setup(&["assay-style.md"]);
    let hooks = Counted::default();
    let mut core = setup.core_with(hooks.clone());
    core.key(Key::Enter);
    let inspected = core.wait(Duration::from_secs(60), |app| {
        app.inspect()
            .is_some_and(|i| matches!(i.dry, Bg::Current(_)))
    });
    assert!(inspected);
    core.key(Key::Char('R'));
    assert_eq!(
        question(&mut core),
        "run",
        "cargo test greet asks under the default policy"
    );
    core.key(Key::Char('o'));
    assert_eq!(question(&mut core), "run");
    assert_eq!(hooks.shells.load(Ordering::SeqCst), 1);
    core.key(Key::Char('v'));
    assert_eq!(question(&mut core), "run");
    let viewed = core.app().run().map(|r| r.inspector.clone());
    assert!(
        matches!(viewed, Some(Inspector::Action((0, 2, 1)))),
        "{viewed:?}"
    );
    assert!(screen(&mut core).contains("ACTION"));
    core.key(Key::Char('d'));
    let shown = core.wait(Duration::from_secs(30), |app| {
        app.run()
            .is_some_and(|r| matches!(r.inspector, Inspector::Tree(..)))
    });
    assert!(shown, "the diff was not shown");
    assert!(screen(&mut core).contains("WORKING TREE"));
    core.key(Key::Ctrl('c'));
    assert!(screen(&mut core).contains("END PLANRUN"));
    core.key(Key::Char('n'));
    assert!(!screen(&mut core).contains("END PLANRUN"));
    assert_eq!(hooks.ends.load(Ordering::SeqCst), 0);
    core.key(Key::Ctrl('c'));
    core.key(Key::Ctrl('c'));
    assert_eq!(hooks.ends.load(Ordering::SeqCst), 1);
    assert!(core.done());
}

#[test]
fn q_at_a_commit_s_confirmation_needs_pressing_twice() {
    let (tx, rx) = std::sync::mpsc::channel();
    let q = Question {
        kind: QuestionKind::Confirm {
            reasons: Vec::new(),
            errored: false,
        },
        at: None,
        text: Vec::new(),
        choices: vec![Choice::Yes, Choice::No, Choice::Quit],
        typed: None,
    };
    let mut dock = Dock::new(q, tx);
    dock.guard_quit = true;
    assert_eq!(dock.key(Key::Char('q')), None);
    assert!(dock.armed);
    assert_eq!(dock.key(Key::Char('q')), Some(Reply::Choice(Choice::Quit)));
    let mut plain = Dock::new(
        Question {
            kind: QuestionKind::Manual,
            at: None,
            text: Vec::new(),
            choices: vec![Choice::Continue, Choice::Quit],
            typed: None,
        },
        std::sync::mpsc::channel().0,
    );
    assert_eq!(plain.key(Key::Char('q')), Some(Reply::Choice(Choice::Quit)));
    assert_eq!(plain.key(Key::Enter), Some(Reply::Choice(Choice::Continue)));
    drop(rx);
}
```

```rust
//! The TUI's test harness (decision D93): a greeter fixture copy, the synthetic plans in a
//! plans directory beside it, an app with a fixed clock and fixed labels, and a core over a
//! 120 × 36 `TestBackend`.

#![allow(dead_code)]

#[path = "../../../../tests/support/fixture.rs"]
pub mod fixture;

use std::fs::File;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::{Duration, SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

use ratatui::Terminal;
use ratatui::backend::TestBackend;

use fixture::Fixture;
use planrun_tui::app::App;
use planrun_tui::driver::Core;
use planrun_tui::glyph::Glyphs;
use planrun_tui::grid::grid;
use planrun_tui::jobs::Bg;
use planrun_tui::terminal::context;

/// 2026-11-27 12:34:56 UTC.
pub const NOW: u64 = 1_795_782_896_000;

/// The synthetic plans, and the corpus README, which is not a plan.
pub const PLANS: [&str; 5] = [
    "README.md",
    "assay-style.md",
    "before-after-style.md",
    "devready-style.md",
    "superpowers-style.md",
];

/// The tests' hooks: they count the shell round trips and the ends.
#[derive(Clone, Default)]
pub struct Counted {
    /// `[o]` shells run.
    pub shells: std::sync::Arc<std::sync::atomic::AtomicUsize>,
    /// Confirmed Ctrl-Cs.
    pub ends: std::sync::Arc<std::sync::atomic::AtomicUsize>,
}

impl planrun_tui::driver::Hooks for Counted {
    fn shell(&mut self, _root: &std::path::Path) {
        self.shells
            .fetch_add(1, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::SeqCst);
    }

    fn end_now(&mut self) {
        self.ends.fetch_add(1, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::SeqCst);
    }
}

/// A fixture with its plans directory.
pub struct Setup {
    /// The repository.
    pub fixture: Fixture,
    /// The plans directory, outside the repository.
    pub plans: PathBuf,
}

/// The greeter fixture and a plans directory with `plans`, each file's time set to [`NOW`].
pub fn setup(plans: &[&str]) -> Setup {
    let fixture = Fixture::greeter(false);
    let dir = fixture.outside().join("plans");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
    let corpus =
        std::path::Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../../tests/corpus/synthetic");
    for name in plans {
        let target = dir.join(name);
        std::fs::copy(corpus.join(name), &target).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{name}: {e}"));
        let file = File::options()
            .write(true)
            .open(&target)
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
        file.set_modified(UNIX_EPOCH + Duration::from_millis(NOW))
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
    }
    Setup {
        fixture,
        plans: dir,
    }
}

impl Setup {
    /// The app on this setup, with the labels of the design's artboards.
    pub fn app(&self) -> App {
        self.app_with(None)
    }

    /// The app with `update` as its update check.
    pub fn app_with(&self, update: Option<planrun_tui::jobs::UpdateCheck>) -> App {
        let mut ctx = context(
            self.fixture.path(),
            Some(&self.plans),
            "0.1.0",
            self.fixture.env(),
        )
        .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
        ctx.repo = "greeter".to_owned();
        ctx.target = r"C:\src\greeter".to_owned();
        ctx.plans_label = "./plans".to_owned();
        App::new(ctx, Glyphs::unicode(), Arc::new(|| NOW), update)
    }

    /// A started core whose plans are all checked.
    pub fn core(&self) -> Core<TestBackend> {
        self.core_with(Counted::default())
    }

    /// A started core with `hooks`, its plans all checked.
    pub fn core_with(&self, hooks: Counted) -> Core<TestBackend> {
        let terminal = Terminal::new(TestBackend::new(120, 36)).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
        let mut core = Core::new(self.app(), terminal, Box::new(hooks));
        core.start();
        let n = std::fs::read_dir(&self.plans).map_or(0, |d| d.count());
        let checked = core.wait(Duration::from_secs(60), |app| {
            let entries = &app.plans().entries;
            entries.len() == n && entries.iter().all(|e| matches!(e.check, Bg::Current(_)))
        });
        assert!(checked, "the plans were not checked in time");
        core
    }
}

/// The screen as text, for a snapshot.
pub fn screen(core: &mut Core<TestBackend>) -> String {
    core.draw().unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
    grid(core.terminal().backend().buffer())
}

/// The current time, for tests that wait.
pub fn now() -> SystemTime {
    SystemTime::now()
}
```

Expected: the commit, then no output from `git status --porcelain`. A line naming files instead of the commit means those files differ from the verified tree.

### P6c-T6: History, the resume preview and resume

**Goal:** History lists the runs of every journal with how each ended (D90); `R` opens the read-only resume preview (D85), and `enter` there starts `Runner::resume`. Its test crashes a run, finds it killed in History, checks that the preview wrote nothing (`.git` included) and resumes the run to its two commits.

**Files**

- Modify `crates/planrun-tui/src/app.rs`
- Create `crates/planrun-tui/src/history.rs`
- Modify `crates/planrun-tui/src/jobs.rs`
- Modify `crates/planrun-tui/src/lib.rs`
- Create `crates/planrun-tui/src/preview.rs`
- Create `crates/planrun-tui/tests/resume.rs`
- Create `crates/planrun-tui/tests/snapshots/resume__a_crashed_run_shows_in_history_its_preview_reads_only_and_resume_completes_it.snap`
- Modify `crates/planrun-tui/tests/support/mod.rs`

Over 300 lines, so they come as exact diffs: `crates/planrun-tui/src/app.rs`, `crates/planrun-tui/src/jobs.rs`.

```diff
+    fn history_key(&mut self, key: Key) -> Vec<Effect> {

+                vec![Effect::Job(Job::Preview {
+                    journal: view.entry.journal.clone(),
+                    plan: view.entry.plan.clone(),
+                    now: (self.clock)(),

+            Key::Enter => self.start_resume(),
+            Key::Esc | Key::Char('q') => {

+            }
+            _ => Vec::new(),
+        }

+
+    /// Starts `Runner::resume` from the preview: with the journaled policy and range, as
+    /// `planrun resume` without flags (D77); it checks everything again itself.
+    fn start_resume(&mut self) -> Vec<Effect> {
+        if self.running() {
+            return Vec::new();
+        }
+        let Some(data) = self.resume.as_ref().and_then(|v| v.data.value()) else {
+            return Vec::new();
```

File `crates/planrun-tui/src/history.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! History (docs/tui-design.md §6.E): the runs in `<git-dir>/planrun/*.jsonl`, journals set
//! aside by `--restart` included, newest first and grouped by date (UTC, D94). A run ends with
//! its `run-end`; without one it is "running in pid N" while `lock.live` says a run is live, and
//! "killed" otherwise (D90). `R` opens the resume preview of an unfinished run.

use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use ratatui::buffer::Buffer;
use ratatui::layout::Rect;
use ratatui::style::Style;

use planrun_exec::journal::{self, End, Line, Record};
use planrun_exec::runner::resume::analyse;

use crate::draw::{Pen, band, section, select_row};
use crate::glyph::{Glyph, Glyphs};
use crate::keys::Key;
use crate::plans::{MONTHS, civil, clock_utc};
use crate::theme::{Token, bold, fg, on};

/// One task as a run's journal has it.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct TaskSeen {
    /// Its id.
    pub id: String,
    /// Its `task-end` HEAD, when it ended.
    pub commit: Option<String>,
    /// Milliseconds from `task-start` to `task-end`.
    pub took: Option<u64>,
}

/// One run: a chain of a journal, its first `run` record and the resumes appended to it.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct RunEntry {
    /// The journal file.
    pub journal: PathBuf,
    /// Set aside by `--restart` (`<stem>.<ms>.jsonl`).
    pub set_aside: bool,
    /// The last chain of its journal: the one resume would go on with.
    pub last: bool,
    /// The plan's path as recorded.
    pub plan: String,
    /// When it started.
    pub started: u64,
    /// When its last record was written.
    pub last_ms: u64,
    /// How its last process ended; `None` without a `run-end`.
    pub end: Option<End>,
    /// Why it stopped.
    pub reason: Option<String>,
    /// `--auto`.
    pub auto: Vec<String>,
    /// HEAD at the start.
    pub head: Option<String>,
    /// Resumes appended.
    pub resumes: usize,
    /// Its records.
    pub records: usize,
    /// The tasks it started, in order.
    pub tasks: Vec<TaskSeen>,
}

impl RunEntry {
    /// The plan file's name.
    pub fn name(&self) -> String {
        Path::new(&self.plan)
            .file_name()
            .map_or_else(|| self.plan.clone(), |n| n.to_string_lossy().into_owned())
    }

    /// Whether resume would go on with it.
    pub fn resumable(&self) -> bool {
        self.last && !self.set_aside && self.end != Some(End::Completed)
    }
}

/// Every run in the journal directory `dir`, newest first.
pub fn scan(dir: &Path) -> Result<Vec<RunEntry>, String> {
    let entries = match std::fs::read_dir(dir) {
        Ok(entries) => entries,
        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(Vec::new()),
        Err(e) => return Err(format!("{}: {e}", dir.display())),
    };
    let mut runs = Vec::new();
    for path in entries.flatten().map(|e| e.path()) {
        if path.extension().is_none_or(|x| x != "jsonl") {
            continue;
        }
        let lines = journal::read(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let set_aside = path
            .file_stem()
            .and_then(|s| Path::new(s).extension())
            .is_some_and(|x| x.to_string_lossy().chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_digit()));
        runs.extend(chains(&path, set_aside, &lines));
    }
    runs.sort_by_key(|r| std::cmp::Reverse(r.started));
    Ok(runs)
}

/// The chains of one journal.
pub fn chains(path: &Path, set_aside: bool, lines: &[Line]) -> Vec<RunEntry> {
    let starts: Vec<usize> = lines
        .iter()
        .enumerate()
        .filter(|(_, l)| matches!(l.record, Record::Run { resume: false, .. }))
        .map(|(i, _)| i)
        .collect();
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    for (n, &start) in starts.iter().enumerate() {
        let end = starts.get(n + 1).copied().unwrap_or(lines.len());
        let chain = &lines[start..end];
        let Some(analysis) = analyse(chain) else {
            continue;
        };
        let mut entry = RunEntry {
            journal: path.to_path_buf(),
            set_aside,
            last: n + 1 == starts.len(),
            plan: analysis.run.plan.clone(),
            started: chain[0].ms,
            last_ms: chain.last().map_or(0, |l| l.ms),
            end: analysis.end,
            reason: None,
            auto: analysis.run.auto.clone(),
            head: None,
            resumes: 0,
            records: chain.len(),
            tasks: Vec::new(),
        };
        let mut started_at: Vec<(String, u64)> = Vec::new();
        for line in chain {
            match &line.record {
                Record::Run { resume: true, .. } => entry.resumes += 1,
                Record::Run { head, .. } => entry.head.clone_from(&head.commit),
                Record::TaskStart { task, .. } => {
                    started_at.push((task.clone(), line.ms));
                    if !entry.tasks.iter().any(|t| &t.id == task) {
                        entry.tasks.push(TaskSeen {
                            id: task.clone(),
                            commit: None,
                            took: None,
                        });
                    }
                }
                Record::TaskEnd { task, head } => {
                    let since = started_at
                        .iter()
                        .rev()
                        .find(|(t, _)| t == task)
                        .map(|(_, ms)| *ms);
                    if let Some(t) = entry.tasks.iter_mut().find(|t| &t.id == task) {
                        t.commit.clone_from(&head.commit);
                        t.took = since.map(|s| line.ms.saturating_sub(s));
                    }
                }
                Record::RunEnd { reason, .. } => entry.reason.clone_from(reason),
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        out.push(entry);
    }
    out
}

/// The History view.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct History {
    /// The runs, newest first; `None` until scanned.
    pub runs: Option<Result<Vec<RunEntry>, String>>,
    /// The selected run.
    pub selected: usize,
}

/// What a key did in History.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Did {
    /// Nothing for the app.
    None,
    /// Open the resume preview of the selected run.
    Preview(RunEntry),
    /// Back.
    Back,
}

impl History {
    /// The selected run.
    pub fn current(&self) -> Option<&RunEntry> {
        self.runs.as_ref()?.as_ref().ok()?.get(self.selected)
    }

    /// Handles `key`.
    pub fn key(&mut self, key: Key) -> Did {
        let n = self
            .runs
            .as_ref()
            .and_then(|r| r.as_ref().ok())
            .map_or(0, Vec::len);
        match key {
            Key::Up | Key::Char('k') => self.selected = self.selected.saturating_sub(1),
            Key::Down | Key::Char('j') => {
                self.selected = (self.selected + 1).min(n.saturating_sub(1))
            }
            Key::Char('R') => {
                if let Some(run) = self.current().filter(|r| r.resumable()) {
                    return Did::Preview(run.clone());
                }
            }
            Key::Esc | Key::Char('q') => return Did::Back,
            _ => {}
        }
        Did::None
    }

    /// The keys the footer shows.
    pub fn footer(&self) -> Vec<(String, String)> {
        vec![
            ("R".into(), "resume preview…".into()),
            ("↑↓".into(), "select".into()),
            ("esc".into(), "back".into()),
        ]
    }

    /// The header's right side.
    pub fn state(&self, ctx_journals: &str) -> Vec<(String, Style)> {
        let n = self
            .runs
            .as_ref()
            .and_then(|r| r.as_ref().ok())
            .map_or(0, Vec::len);
        let journals: std::collections::BTreeSet<&PathBuf> = self
            .runs
            .as_ref()
            .and_then(|r| r.as_ref().ok())
            .map(|r| r.iter().map(|e| &e.journal).collect())
            .unwrap_or_default();
        vec![
            (
                format!("{n} runs, {} journals in ", journals.len()),
                on(Token::Fg3, Token::Bg1),
            ),
            (ctx_journals.to_owned(), on(Token::Fg2, Token::Bg1)),
        ]
    }

    /// Draws History in `area`; `live` is the pid of a live run, if one is live.
    pub fn draw(&self, buf: &mut Buffer, area: Rect, live: Option<Option<u32>>, g: Glyphs) {
        let split = area.x + 58;
        let list = Rect::new(area.x, area.y, split - area.x, area.height);
        let detail = Rect::new(split + 1, area.y, area.right() - split - 1, area.height);
        crate::draw::vrule(buf, split, area.y, area.bottom(), g);
        band(buf, list, true, "RUNS", "newest first, dates in UTC", g);
        let runs = match &self.runs {
            None => {
                Pen::new(buf, list.x + 2, list.y + 2, list.right(), g).text(
                    &format!("{} reading the journals", g.get(Glyph::Background)),
                    fg(Token::Fg2),
                );
                return;
            }
            Some(Err(e)) => {
                Pen::new(buf, list.x + 2, list.y + 2, list.right(), g).text(e, fg(Token::Err));
                return;
            }
            Some(Ok(runs)) => runs,
        };
        if runs.is_empty() {
            Pen::new(buf, list.x + 2, list.y + 2, list.right(), g)
                .text("no journal yet: nothing has run here", fg(Token::Fg2));
        }
        let mut y = list.y + 2;
        let mut day = None;
        for (i, run) in runs.iter().enumerate() {
            if y + 1 >= list.bottom() {
                break;
            }
            let (yy, mm, dd) = civil(run.started);
            if day != Some((yy, mm, dd)) {
                if day.is_some() {
                    y += 1;
                }
                day = Some((yy, mm, dd));
                let label = format!("{dd} {} {yy}", MONTHS[mm - 1].to_uppercase());
                Pen::new(buf, list.x + 2, y, list.right(), g).text(&label, bold(Token::Fg3));
                y += 1;
            }
            let selected = i == self.selected;
            if selected {
                select_row(buf, list, y, g);
            }
            draw_run(buf, list, y, run, live, selected, g);
            y += 1;
        }
        if let Some(run) = self.current() {
            draw_detail(buf, detail, run, live, g);
        }
    }
}

/// The run's end: glyph, word, colour.
pub fn ending(run: &RunEntry, live: Option<Option<u32>>) -> (Glyph, String, Token) {
    if run.set_aside && run.end != Some(End::Completed) {
        return (Glyph::Skipped, "set aside".into(), Token::Fg3);
    }
    match run.end {
        Some(End::Completed) => (Glyph::Done, "completed".into(), Token::Ok),
        Some(End::Stopped) => (Glyph::Failed, "stopped".into(), Token::Err),
        None if run.last && live.is_some() => {
            let pid = live
                .flatten()
                .map_or_else(|| "running".to_owned(), |p| format!("running in pid {p}"));
            (Glyph::Running, pid, Token::Act)
        }
        None => (Glyph::Waiting, "killed".into(), Token::Warn),
    }
}

fn draw_run(
    buf: &mut Buffer,
    area: Rect,
    y: u16,
    run: &RunEntry,
    live: Option<Option<u32>>,
    selected: bool,
    g: Glyphs,
) {
    let bg = if selected { Token::Bg2 } else { Token::Bg0 };
    let on_bg = |t: Token| fg(t).bg(bg.color());
    let (glyph, word, color) = ending(run, live);
    let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, area.x + 2, y, area.right().saturating_sub(1), g);
    pen.glyph(glyph, bold(color).bg(bg.color())).gap(1);
    let name = if selected {
        bold(Token::Fg).bg(bg.color())
    } else {
        on_bg(Token::Fg)
    };
    pen.cell(&run.name(), 25, name);
    pen.cell(
        &word,
        18,
        on_bg(if color == Token::Ok {
            Token::Fg2
        } else {
            color
        }),
    );
    pen.text(&clock_utc(run.started), on_bg(Token::Fg3)).gap(1);
    let done = run.tasks.iter().filter(|t| t.commit.is_some()).count();
    let detail = if run.set_aside {
        "--restart".to_owned()
    } else {
        format!("{done}/{} tasks", run.tasks.len())
    };
    pen.text(&detail, on_bg(Token::Fg2));
}

fn draw_detail(buf: &mut Buffer, area: Rect, run: &RunEntry, live: Option<Option<u32>>, g: Glyphs) {
    let band_area = Rect::new(area.x, area.y, area.width, 1);
    band(buf, band_area, false, "RUN", &run.name(), g);
    let x = area.x + 1;
    let right = area.right().saturating_sub(1);
    let mut y = area.y + 2;
    Pen::new(buf, x, y, right, g).text(&run.plan, bold(Token::Fg));
    y += 1;
    let (yy, mm, dd) = civil(run.started);
    let auto = if run.auto.is_empty() {
        "policy default".to_owned()
    } else {
        format!("auto {}", run.auto.join(","))
    };
    let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, x, y, right, g);
    pen.cell("started", 9, fg(Token::Fg3));
    pen.text(
        &format!(
            "{dd} {} {yy} {} UTC {} {auto}",
            MONTHS[mm - 1],
            clock_utc(run.started),
            g.get(Glyph::Dot)
        ),
        fg(Token::Fg2),
    );
    if let Some(head) = &run.head {
        pen.text(&format!(" {} HEAD ", g.get(Glyph::Dot)), fg(Token::Fg2))
            .text(&head[..head.len().min(7)], fg(Token::Meta));
    }
    y += 1;
    let (glyph, word, color) = ending(run, live);
    let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, x, y, right, g);
    pen.cell("ended", 9, fg(Token::Fg3));
    let why = match (&run.end, &run.reason) {
        (None, _) if word == "killed" => {
            "no run-end, no live run: the process was killed".to_owned()
        }
        (_, Some(reason)) => format!("{word}: {reason}"),
        _ => word.clone(),
    };
    pen.glyph(glyph, fg(color)).gap(1).text(&why, fg(color));
    y += 1;
    let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, x, y, right, g);
    pen.cell("journal", 9, fg(Token::Fg3));
    let file = run
        .journal
        .file_name()
        .map_or_else(String::new, |n| n.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
    let resumes = if run.resumes == 0 {
        String::new()
    } else {
        format!(
            " {} {} resume{}",
            g.get(Glyph::Dot),
            run.resumes,
            if run.resumes == 1 { "" } else { "s" }
        )
    };
    pen.text(
        &format!(
            "{file} {} {} records{resumes}",
            g.get(Glyph::Dot),
            run.records
        ),
        fg(Token::Fg3),
    );
    y += 2;
    let width = Rect::new(x, area.y, right - x, area.height);
    section(buf, width, y, "TASKS", &[], g);
    y += 1;
    for task in &run.tasks {
        if y + 6 >= area.bottom() {
            break;
        }
        let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, x, y, right, g);
        match &task.commit {
            Some(commit) => {
                pen.glyph(Glyph::Done, bold(Token::Ok))
                    .gap(1)
                    .text(&task.id, bold(Token::Fg))
                    .gap(2);
                pen.glyph(Glyph::Commit, fg(Token::Fg2))
                    .gap(1)
                    .text(&commit[..commit.len().min(7)], fg(Token::Meta));
                if let Some(took) = task.took {
                    pen.gap(2)
                        .text(&crate::text::duration(took), fg(Token::Fg3));
                }
            }
            None => {
                pen.glyph(Glyph::Waiting, bold(Token::Warn))
                    .gap(1)
                    .text(&task.id, bold(Token::Fg))
                    .gap(2)
                    .text("did not end", fg(Token::Warn));
            }
        }
        y += 1;
    }
    y += 1;
    section(buf, width, y, "RESUME", &[], g);
    let text = if run.resumable() {
        [
            "R opens the resume preview. It reads only; resume itself",
            "takes the lock, checks again and asks in the dock.",
        ]
    } else if run.set_aside {
        ["set aside by --restart: nothing resumes it", ""]
    } else {
        ["nothing to resume", ""]
    };
    for (i, l) in text.iter().enumerate() {
        Pen::new(buf, x, y + 1 + u16::try_from(i).unwrap_or(0), right, g).text(l, fg(Token::Fg2));
    }
}
```

```diff
+/// Reads what resume would find: the plan parsed now, the journal's last chain, the disk.
+fn preview(
+    ctx: &Context,
+    journal_path: &Path,
+    plan: &str,
+    now: u64,
+) -> Result<crate::preview::PreviewData, String> {
+    let path = if Path::new(plan).is_absolute() {
+        PathBuf::from(plan)
+    } else {
+        ctx.root.join(plan)
+    };
+    let bytes = std::fs::read(&path).map_err(|e| format!("{}: {e}", path.display()))?;
+    let registry = Registry::with_builtins().map_err(|e| format!("the built-in formats: {e}"))?;
+    let name = path.display().to_string();
+    let parsed = parse_plan(&registry, &name, &bytes, None)
+        .map_err(|_| format!("{plan} does not parse any more: resume would refuse it"))?;
+    let lines = journal::read(journal_path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
+    let analysis = analyse(&lines).ok_or_else(|| "the journal has no run".to_owned())?;
+    let ws = Workspace::open(&ctx.root, ctx.env.clone()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
+    let preview = planrun_exec::runner::preview::preview(&ws, &parsed.plan, &analysis)
+        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
+    let untracked = git_out(ctx, &["ls-files", "--others", "--exclude-standard"])
+        .lines()
+        .map(str::to_owned)
+        .collect();
+    Ok(crate::preview::PreviewData {
+        taken: now,
+        path,
+        parsed: Arc::new(parsed),
+        analysis,
+        preview,
+        untracked,
+    })
+}
+
 /// The output of `git --no-optional-locks args…`; empty when git fails.
 fn git_out(ctx: &Context, args: &[&str]) -> String {
     let out = std::process::Command::new("git")
```

```rust
//! The planrun TUI (docs/tui-design.md; decisions D84–D94): a second [`Ui`](planrun_exec::Ui)
//! over the same crates as the line prompts, composed by planrun-cli as `planrun tui`. It shows
//! the domain model and adds no model of its own: resume, policy and preflight stay
//! authoritative; the TUI explains and asks, and never decides.

#![forbid(unsafe_code)]

pub mod app;
pub mod chrome;
pub mod diff;
pub mod dock;
pub mod draw;
pub mod driver;
pub mod glyph;
pub mod grid;
pub mod history;
```

File `crates/planrun-tui/src/preview.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! The resume preview (docs/tui-design.md §6.F, decision D85): a read-only full view of what
//! `Runner::resume` will find, built from `analyse` and `runner::preview::preview`. It takes no
//! lock and writes nothing, and it shows when it was read (`g` reads again). Every question in
//! it is asked when resume reaches it; `enter` starts resume, which takes the lock, checks
//! everything again against the disk, and asks in the dock.

use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::Arc;

use ratatui::buffer::Buffer;
use ratatui::layout::Rect;
use ratatui::style::Style;

use planrun_exec::DoneState;
use planrun_exec::done::Seen;
use planrun_exec::runner::preview::Preview;
use planrun_exec::runner::resume::{Analysis, HeadVerdict};
use planrun_parse::Parsed;

use crate::draw::{Pen, section};
use crate::glyph::{Glyph, Glyphs};
use crate::history::RunEntry;
use crate::jobs::Bg;
use crate::theme::{Token, bold, fg, on};
use crate::tree;

/// What the preview read.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct PreviewData {
    /// When it was read.
    pub taken: u64,
    /// The plan file.
    pub path: PathBuf,
    /// The plan, parsed now.
    pub parsed: Arc<Parsed>,
    /// The journal's last chain.
    pub analysis: Analysis,
    /// What resume would find.
    pub preview: Preview,
    /// Untracked files, left alone by resume.
    pub untracked: Vec<String>,
}

/// The preview view.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ResumeView {
    /// The run it previews.
    pub entry: RunEntry,
    /// What it read.
    pub data: Bg<Box<PreviewData>>,
}

impl ResumeView {
    /// Whether resume would stop at once on what the preview read.
    pub fn blocked(&self) -> Option<&'static str> {
        let p = &self.data.value()?.preview;
        if p.live {
            return Some("a run is live: resume would be refused");
        }
        if p.plan_changed {
            return Some("the plan changed since the run: resume would stop");
        }
        if p.index_lock {
            return Some(".git/index.lock exists: resume would stop");
        }
        if p.head == HeadVerdict::Moved {
            return Some("HEAD moved: resume would stop");
        }
        None
    }

    /// The keys the footer shows.
    pub fn footer(&self) -> Vec<(String, String)> {
        vec![
            ("enter".into(), "start resume".into()),
            ("g".into(), "refresh preview".into()),
            ("esc".into(), "back".into()),
        ]
    }

    /// The header's right side.
    pub fn state() -> Vec<(String, Style)> {
        vec![
            (
                " READ-ONLY ".to_owned(),
                bold(Token::Meta).bg(Token::Bg1.color()),
            ),
            (
                "  nothing locked, run or written".to_owned(),
                on(Token::Fg3, Token::Bg1),
            ),
        ]
    }

    /// Draws the preview in `area`.
    pub fn draw(&self, buf: &mut Buffer, area: Rect, g: Glyphs) {
        let x = area.x + 6;
        let right = area.right().saturating_sub(6);
        let width = Rect::new(x, area.y, right - x, area.height);
        let mut y = area.y + 1;
        let data = match &self.data {
            Bg::Current(d) | Bg::Stale(d) => d,
            Bg::Running => {
                Pen::new(buf, x, y, right, g).text(
                    &format!(
                        "{} reading the journal and the disk",
                        g.get(Glyph::Background)
                    ),
                    fg(Token::Fg2),
                );
                return;
            }
            Bg::Failed(e) | Bg::Unavailable(e) => {
                Pen::new(buf, x, y, right, g).text(e, fg(Token::Err));
                return;
            }
        };
        let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, x, y, right, g);
        pen.text("RESUME PREVIEW", bold(Token::Fg)).gap(2);
        let secs = data.taken / 1000;
        let taken = format!(
            "{:02}:{:02}:{:02}",
            (secs / 3600) % 24,
            (secs / 60) % 60,
            secs % 60
        );
        pen.text(
            &format!(
                "from the journal (analyse) and a read-only look at the disk, taken {taken} UTC"
            ),
            fg(Token::Fg3),
        );
        y += 1;
        Pen::new(buf, x, y, right, g).text(
            &format!("Resume itself decides again when it runs; questions marked {} are asked in the dock when it reaches them.", g.get(Glyph::Waiting)),
            fg(Token::Fg2),
        );
        y += 2;
        let (glyph, word, color) = match self.blocked() {
            Some(why) => (Glyph::Failed, why.to_owned(), Token::Err),
            None => (Glyph::Done, "nothing blocks a resume".to_owned(), Token::Ok),
        };
        section(
            buf,
            width,
            y,
            "JOURNAL AGAINST REPOSITORY",
            &[
                (format!("{} ", g.get(glyph)), fg(color)),
                (word, fg(Token::Fg2)),
            ],
            g,
        );
        y += 1;
        let p = &data.preview;
        let fingerprint = &data.analysis.run.fingerprint;
        let short = &fingerprint[..fingerprint.len().min(7)];
        let checks: [(bool, &str, String); 3] = [
            (
                !p.plan_changed,
                if p.plan_changed {
                    "plan changed"
                } else {
                    "plan unchanged"
                },
                format!(
                    "fingerprint {short} {} the run record",
                    if p.plan_changed {
                        "differs from"
                    } else {
                        "matches"
                    }
                ),
            ),
            (
                !p.index_lock,
                if p.index_lock {
                    "index.lock"
                } else {
                    "no index.lock"
                },
                if p.index_lock {
                    "git is busy, or crashed: planrun never removes it".into()
                } else {
                    "git is not busy".into()
                },
            ),
            (
                !p.live,
                if p.live {
                    "a run is live"
                } else {
                    "no live run"
                },
                if p.live {
                    "another planrun holds .git/planrun/lock".into()
                } else {
                    "no planrun holds .git/planrun/lock".into()
                },
            ),
        ];
        for (ok, what, detail) in checks {
            let (glyph, color) = if ok {
                (Glyph::Done, Token::Ok)
            } else {
                (Glyph::Failed, Token::Err)
            };
            let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, x, y, right, g);
            pen.glyph(glyph, bold(color))
                .gap(1)
                .cell(what, 20, fg(Token::Fg))
                .text(&detail, fg(Token::Fg3));
            y += 1;
        }
        y = self.draw_head(buf, x, y, right, data, g);
        y += 1;
        section(buf, width, y, "WHAT RESUME WILL DO", &[], g);
        y += 1;
        y = draw_steps(buf, x, y, right, data, g);
        y += 1;
        section(buf, width, y, "YOUR FILES", &[], g);
        y += 1;
        for path in &p.resume_dirt {
            let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, x, y, right, g);
            pen.glyph(Glyph::Waiting, bold(Token::Warn))
                .gap(1)
                .text(&path.to_string(), fg(Token::Fg));
            pen.text(
                &format!(
                    " {} no verified step wrote it {} a remaining action writes over it",
                    g.get(Glyph::Dot),
                    g.get(Glyph::Dot)
                ),
                fg(Token::Fg2),
            );
            Pen::new(buf, x + 2, y + 1, right, g).text(
                "asked once when resume reaches the task (resume-dirt)",
                fg(Token::Fg3),
            );
            y += 2;
        }
        for path in &data.untracked {
            if y + 6 >= area.bottom() {
                break;
            }
            let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, x, y, right, g);
            pen.glyph(Glyph::Dot, bold(Token::Fg3)).gap(1).text(
                &format!("{path} untracked {} left alone", g.get(Glyph::Dot)),
                fg(Token::Fg3),
            );
            y += 1;
        }
        if p.resume_dirt.is_empty() && data.untracked.is_empty() {
            Pen::new(buf, x, y, right, g).text(
                "no changes of yours that resume would touch",
                fg(Token::Fg2),
            );
        }
        let y = area.bottom().saturating_sub(5);
        crate::draw::hrule(buf, x, right, y, g);
        let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, x, y + 1, right, g);
        pen.chip(
            "enter",
            "start resume: takes the lock and checks everything again; questions come in the dock",
            Token::Bg0,
        );
        Pen::new(buf, x, y + 3, right, g).text("This preview explains what resume (D14, D77) will find; it holds no answers and decides nothing.", fg(Token::Fg3));
    }

    fn draw_head(
        &self,
        buf: &mut Buffer,
        x: u16,
        y: u16,
        right: u16,
        data: &PreviewData,
        g: Glyphs,
    ) -> u16 {
        let now = data
            .preview
            .now
            .commit
            .as_deref()
            .map_or("(none)", |c| &c[..c.len().min(7)]);
        let (glyph, color, what, detail) = match &data.preview.head {
            HeadVerdict::Unchanged => (Glyph::Done, Token::Ok, "HEAD as recorded", now.to_owned()),
            HeadVerdict::CommitMade { at } => (
                Glyph::Done,
                Token::Ok,
                "HEAD moved",
                format!("{now}: the commit at {} was made; done", place(at)),
            ),
            HeadVerdict::MovedByRunning { at, kind, .. } => (
                Glyph::Waiting,
                Token::Warn,
                "HEAD moved",
                format!(
                    "{now} while the {kind} at {} ran: asked (keep or retry)",
                    place(at)
                ),
            ),
            HeadVerdict::Moved => (
                Glyph::Failed,
                Token::Err,
                "HEAD moved",
                format!("{now}: not by the running action; resume stops"),
            ),
        };
        let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, x, y, right, g);
        pen.glyph(glyph, bold(color))
            .gap(1)
            .cell(what, 20, fg(Token::Fg))
            .text(&detail, fg(Token::Meta));
        y + 1
    }
}

fn place(at: &planrun_exec::journal::At) -> String {
    format!("{} step {} action {}", at.task, at.step + 1, at.action + 1)
}

fn draw_steps(
    buf: &mut Buffer,
    x: u16,
    mut y: u16,
    right: u16,
    data: &PreviewData,
    g: Glyphs,
) -> u16 {
    let plan = &data.parsed.plan;
    let Some(task_id) = &data.analysis.interrupted else {
        let next = plan
            .tasks
            .iter()
            .find(|t| !data.analysis.done.contains(&t.id.raw.to_string()));
        let text = match next {
            Some(t) => format!("{} starts: no task was interrupted", t.id.raw),
            None => "every task is done: resume ends the run".to_owned(),
        };
        Pen::new(buf, x, y, right, g).text(&text, fg(Token::Fg2));
        return y + 1;
    };
    let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, x, y, right, g);
    pen.glyph(Glyph::Running, bold(Token::Act))
        .gap(1)
        .text(task_id, fg(Token::Meta));
    pen.text(
        " runs again from its start, in a new session",
        fg(Token::Fg),
    );
    y += 1;
    let task = plan.tasks.iter().find(|t| &*t.id.raw == task_id.as_str());
    for step in &data.preview.steps {
        let number = task
            .and_then(|t| t.steps.get(step.at.step))
            .and_then(|s| s.number)
            .map_or_else(|| (step.at.step + 1).to_string(), |n| n.to_string());
        let paths: Vec<String> = step.disk.paths.iter().map(|(p, _)| p.to_string()).collect();
        let (glyph, color, what) = match step.disk.state {
            DoneState::Done => (
                Glyph::Done,
                Token::Ok,
                "on disk as planned → done, not written again",
            ),
            DoneState::NotDone => (
                Glyph::Pending,
                Token::Fg3,
                "as before → prepared and written again",
            ),
            DoneState::Unknown => (
                Glyph::Waiting,
                Token::Warn,
                "neither as planned nor as before → unknown",
            ),
        };
        let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, x + 2, y, right, g);
        pen.glyph(glyph, bold(color))
            .gap(1)
            .text(&format!("step {number}  "), fg(Token::Fg2));
        pen.text(&paths.join("  "), fg(Token::Fg)).gap(2).text(
            what,
            fg(if color == Token::Warn {
                Token::Warn
            } else {
                Token::Fg2
            }),
        );
        y += 1;
        if step.disk.state == DoneState::Unknown {
            let seen: Vec<String> = step
                .disk
                .paths
                .iter()
                .map(|(p, s)| format!("{p} {}", seen_word(*s)))
                .collect();
            Pen::new(buf, x + 6, y, right, g).text(&seen.join(", "), fg(Token::Fg3));
            y += 1;
            let how = if step.restorable {
                "asked when resume reaches it: restore from the pre-images (present) or accept with a typed yes"
            } else {
                "asked when resume reaches it: accept with a typed yes (a pre-image is gone, no restore)"
            };
            Pen::new(buf, x + 6, y, right, g).text(how, fg(Token::Fg3));
            y += 1;
        }
        if !step.temps.is_empty() {
            Pen::new(buf, x + 6, y, right, g).text(
                &format!("removed: the writer's temp files {}", step.temps.join(", ")),
                fg(Token::Fg3),
            );
            y += 1;
        }
    }
    for again in &data.preview.again {
        let label = task
            .and_then(|t| t.steps.get(again.at.step))
            .and_then(|s| s.actions.get(again.at.action))
            .map_or_else(
                || place(&again.at),
                |a| format!("{} {}", g.get(tree::kind_glyph(a)), tree::label(plan, a)),
            );
        let when = if again.running {
            "was running when interrupted"
        } else {
            "ran before the interruption"
        };
        let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, x + 2, y, right, g);
        pen.glyph(Glyph::Waiting, bold(Token::Warn))
            .gap(1)
            .text(&label, fg(Token::Fg))
            .gap(2);
        pen.text(
            &format!("{when} → asked again (run again or keep)"),
            fg(Token::Fg2),
        );
        y += 1;
    }
    y
}

fn seen_word(seen: Seen) -> &'static str {
    match seen {
        Seen::Post => "as planned",
        Seen::Pre => "as before",
        Seen::Neither => "neither",
    }
}
```

File `crates/planrun-tui/tests/resume.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! A crash, History, the resume preview and resume in the TUI (decisions D85, D93). A crash is
//! a panic from a journal observer, as in planrun-exec's resume tests: here at the start of
//! P1-T1's first script, after its step was written.

mod support;

use std::time::Duration;

use planrun_exec::journal::{Observer, Record, Seam};
use planrun_exec::ui::Choice;
use planrun_tui::driver::Core;
use planrun_tui::jobs::Bg;
use planrun_tui::keys::Key;
use planrun_tui::run::Phase;
use ratatui::backend::TestBackend;
use support::{screen, setup};

fn crash_at_the_first_script() -> Observer {
    Box::new(|seam| {
        if let Seam::Appended(Record::ActionStart { at, kind, .. }) = seam
            && kind == "script"
            && at.task == "P1-T1"
        {
            panic!("crash at {at:?}");
        }
    })
}

fn ended(core: &mut Core<TestBackend>) -> bool {
    core.wait(Duration::from_secs(300), |app| {
        app.run()
            .is_some_and(|r| r.dock.is_some() || matches!(r.phase, Phase::Ended(_)))
    })
}

fn answer_all(core: &mut Core<TestBackend>) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut asked = Vec::new();
    loop {
        assert!(ended(core), "the run neither asked nor ended in time");
        let Some(dock) = core.app().run().and_then(|r| r.dock.as_ref()) else {
            return asked;
        };
        asked.push(dock.question.kind.name().to_owned());
        assert!(asked.len() < 20, "{asked:?}");
        let keys: Vec<Key> = match dock.question.typed {
            Some(_) => "yes".chars().map(Key::Char).chain([Key::Enter]).collect(),
            None if dock.question.choices.contains(&Choice::Yes) => vec![Key::Char('y')],
            None if dock.question.choices.contains(&Choice::Retry) => vec![Key::Char('r')],
            None => vec![Key::Enter],
        };
        for key in keys {
            core.key(key);
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn a_crashed_run_shows_in_history_its_preview_reads_only_and_resume_completes_it() {
    if !support::pwsh() {
        return;
    }
    let setup = setup(&["assay-style.md"]);
    let mut core = setup.core();
    core.app_mut()
        .observe_next_run(Box::new(crash_at_the_first_script));
    core.key(Key::Enter);
    let inspected = core.wait(Duration::from_secs(60), |app| {
        app.inspect()
            .is_some_and(|i| matches!(i.dry, Bg::Current(_)))
    });
    assert!(inspected);
    for key in [
        Key::Char('a'),
        Key::Char('r'),
        Key::Char('b'),
        Key::Char('m'),
        Key::Enter,
        Key::Char('R'),
    ] {
        core.key(key);
    }
    assert!(ended(&mut core));
    let phase = core.app().run().map(|r| r.phase.clone());
    assert!(matches!(phase, Some(Phase::Ended(Err(_)))), "{phase:?}");

    core.key(Key::F(4));
    let listed = core.wait(Duration::from_secs(30), |app| app.history().runs.is_some());
    assert!(listed);
    let text = screen(&mut core);
    assert!(text.contains("killed"), "{text}");
    assert!(
        text.contains("no run-end, no live run: the process was killed"),
        "{text}"
    );

    let before = setup.fixture.snapshot();
    core.key(Key::Char('R'));
    let read = core.wait(Duration::from_secs(60), |app| {
        app.resume_view()
            .is_some_and(|v| matches!(v.data, Bg::Current(_)))
    });
    assert!(read, "the preview did not read in time");
    assert_eq!(
        setup.fixture.snapshot(),
        before,
        "the preview wrote nothing, .git included"
    );
    let head = setup.fixture.git(&["rev-parse", "--short=7", "HEAD"]);
    // The plan's fingerprint is of its bytes, which a checkout with `core.autocrlf=true`
    // changes; the commit's id has the fixture's commit time in it.
    let fingerprint = core
        .app()
        .resume_view()
        .and_then(|v| v.data.value())
        .map(|d| d.analysis.run.fingerprint[..7].to_owned())
        .unwrap_or_default();
    let text = screen(&mut core)
        .replace(head.trim(), "<head7>")
        .replace(&fingerprint, "<plan7>");
    insta::assert_snapshot!(text);

    core.key(Key::Enter);
    let asked = answer_all(&mut core);
    assert!(
        asked.contains(&"again".to_owned()),
        "the running script asks again: {asked:?}"
    );
    let run = core.app().run().unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("no run"));
    match &run.phase {
        Phase::Ended(Ok(f)) => assert_eq!(f.reason, None, "{f:?}"),
        other => panic!("{other:?}"),
    }
    let log = setup.fixture.git(&["log", "--format=%s"]);
    assert_eq!(log.lines().count(), 3, "{log}");
}
```

```rust
//! The TUI's test harness (decision D93): a greeter fixture copy, the synthetic plans in a
//! plans directory beside it, an app with a fixed clock and fixed labels, and a core over a
//! 120 × 36 `TestBackend`.

#![allow(dead_code)]

#[path = "../../../../tests/support/fixture.rs"]
pub mod fixture;

use std::fs::File;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::{Duration, SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

use ratatui::Terminal;
use ratatui::backend::TestBackend;

use fixture::Fixture;
use planrun_tui::app::App;
use planrun_tui::driver::Core;
use planrun_tui::glyph::Glyphs;
use planrun_tui::grid::grid;
use planrun_tui::jobs::Bg;
use planrun_tui::terminal::context;

/// 2026-11-27 12:34:56 UTC.
pub const NOW: u64 = 1_795_782_896_000;

/// The synthetic plans, and the corpus README, which is not a plan.
pub const PLANS: [&str; 5] = [
    "README.md",
    "assay-style.md",
    "before-after-style.md",
    "devready-style.md",
    "superpowers-style.md",
];

/// The tests' hooks: they count the shell round trips and the ends.
#[derive(Clone, Default)]
pub struct Counted {
    /// `[o]` shells run.
    pub shells: std::sync::Arc<std::sync::atomic::AtomicUsize>,
    /// Confirmed Ctrl-Cs.
    pub ends: std::sync::Arc<std::sync::atomic::AtomicUsize>,
}

impl planrun_tui::driver::Hooks for Counted {
    fn shell(&mut self, _root: &std::path::Path) {
        self.shells
            .fetch_add(1, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::SeqCst);
    }

    fn end_now(&mut self) {
        self.ends.fetch_add(1, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::SeqCst);
    }
}

/// A fixture with its plans directory.
pub struct Setup {
    /// The repository.
    pub fixture: Fixture,
    /// The plans directory, outside the repository.
    pub plans: PathBuf,
}

/// The greeter fixture and a plans directory with `plans`, each file's time set to [`NOW`].
pub fn setup(plans: &[&str]) -> Setup {
    let fixture = Fixture::greeter(false);
    let dir = fixture.outside().join("plans");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
    let corpus =
        std::path::Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../../tests/corpus/synthetic");
    for name in plans {
        let target = dir.join(name);
        std::fs::copy(corpus.join(name), &target).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{name}: {e}"));
        let file = File::options()
            .write(true)
            .open(&target)
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
        file.set_modified(UNIX_EPOCH + Duration::from_millis(NOW))
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
    }
    Setup {
        fixture,
        plans: dir,
    }
}

impl Setup {
    /// The app on this setup, with the labels of the design's artboards.
    pub fn app(&self) -> App {
        self.app_with(None)
    }

    /// The app with `update` as its update check.
    pub fn app_with(&self, update: Option<planrun_tui::jobs::UpdateCheck>) -> App {
        let mut ctx = context(
            self.fixture.path(),
            Some(&self.plans),
            "0.1.0",
            self.fixture.env(),
        )
        .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
        ctx.repo = "greeter".to_owned();
        ctx.target = r"C:\src\greeter".to_owned();
        ctx.plans_label = "./plans".to_owned();
        App::new(ctx, Glyphs::unicode(), Arc::new(|| NOW), update)
    }

    /// A started core whose plans are all checked.
    pub fn core(&self) -> Core<TestBackend> {
        self.core_with(Counted::default())
    }

    /// A started core with `hooks`, its plans all checked.
    pub fn core_with(&self, hooks: Counted) -> Core<TestBackend> {
        let terminal = Terminal::new(TestBackend::new(120, 36)).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
        let mut core = Core::new(self.app(), terminal, Box::new(hooks));
        core.start();
        let n = std::fs::read_dir(&self.plans).map_or(0, |d| d.count());
        let checked = core.wait(Duration::from_secs(60), |app| {
            let entries = &app.plans().entries;
            entries.len() == n && entries.iter().all(|e| matches!(e.check, Bg::Current(_)))
        });
        assert!(checked, "the plans were not checked in time");
        core
    }
}

/// The screen as text, for a snapshot.
pub fn screen(core: &mut Core<TestBackend>) -> String {
    core.draw().unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
    grid(core.terminal().backend().buffer())
}

/// Whether pwsh is there; without it a test is skipped unless shells are required (D63).
pub fn pwsh() -> bool {
    use planrun_exec::shell::{ShellError, locate};
    match locate(planrun_model::Shell::Pwsh) {
        Ok(_) => true,
        Err(e @ ShellError::Unavailable { .. })
            if std::env::var_os("PLANRUN_REQUIRE_SHELLS").is_none() =>
        {
            eprintln!("skipped: {e}");
            false
        }
        Err(e) => panic!("{e}"),
    }
}

/// The current time, for tests that wait.
pub fn now() -> SystemTime {
    SystemTime::now()
}
```

```diff
+    /// `ctrl+p`: the plan's files and actions.

+                    if matches!(action.kind, planrun_model::ActionKind::Note { .. }) {
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    let group = if planrun_exec::handler::file_path(&action.kind).is_some() {
+                        "FILES"
+                    } else {
+                        "ACTIONS"

+            }
+        }
+        out.sort_by_key(|c| c.group != "FILES");

+                self.view = View::Plan;
+                match (&mut self.run, &mut self.inspect) {
+                    (Some(run), _) => {
+                        run.tree.select(node);
+                        run.focus = 0;
+                    }
+                    (None, Some(inspect)) => {
+                        inspect.tree.select(node);
+                        inspect.focus = crate::inspect::Focus::Tree;
+                    }
+                    _ => {}
+                }
+                Vec::new()
+            }
+        }
```

```rust
//! Help (F1, `?`): the keys of docs/tui-design.md §7 over the view, and the view's own keys
//! from its footer.

use ratatui::buffer::Buffer;
use ratatui::layout::Rect;

use crate::draw::{Pen, fill, hrule};
use crate::glyph::Glyphs;
use crate::theme::{Token, bold, on};

/// The keys, as §7 lists them.
pub const KEYS: [(&str, &str); 17] = [
    ("F1 / ?", "help"),
    ("F2 F3 F4", "Plans · Plan · History"),
    ("↑↓ j k", "move"),
    ("←→ h l", "fold and unfold"),
    ("enter", "open / inspect"),
    ("esc", "back (in a dock: nothing; answers are explicit)"),
    ("tab shift+tab", "focus the next or previous panel"),
    (
        "R / shift+R",
        "run / run from this task, with Inspect's policy",
    ),
    ("a", "Inspect: the assumed policy"),
    ("c", "check the plans again"),
    ("o", "open a shell, when a question offers it"),
    ("ctrl+c", "end planrun after a confirm in the dock (D91)"),
    ("f · 1–5", "console: follow · filter"),
    ("ctrl+k", "commands"),
    ("ctrl+p", "go to a file or an action of the plan"),
    ("q", "back; quits only from Plans with no run active"),
    (
        "typed answers",
        "the whole word, then enter; a single key never answers",
    ),
];

/// Draws help over `area`; `view` is the current view's footer keys.
pub fn draw(buf: &mut Buffer, area: Rect, view: &[(String, String)], g: Glyphs) {
    let width = 84.min(area.width.saturating_sub(4));
    let rows = u16::try_from(KEYS.len() + view.len() + 6).unwrap_or(u16::MAX);
    let height = rows.min(area.height.saturating_sub(2));
    let x = area.x + (area.width - width) / 2;
    let y = area.y + 1;
    let frame = Rect::new(x, y, width, height);
    fill(buf, frame, on(Token::Fg, Token::Bg2));
    let mut row = y + 1;
    Pen::new(buf, x + 2, row, x + width - 2, g)
        .text("KEYS", bold(Token::Act).bg(Token::Bg2.color()));
    Pen::new(buf, x + 2, row, x + width - 2, g).right("esc closes", on(Token::Fg3, Token::Bg2));
    row += 1;
    for (key, what) in KEYS {
        if row >= y + height - 1 {
            return;
        }
        let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, x + 2, row, x + width - 2, g);
        pen.cell(key, 16, bold(Token::Fg).bg(Token::Bg2.color()));
        pen.text(what, on(Token::Fg2, Token::Bg2));
        row += 1;
    }
    if view.is_empty() || row + 2 >= y + height {
        return;
    }
    hrule(buf, x, x + width, row, g);
    row += 1;
    Pen::new(buf, x + 2, row, x + width - 2, g)
        .text("THIS VIEW", bold(Token::Fg3).bg(Token::Bg2.color()));
    row += 1;
    for (key, what) in view {
        if row >= y + height - 1 {
            return;
        }
        let mut pen = Pen::new(buf, x + 2, row, x + width - 2, g);
        pen.cell(key, 16, bold(Token::Fg).bg(Token::Bg2.color()));
        pen.text(what, on(Token::Fg2, Token::Bg2));
        row += 1;
    }
}
```
